<script>
  
  window.ppLayoutVersion = 10.84;

  /* V10.54 — Mount the existing guide into PitchPrint's real toolbar spacer.
     Keeping the same DOM node/IDs means Creator/Public copy updates continue
     to work exactly as before. */
  window.chPitchPrintMountNextStepGuideInToolbar = function () {
    var container = document.getElementById('container');
    if (!container) return false;

    var guide = document.getElementById('ch-design-next-step-guide');
    // V10.59: every viewport reserves toolbar space for the role-aware guide.
    var toolbar = container.querySelector('[data-module="menus"]');
    var spacer = toolbar && toolbar.querySelector(':scope > .spacer');

    // Fallback for engines where :scope support is limited.
    if (!spacer && toolbar) {
      var candidates = toolbar.children || [];
      for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length; i += 1) {
        if (candidates[i].classList && candidates[i].classList.contains('spacer')) {
          spacer = candidates[i];
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!guide || !spacer) return false;

    if (guide.parentNode !== spacer) {
      spacer.appendChild(guide);
    }

    if (
      window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig &&
      typeof window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig.syncNextStepGuideCopy === 'function'
    ) {
      window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig.syncNextStepGuideCopy();
    }

    return true;
  };

  window.chPitchPrintScheduleToolbarGuideMount = function () {
    [0, 50, 180, 500, 1000].forEach(function (delay) {
      setTimeout(function () {
        window.chPitchPrintMountNextStepGuideInToolbar();
      }, delay);
    });
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', window.chPitchPrintScheduleToolbarGuideMount);
  } else {
    window.chPitchPrintScheduleToolbarGuideMount();
  }

  window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
    window.chPitchPrintMountNextStepGuideInToolbar();
  });


  
  
  window.chPitchPrintOpenUpload = function (event) {
    if (event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
    }
    var nativeUploadButton = document.querySelector('#container [data-module="uploads"] [data-cmd="upload"]');
    if (nativeUploadButton) nativeUploadButton.click();
  };

  
  window.chPitchPrintHideClientRemovedTools = function () {
    var selector = [
      '#container [data-module="context"] [data-cmd="dock-background-image"]',
      '#container [data-module="context"] [data-cmd="cut"]',
      '#container [data-module="context"] [data-cmd="copy-styles"]',
      '#container [data-module="menus"] [data-cmd="cut"]'
    ].join(',');

    document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(function (button) {
      button.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
      button.style.setProperty('visibility', 'hidden', 'important');
      button.style.setProperty('pointer-events', 'none', 'important');
      button.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      button.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1');
    });
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', window.chPitchPrintHideClientRemovedTools);
  } else {
    window.chPitchPrintHideClientRemovedTools();
  }

  new MutationObserver(function () {
    window.chPitchPrintHideClientRemovedTools();
  }).observe(document.documentElement, {
    childList: true,
    subtree: true
  });


  
  window.chPitchPrintSyncFinalOrderPanel = function () {
    var preview = document.querySelector('#container [data-module="preview"]');
    var panel = document.getElementById('ch-public-order-config');
    if (!preview || !panel) return;

    var container = document.getElementById('container');
    var flowMode = container && container.dataset
      ? (container.dataset.chFlowMode || 'PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE')
      : 'PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE';
    var isShown =
      preview.classList.contains('shown') &&
      flowMode !== 'CREATOR_BUY_ONLY';

    var panelDisplay = isShown ? 'flex' : 'none';
    if (panel.style.getPropertyValue('display') !== panelDisplay) {
      panel.style.setProperty('display', panelDisplay, 'important');
    }
    if (container) {
      container.classList.toggle('ch-save-preview-open', !!isShown);
    }

    if (isShown && !panel.__chExampleCleanupRequested) {
      panel.__chExampleCleanupRequested = true;

      setTimeout(function () {
        if (
          window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig &&
          window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig.requestConfig
        ) {
          window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig.requestConfig();
        }


        if (
          window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig &&
          window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig.requestExampleTextCleanup
        ) {
          window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig.requestExampleTextCleanup();
          window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig.requestDesignDimensions();
          if (flowMode !== 'CREATOR_DESIGN') {
            window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig.requestDimensionEnforcement();
          }
        }

        if (
          flowMode === 'CREATOR_DESIGN' &&
          window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig &&
          window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig.refreshCreatorFinalReview
        ) {
          window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig.refreshCreatorFinalReview();
        }
      }, 0);
    }

    if (!isShown) {
      panel.__chExampleCleanupRequested = false;
    }
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', window.chPitchPrintSyncFinalOrderPanel);
  } else {
    window.chPitchPrintSyncFinalOrderPanel();
  }

  new MutationObserver(window.chPitchPrintSyncFinalOrderPanel).observe(
    document.documentElement,
    { attributes: true, childList: true, subtree: true, attributeFilter: ['class', 'style'] }
  );

  

  (function () {
    var state = {
      active: false,
      flowMode: "PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE",
      launchContext: null,
      launchContextLocked: false,
      launchContextResolved: false,
      config: null,
      variants: [],
      options: [],
      colorName: null,
      sizeName: null,
      selectedColor: null,
      selectedMethod: null,
      selectedEmbroiderySubtype: null,
      creatorFixedMethod: null,
      creatorArtworkSummary: null,
      creatorArtworkDiagnostics: null,
      publicArtworkSummary: null,
      publicArtworkDiagnostics: null,
      publicArtworkDiagnosticSignature: null,
      creatorPreSaveSummary: null,
      creatorNativeColorVerified: false,
      creatorNativeColorSource: null,
      creatorNativeColorDetail: null,
      methods: [],
      methodConfigReceived: false,
      methodListReceived: false,
      methodConfigSource: null,
      methodConfigRevision: null,
      methodConfigProductId: null,
      methodConfigChannel: null,
      methodTrace: null,
      methodDiagnosticsSignature: null,
      quantities: {},
      variantSelections: [],
      activePreviewColor: null,
      publicVariantMatrixSource: null,
      publicLaunchDiagnostics: null,
      publicConfigAckSignature: null,
      publicCartHandoff: {
        clickedCount: 0,
        valid: null,
        built: false,
        emittedCount: 0,
        eventName: "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_CART_READY",
        target: null,
        targetCount: 0,
        ackReceived: false,
        inFlight: false,
        handoffId: null,
        failureStage: null,
        error: null,
        validation: null
      },
      designDimensionsCm: null,
      hasUnusedExampleText: false,
      initializedFromProduct: false,
      userChangedColor: false,
      userChangedMethod: false,
      userChangedEmbroiderySubtype: false,
      copyrightConfirmed: false,
      nonReturnConfirmed: false
    };

    function normalizeName(value) {
      return String(value || "")
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[åä]/g, "a")
        .replace(/ö/g, "o")
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ")
        .trim();
    }

    function unique(values) {
      var seen = {};
      var out = [];
      values.forEach(function (value) {
        if (value == null || value === "") return;
        var key = String(value);
        if (seen[key]) return;
        seen[key] = true;
        out.push(value);
      });
      return out;
    }

    function colorLabel(value) {
      if (value == null || value === "") return null;
      if (typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "number") {
        return String(value).trim() || null;
      }
      if (typeof value !== "object") return null;

      var candidate =
        value.label != null ? value.label :
        value.name != null ? value.name :
        value.title != null ? value.title :
        value.value;
      return candidate == null ? null : (String(candidate).trim() || null);
    }

    function colorArrayFrom(value) {
      var parsed = parsePossibleJson(value);
      var list = [];

      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        list = parsed;
      } else if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object") {
        list =
          (Array.isArray(parsed.values) && parsed.values) ||
          (Array.isArray(parsed.colors) && parsed.colors) ||
          (Array.isArray(parsed.nodes) && parsed.nodes) ||
          [];
      } else if (parsed != null && parsed !== "") {
        list = [parsed];
      }

      return list.map(colorLabel).filter(Boolean);
    }

    function configuredProductColors(config) {
      if (!config || typeof config !== "object") return [];

      var colors = [];
      [
        "colorValues",
        "colorOptionValues",
        "colors",
        "availableColors",
        "productColors"
      ].forEach(function (key) {
        colors = colors.concat(colorArrayFrom(config[key]));
      });

      var optionCollections = [config.options, config.productOptions];
      optionCollections.forEach(function (collection) {
        var parsed = parsePossibleJson(collection);
        if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return;
        parsed.forEach(function (option) {
          var identity = normalizeName(option && (option.id || option.name || option.label));
          if (identity !== "color" && identity !== "colour" && identity !== "product color") return;
          colors = colors.concat(colorArrayFrom(option.values || option.options));
        });
      });

      var groups = parsePossibleJson(config.optionGroups);
      if (Array.isArray(groups)) {
        groups.forEach(function (group) {
          var identity = normalizeName(group && (group.id || group.name || group.label));
          if (identity !== "color" && identity !== "colour" && identity !== "product color") return;
          colors = colors.concat(colorArrayFrom(group.values || group.options));
        });
      }

      extractFullVariantArray(config).forEach(function (variant) {
        var selectedOptions = Array.isArray(variant && variant.selectedOptions)
          ? variant.selectedOptions
          : [];
        selectedOptions.forEach(function (option) {
          var identity = normalizeName(option && option.name);
          if (identity === "color" || identity === "colour") {
            var label = colorLabel(option.value);
            if (label) colors.push(label);
          }
        });
      });

      // A saved Creator color can keep an older design editable when the host
      // has no option matrix at all. Never mix it into a real availability
      // list, because an obsolete color must remain invalid.
      if (!colors.length) {
        colors = colors.concat(colorArrayFrom(config.selectedColors));
        [config.fixedColor, config.selectedColor].forEach(function (value) {
          var label = colorLabel(value);
          if (label) colors.push(label);
        });
      }

      return unique(colors);
    }

    function findOptionName(options, aliases) {
      var names = (options || []).map(function (option) {
        return typeof option === "string" ? option : option && option.name;
      }).filter(Boolean);

      for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i += 1) {
        var candidate = normalizeName(names[i]);
        for (var j = 0; j < aliases.length; j += 1) {
          if (candidate === normalizeName(aliases[j])) return names[i];
        }
      }
      return null;
    }

    function optionMapFromVariant(variant, optionDefinitions) {
      var map = {};
      if (!variant) return map;

      // Shopify Admin GraphQL commonly returns:
      // selectedOptions: [{ name: "Color", value: "White" }, ...]
      // Support it without requiring a separate `options` definition array.
      if (Array.isArray(variant.selectedOptions)) {
        variant.selectedOptions.forEach(function (item) {
          if (item && item.name != null) {
            map[String(item.name)] = item.value;
          }
        });
      }

      if (variant.options && !Array.isArray(variant.options) && typeof variant.options === "object") {
        Object.keys(variant.options).forEach(function (key) {
          map[key] = variant.options[key];
        });
      } else if (Array.isArray(variant.options)) {
        variant.options.forEach(function (item, index) {
          if (item && typeof item === "object" && item.name != null) {
            map[item.name] = item.value;
          } else {
            var def = optionDefinitions && optionDefinitions[index];
            var name = typeof def === "string" ? def : def && def.name;
            if (name) map[name] = item;
          }
        });
      }

      ["option1", "option2", "option3"].forEach(function (key, index) {
        if (variant[key] == null) return;
        var def = optionDefinitions && optionDefinitions[index];
        var name = typeof def === "string" ? def : def && def.name;
        if (name && map[name] == null) map[name] = variant[key];
      });

      return map;
    }

    function readOption(map, name) {
      if (!name || !map) return null;
      if (map[name] != null) return map[name];

      var target = normalizeName(name);
      var keys = Object.keys(map);
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i += 1) {
        if (normalizeName(keys[i]) === target) return map[keys[i]];
      }
      return null;
    }

    function normalizeVariantId(value) {
      if (value == null) return "";
      return String(value).replace(/^gid:\/\/shopify\/ProductVariant\//, "");
    }

    function normalizeMoneyFromMinor(minor) {
      if (minor == null || minor === "" || !isFinite(Number(minor))) {
        return null;
      }
      return Math.round(Number(minor)) / 100;
    }

    function normalizeVariantPrice(variant) {
      if (!variant) return null;

      var explicitMinor =
        variant.priceMinor != null ? variant.priceMinor :
        variant.unitPriceMinor != null ? variant.unitPriceMinor :
        variant.priceInMinor != null ? variant.priceInMinor :
        null;

      var fromMinor = normalizeMoneyFromMinor(explicitMinor);
      if (fromMinor != null) return fromMinor;

      var price =
        variant.price != null ? variant.price :
        variant.unitPrice != null ? variant.unitPrice :
        variant.priceAmount != null ? variant.priceAmount :
        null;

      if (price && typeof price === "object") {
        if (price.amount != null) price = price.amount;
        else if (price.value != null) price = price.value;
        else if (price.amountMinor != null) {
          return normalizeMoneyFromMinor(price.amountMinor);
        }
      }

      if (price == null || price === "" || !isFinite(Number(price))) {
        return null;
      }

      return Number(price);
    }

    function deriveOptionDefinitions(config, variants) {
      var configured = config && Array.isArray(config.options)
        ? config.options.filter(Boolean)
        : [];

      if (configured.length) return configured;

      var configuredGroups = parsePossibleJson(config && config.optionGroups);
      if (isPublicFlow() && Array.isArray(configuredGroups) && configuredGroups.length) {
        var groupDefinitions = configuredGroups.map(function (group) {
          if (!group || typeof group !== "object") return null;
          var name = group.name || group.label || group.id;
          if (!name) return null;
          return {
            name: String(name),
            id: group.id != null ? String(group.id) : null,
            values: colorArrayFrom(group.values || group.options)
          };
        }).filter(Boolean);
        if (groupDefinitions.length) return groupDefinitions;
      }

      var names = [];
      var seen = {};

      (variants || []).forEach(function (variant) {
        if (Array.isArray(variant && variant.selectedOptions)) {
          variant.selectedOptions.forEach(function (item) {
            var name = item && item.name;
            var key = normalizeName(name);
            if (!key || seen[key]) return;
            seen[key] = true;
            names.push({ name: String(name) });
          });
        }

        if (Array.isArray(variant && variant.options)) {
          variant.options.forEach(function (item) {
            var name = item && typeof item === "object" ? item.name : null;
            var key = normalizeName(name);
            if (!key || seen[key]) return;
            seen[key] = true;
            names.push({ name: String(name) });
          });
        }
      });

      if (!names.length && config && config.optionNames) {
        ["color", "size"].forEach(function (key) {
          var name = config.optionNames[key];
          var normalized = normalizeName(name);
          if (!normalized || seen[normalized]) return;
          seen[normalized] = true;
          names.push({ name: String(name) });
        });
      }

      return names;
    }

    function normalizeVariant(variant, optionDefinitions) {
      var rawId = variant && (
        variant.id ||
        variant.variantId ||
        variant.legacyResourceId
      );

      var id = normalizeVariantId(rawId);
      var rawGid = variant && (
        variant.gid ||
        variant.adminGraphqlApiId ||
        (
          String(rawId || "").indexOf("gid://") === 0
            ? String(rawId)
            : null
        )
      );

      var available = true;
      if (variant) {
        if (variant.available === false) available = false;
        if (variant.availableForSale === false) available = false;
        if (variant.availableForSale === true) available = true;
      }

      return {
        id: id,
        gid: rawGid || null,
        title: variant && variant.title ? String(variant.title) : "",
        available: available,
        price: normalizeVariantPrice(variant),
        options: optionMapFromVariant(variant, optionDefinitions),
        raw: variant
      };
    }

    function getSelectedMethod() {
      return state.methods.find(function (method) {
        return method.id === state.selectedMethod;
      }) || null;
    }

    function methodCode(value) {
      if (value && typeof value === "object") value = value.code || value.id || value.key || value.method || value.label;
      return value == null ? "" : String(value).trim();
    }

    function sameMethod(left, right) {
      return normalizeName(methodCode(left)) === normalizeName(methodCode(right));
    }

    function isEmbroidery(method) {
      return !!method && sameMethod(method.id, "EMBROIDERY");
    }

    function normalizeEmbroiderySubtype(value) {
      var code = String(value || "").trim().toUpperCase();
      return code === "TEXT_ONLY" || code === "IMAGE_OR_LOGO" ? code : null;
    }

    function embroiderySubtypeConfig(method, subtype) {
      var code = normalizeEmbroiderySubtype(subtype);
      if (!method || !code || !method.embroideryTypes) return null;
      var tier = method.embroideryTypes[code];
      return tier && tier.enabled !== false ? tier : null;
    }

    function rawEmbroideryPricingConfig(config) {
      if (!config || typeof config !== "object") return null;
      var pricingMap = config.productionMethodPricing || config.production_method_pricing;
      if (pricingMap && typeof pricingMap === "object") {
        var mapKeys = Object.keys(pricingMap);
        for (var mapIndex = 0; mapIndex < mapKeys.length; mapIndex += 1) {
          if (normalizeName(mapKeys[mapIndex]) === "embroidery") {
            return pricingMap[mapKeys[mapIndex]];
          }
        }
      }
      if (Array.isArray(config.productionMethods)) {
        var arrayMatch = config.productionMethods.find(function (method) {
          return isEmbroidery(method);
        });
        if (arrayMatch) return arrayMatch;
      }
      return config.embroideryPricing && typeof config.embroideryPricing === "object"
        ? config.embroideryPricing
        : null;
    }

    function rawEmbroiderySubtypeConfig(config, subtype) {
      var rawEmbroidery = rawEmbroideryPricingConfig(config);
      var code = normalizeEmbroiderySubtype(subtype);
      if (!rawEmbroidery || !code) return null;
      var subtypeMap = rawEmbroidery.embroiderySubtypes ||
        rawEmbroidery.embroideryTypes ||
        rawEmbroidery.embroidery_subtypes ||
        rawEmbroidery.embroidery_types ||
        rawEmbroidery;
      return subtypeMap && subtypeMap[code] || null;
    }

    function embroiderySubtypePriceMinor(config) {
      if (!config || typeof config !== "object") return null;
      if (
        Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(config, "surchargeMinor") &&
        config.surchargeMinor !== "" &&
        config.surchargeMinor != null &&
        isFinite(Number(config.surchargeMinor)) &&
        Number(config.surchargeMinor) >= 0
      ) {
        return Math.round(Number(config.surchargeMinor));
      }
      if (
        Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(config, "surcharge") &&
        config.surcharge !== "" &&
        config.surcharge != null &&
        isFinite(Number(config.surcharge)) &&
        Number(config.surcharge) >= 0
      ) {
        return Math.round(Number(config.surcharge) * 100);
      }
      return null;
    }

    function resolvedEmbroideryMethod(method, subtype) {
      if (!isEmbroidery(method)) return method;

      var normalizedSubtype = normalizeEmbroiderySubtype(subtype);
      var configured = embroiderySubtypeConfig(method, normalizedSubtype);
      var configuredPriceMinor = embroiderySubtypePriceMinor(configured);
      var copy = {};
      Object.keys(method).forEach(function (key) { copy[key] = method[key]; });
      if (!configured || configuredPriceMinor == null) {
        if (isPublicFlow() || state.methodConfigReceived) {
          copy.surchargeMinor = null;
          copy.surcharge = null;
          copy.feeVariantId = "";
          copy.feeVariantGid = "";
          copy.embroiderySubtype = normalizedSubtype;
          copy.embroiderySubtypeLabel = null;
          copy.pricingResolved = false;
          copy.pricingUnavailable = !!copy.embroiderySubtype && configuredPriceMinor == null;
          return copy;
        }
        return method;
      }
      copy.surchargeMinor = configuredPriceMinor;
      copy.surcharge = copy.surchargeMinor / 100;
      copy.feeVariantId = configured.feeVariantId || "";
      copy.feeVariantGid = configured.feeVariantGid || "";
      copy.embroiderySubtype = normalizedSubtype;
      copy.embroiderySubtypeLabel = configured.label || normalizedSubtype;
      copy.pricingResolved = true;
      return copy;
    }

    function effectiveSelectedMethod() {
      return resolvedEmbroideryMethod(getSelectedMethod(), state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype);
    }

    function formatMoney(value) {
      var number = Number(value);
      if (!isFinite(number)) return "Calculated at checkout";

      var currency = state.config && state.config.currency
        ? state.config.currency
        : "SEK";

      try {
        return new Intl.NumberFormat(undefined, {
          style: "currency",
          currency: currency
        }).format(number);
      } catch (error) {
        return number.toFixed(2) + " " + currency;
      }
    }

    function setNativeOkEnabled(enabled) {
      var nativeButton = document.querySelector(
        '#container [data-module="preview"] [data-cmd="ok"]'
      );
      var continueButton = document.getElementById("ch-continue-cart");
      var productionBlocked = !state.methodConfigReceived || !state.methods.length;

      if ((state.active || productionBlocked) && nativeButton) {
        nativeButton.disabled = productionBlocked || !enabled;
        nativeButton.setAttribute("aria-disabled", nativeButton.disabled ? "true" : "false");
        nativeButton.classList.toggle("disabled", nativeButton.disabled);
      }

      if (continueButton) {
        continueButton.disabled = productionBlocked || (state.active ? !enabled : false);
      }
    }

    var FLOW_MODES = {
      PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE: "PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE",
      CREATOR_DESIGN: "CREATOR_DESIGN",
      CREATOR_BUY_ONLY: "CREATOR_BUY_ONLY"
    };

    function explicitFlowMode(config) {
      if (!config || typeof config !== "object") return null;

      // Launch context is authoritative. A Creator still designs a normal/global
      // base Shopify product, so productOrigin alone must never decide the mode.
      var flowMode = normalizeName(config.flowMode || "");
      if (flowMode === "creator buy only" || flowMode === "buy only") {
        return FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_BUY_ONLY;
      }
      if (flowMode === "creator design") {
        return FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_DESIGN;
      }
      if (flowMode === "public customize" || flowMode === "public") {
        return FLOW_MODES.PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE;
      }

      var designMode = normalizeName(config.designMode || "");
      if (designMode === "creator buy only" || designMode === "buy only") {
        return FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_BUY_ONLY;
      }
      if (designMode === "creator design") {
        return FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_DESIGN;
      }
      if (designMode === "customize" || designMode === "public customize") {
        return FLOW_MODES.PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE;
      }

      return null;
    }

    function resolveFlowMode(config) {
      if (!config) return FLOW_MODES.PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE;

      var explicit = explicitFlowMode(config);
      if (explicit) return explicit;

      // Legacy explicit Creator intent is supported; product origin is not a role.
      if (config.isCreatorProduct === true) {
        return FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_DESIGN;
      }

      return FLOW_MODES.PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE;
    }

    function normalizeLaunchContext(context) {
      if (!context || typeof context !== "object") return null;

      var mode = explicitFlowMode(context);
      if (!mode && context.isCreatorProduct === true) {
        mode = FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_DESIGN;
      }
      if (!mode) return null;

      var creatorDesign = mode === FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_DESIGN;
      var creatorBuyOnly = mode === FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_BUY_ONLY;
      var fixedProductionMethod = creatorDesign
        ? methodCode(
            context.fixedProductionMethod ||
            context.selectedProductionMethod ||
            context.productionMethod ||
            ""
          )
        : null;

      return {
        flowMode: mode,
        // productOrigin describes the underlying/base product when supplied.
        // Creator behavior is controlled by flowMode/designMode instead.
        productOrigin: context.productOrigin || context.baseProductOrigin || null,
        baseProductOrigin: context.baseProductOrigin || context.productOrigin || null,
        designMode: creatorDesign
          ? "creator_design"
          : (creatorBuyOnly ? "buy_only" : "customize"),
        isCreatorProduct: creatorDesign || creatorBuyOnly || context.isCreatorProduct === true,
        creatorProductId:
          context.creatorProductId || context.creator_product_id || null,
        creatorPublicHandle:
          context.creatorPublicHandle || context.publicHandle || context.creator_public_handle || null,
        launchSource: context.launchSource || context.source || null,
        fixedColor: creatorDesign ? (context.fixedColor || context.selectedColor || null) : null,
        fixedProductionMethod: fixedProductionMethod
      };
    }

    function mergeLaunchContextIntoConfig(config) {
      var source = config && typeof config === "object" ? config : {};
      var merged = {};

      Object.keys(source).forEach(function (key) {
        merged[key] = source[key];
      });

      if (!state.launchContext) return merged;

      // Once Creator launch context is locked, later generic/public product
      // config refreshes may enrich variants/pricing but cannot downgrade mode.
      [
        "flowMode",
        "designMode",
        "isCreatorProduct",
        "creatorProductId",
        "creatorPublicHandle",
        "launchSource",
        "baseProductOrigin",
        "fixedColor",
        "fixedProductionMethod"
      ].forEach(function (key) {
        var value = state.launchContext[key];
        if (value !== undefined && value !== null && value !== "") {
          merged[key] = value;
        }
      });

      // Preserve the base product's own origin if the richer product snapshot
      // already provides it. Creator interaction mode does not require origin=creator.
      if (merged.productOrigin == null && state.launchContext.productOrigin) {
        merged.productOrigin = state.launchContext.productOrigin;
      }

      return merged;
    }

    function applyLaunchContext(context) {
      var normalized = normalizeLaunchContext(context);
      if (!normalized) return false;

      var nextMode = normalized.flowMode;
      var creatorMode =
        nextMode === FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_DESIGN ||
        nextMode === FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_BUY_ONLY;

      // Never let a late public/default response unlock a Creator editor.
      if (state.launchContextLocked && !creatorMode) return false;

      state.launchContext = normalized;
      state.launchContextResolved = true;
      if (creatorMode) state.launchContextLocked = true;

      var base = state.config || {};
      setConfig(base);
      syncNextStepGuideCopy();
      return true;
    }

    function isPublicFlow() {
      return state.flowMode === FLOW_MODES.PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE;
    }

    function isCreatorDesignFlow() {
      return state.flowMode === FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_DESIGN;
    }

    // V10.55 — Keep the top-toolbar helper copy in sync with late Creator
    // launch context. PitchPrint can receive the Creator context after the
    // guide has already mounted, so never rely on the initial static text.
    function syncNextStepGuideCopy() {
      var title = document.getElementById("ch-design-step-title");
      var message = document.getElementById("ch-design-step-message");

      var config = state.config || {};
      var launchContext = state.launchContext || {};
      var configLaunch = normalizeName(
        config.launchContext || config.launchSource || ""
      );

      var creatorDesign =
        isCreatorDesignFlow() ||
        launchContext.flowMode === FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_DESIGN ||
        config.creatorContext === true ||
        configLaunch === "creator dashboard";

      if (title) {
        title.textContent = "CUSTOMISE YOUR GARMENT";
      }

      if (message) {
        message.textContent = creatorDesign
          ? "Next step: review and save your design."
          : "Next step: choose printing method, colors, sizes & quantities.";
      }
    }

    function isSupportedEditorConfig(config) {
      if (!config || config.enabled === false) return false;
      if (resolveFlowMode(config) === FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_BUY_ONLY) return false;
      if (
        resolveFlowMode(config) === FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_DESIGN &&
        configuredProductColors(config).length > 0
      ) {
        return true;
      }
      return extractFullVariantArray(config).length > 0;
    }

    function activeVariants() {
      return state.variants.filter(function (variant) {
        if (!variant.available) return false;
        if (state.colorName && state.selectedColor != null) {
          return normalizeName(readOption(variant.options, state.colorName)) ===
            normalizeName(state.selectedColor);
        }
        return true;
      });
    }

    function publicMatrixMode() {
      return isPublicFlow() && state.variants.length > 0 && !!state.sizeName;
    }

    function publicOrderConfigReady() {
      if (!isPublicFlow() || !state.active || !state.variants.length) return false;

      var colors = publicMatrixColors();
      var sizes = publicMatrixSizes();
      var expectsMatrix = colors.length > 1 || sizes.length > 1;
      return !expectsMatrix || publicMatrixMode();
    }

    function configuredOptionValues(optionName, fallbackValues) {
      var values = [];
      var target = normalizeName(optionName);
      (state.options || []).forEach(function (option) {
        var name = typeof option === "string" ? option : option && option.name;
        if (normalizeName(name) !== target) return;
        if (option && Array.isArray(option.values)) values = values.concat(option.values);
      });
      if (Array.isArray(fallbackValues)) values = values.concat(fallbackValues);
      return unique(values.filter(function (value) {
        return value != null && String(value).trim() !== "";
      }).map(String));
    }

    function publicMatrixColors() {
      if (!state.colorName) return ["Selected"];
      var configured = state.config && (
        state.config.colorValues || state.config.colors || state.config.colorOptionValues
      );
      return unique(configuredOptionValues(state.colorName, configured).concat(
        state.variants.map(function (variant) {
          return readOption(variant.options, state.colorName);
        }).filter(Boolean).map(String)
      ));
    }

    function publicMatrixSizes() {
      if (!state.sizeName) return [];
      var configured = state.config && (
        state.config.sizeValues || state.config.sizes || state.config.sizeOptionValues
      );
      return unique(configuredOptionValues(state.sizeName, configured).concat(
        state.variants.map(function (variant) {
          return readOption(variant.options, state.sizeName);
        }).filter(Boolean).map(String)
      ));
    }

    function publicSelectionByVariantId(variantId) {
      var id = normalizeVariantId(variantId);
      return state.variantSelections.find(function (selection) {
        return selection.variantId === id;
      }) || null;
    }

    function publicVariantSelections(positiveOnly) {
      var selections = state.variantSelections.filter(function (selection) {
        return selection.available !== false && (!positiveOnly || selection.quantity > 0);
      });
      return selections.map(function (selection) {
        return {
          variantId: selection.variantId,
          variantGid: selection.variantGid,
          color: selection.color,
          size: selection.size,
          quantity: clampQuantity(selection.quantity),
          unitPriceMinor: selection.unitPriceMinor,
          unitPrice: selection.unitPriceMinor == null ? null : selection.unitPriceMinor / 100,
          available: selection.available !== false
        };
      });
    }

    function initializePublicVariantSelections(config, allowInitial) {
      if (!isPublicFlow()) return [];
      var previous = {};
      state.variantSelections.forEach(function (selection) {
        previous[selection.variantId] = clampQuantity(selection.quantity);
      });
      var initialId = allowInitial
        ? normalizeVariantId(config && config.initialVariantId || "")
        : "";
      var initialQuantity = allowInitial && config && config.initialQuantity != null
        ? clampQuantity(config.initialQuantity)
        : 0;
      state.variantSelections = state.variants.map(function (variant) {
        var quantity = previous[variant.id] || 0;
        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(previous, variant.id) && variant.id === initialId) {
          quantity = initialQuantity;
        }
        if (!variant.available) quantity = 0;
        return {
          variantId: variant.id,
          variantGid: variant.gid || null,
          color: state.colorName ? String(readOption(variant.options, state.colorName) || "") : null,
          size: state.sizeName ? String(readOption(variant.options, state.sizeName) || "") : null,
          quantity: quantity,
          unitPriceMinor: variant.price == null ? null : Math.round(Number(variant.price) * 100),
          available: variant.available !== false
        };
      });
      state.quantities = {};
      return state.variantSelections;
    }

    function setPublicVariantQuantity(variantId, quantity) {
      var selection = publicSelectionByVariantId(variantId);
      if (!selection || selection.available === false) return false;
      selection.quantity = clampQuantity(quantity);
      return true;
    }

    function publicVariantMatrixSource() {
      var colors = publicMatrixColors();
      var sizes = publicMatrixSizes();
      var expectedMany = colors.length > 1 || sizes.length > 1;
      if (state.variants.length <= 1 && expectedMany) return "MISSING_FROM_CUSTOMHOUSE";
      if (state.variants.length <= 1) return "SINGLE_VARIANT_PRODUCT";
      var configuredCount = Number(state.config && (
        state.config.totalVariantCount || state.config.variantCount ||
        state.config.product && state.config.product.variantCount
      ));
      if (isFinite(configuredCount) && configuredCount > 0 && configuredCount === state.variants.length) {
        return "CUSTOMHOUSE_FULL_MATRIX";
      }
      return "SUPPLIED_FROM_CUSTOMHOUSE";
    }

    function publicMatrixModel() {
      var colors = publicMatrixColors();
      var sizes = publicMatrixSizes();
      return {
        colors: colors,
        sizes: sizes,
        source: publicVariantMatrixSource(),
        groups: colors.map(function (color) {
          return {
            color: color,
            rows: sizes.map(function (size) {
              var variant = state.variants.find(function (candidate) {
                var colorMatches = !state.colorName || normalizeName(readOption(candidate.options, state.colorName)) === normalizeName(color);
                var sizeMatches = normalizeName(readOption(candidate.options, state.sizeName)) === normalizeName(size);
                return colorMatches && sizeMatches;
              }) || null;
              return {
                color: color,
                size: size,
                variant: variant,
                selection: variant ? publicSelectionByVariantId(variant.id) : null,
                disabled: !variant || variant.available === false
              };
            })
          };
        })
      };
    }

    function logPublicLaunchDiagnostics() {
      if (!isPublicFlow()) return null;
      var methodById = function (id) {
        return state.methods.find(function (method) { return sameMethod(method.id, id); }) || null;
      };
      var embroidery = methodById("embroidery");
      var dtf = methodById("dtf");
      var dtg = methodById("dtg");
      var textConfig = embroiderySubtypeConfig(embroidery, "TEXT_ONLY");
      var imageConfig = embroiderySubtypeConfig(embroidery, "IMAGE_OR_LOGO");
      var colors = publicMatrixColors();
      var sizes = publicMatrixSizes();
      var textPriceMinor = embroiderySubtypePriceMinor(textConfig);
      var imagePriceMinor = embroiderySubtypePriceMinor(imageConfig);
      var currency = state.config && state.config.currency || "SEK";
      var safeKeys = Object.keys(state.config || {}).filter(function (key) {
        return !/(token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|image|base64)/i.test(key);
      }).slice(0, 100);
      var artworkType = normalizeEmbroiderySubtype(state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype);
      var rawEmbroidery = rawEmbroideryPricingConfig(state.config);
      var rawSubtype = rawEmbroiderySubtypeConfig(state.config, artworkType);
      var normalizedSubtype = embroiderySubtypeConfig(embroidery, artworkType);
      var normalizedSurcharge = embroiderySubtypePriceMinor(normalizedSubtype);
      var rawSubtypeCollection = rawEmbroidery && (
        rawEmbroidery.embroiderySubtypes || rawEmbroidery.embroidery_subtypes
      );
      var expectedPricePrefix = rawSubtypeCollection
        ? "embroiderySubtypes."
        : "embroideryTypes.";
      var resolutionFailure = null;
      if (!artworkType) resolutionFailure = "NO_PRINTABLE_ARTWORK";
      else if (!rawEmbroidery) resolutionFailure = "EMBROIDERY_CONFIG_MISSING";
      else if (!rawSubtype && !normalizedSubtype) resolutionFailure = "EMBROIDERY_SUBTYPE_MISSING";
      else if (normalizedSurcharge == null) resolutionFailure = "EMBROIDERY_SUBTYPE_PRICE_MISSING";
      var diagnostics = {
        PUBLIC_RECEIVED_CONFIG_KEYS: safeKeys,
        PUBLIC_RECEIVED_VARIANT_COUNT: state.variants.length,
        PUBLIC_RECEIVED_VARIANTS: state.variants.slice(0, 250).map(function (variant) {
          return {
            variantId: variant.id,
            variantGid: variant.gid,
            color: state.colorName ? readOption(variant.options, state.colorName) : null,
            size: state.sizeName ? readOption(variant.options, state.sizeName) : null,
            options: variant.options,
            available: variant.available,
            price: variant.price
          };
        }),
        PUBLIC_RECEIVED_COLORS: publicMatrixColors(),
        PUBLIC_RECEIVED_SIZES: publicMatrixSizes(),
        PUBLIC_RECEIVED_EMBROIDERY_TEXT_PRICE: embroiderySubtypePriceMinor(textConfig),
        PUBLIC_RECEIVED_EMBROIDERY_IMAGE_PRICE: embroiderySubtypePriceMinor(imageConfig),
        PUBLIC_VARIANT_COUNT: state.variants.length,
        PUBLIC_COLORS: publicMatrixColors(),
        PUBLIC_SIZES: publicMatrixSizes(),
        PUBLIC_TEXT_PRICE: embroiderySubtypePriceMinor(textConfig),
        PUBLIC_IMAGE_PRICE: embroiderySubtypePriceMinor(imageConfig),
        PUBLIC_VARIANT_MATRIX_SOURCE: publicVariantMatrixSource(),
        PP_PUBLIC_CONFIG_RECEIVED: true,
        PP_PUBLIC_VARIANT_COUNT: state.variants.length,
        PP_PUBLIC_COLORS: colors,
        PP_PUBLIC_SIZES: sizes,
        PP_PUBLIC_TEXT_PRICE: textPriceMinor,
        PP_PUBLIC_IMAGE_PRICE: imagePriceMinor,
        PP_PUBLIC_DTF_PRICE: dtf && dtf.surchargeMinor != null ? dtf.surchargeMinor : null,
        PP_PUBLIC_DTG_PRICE: dtg && dtg.surchargeMinor != null ? dtg.surchargeMinor : null,
        PP_PUBLIC_CURRENCY: currency,
        PP_PUBLIC_CONFIG_KEYS: safeKeys,
        PP_EMBROIDERY_CONFIG: rawEmbroidery,
        PP_TEXT_PRICE_RAW: rawEmbroiderySubtypeConfig(state.config, "TEXT_ONLY"),
        PP_IMAGE_PRICE_RAW: rawEmbroiderySubtypeConfig(state.config, "IMAGE_OR_LOGO"),
        PP_CURRENCY: currency,
        PUBLIC_ARTWORK_TYPE: artworkType || "NO_ARTWORK",
        EXPECTED_PRICE_KEY: artworkType
          ? expectedPricePrefix + artworkType + ".surchargeMinor"
          : null,
        RECEIVED_PRICE: rawSubtype && rawSubtype.surchargeMinor != null
          ? rawSubtype.surchargeMinor
          : null,
        NORMALIZED_SURCHARGE: normalizedSurcharge,
        PRICE_RESOLUTION_FAILURE_REASON: resolutionFailure
      };
      state.publicVariantMatrixSource = diagnostics.PUBLIC_VARIANT_MATRIX_SOURCE;
      state.publicLaunchDiagnostics = diagnostics;
      if (window.console && typeof console.info === "function") {
        Object.keys(diagnostics).forEach(function (key) {
          console.info(key, diagnostics[key]);
        });
        if (diagnostics.PUBLIC_VARIANT_MATRIX_SOURCE === "MISSING_FROM_CUSTOMHOUSE") {
          console.info("PUBLIC_VARIANTS_MISSING_FROM_LAUNCH_CONFIG");
        }
      }

      var acknowledgement = {
        variantCount: state.variants.length,
        colorCount: colors.length,
        sizeCount: sizes.length,
        hasTextPrice: textPriceMinor != null,
        hasImagePrice: imagePriceMinor != null,
        hasDtfPrice: !!(dtf && dtf.surchargeMinor != null),
        hasDtgPrice: !!(dtg && dtg.surchargeMinor != null),
        currency: currency
      };
      var acknowledgementSignature = JSON.stringify(acknowledgement);
      if (
        publicOrderConfigReady() &&
        acknowledgementSignature !== state.publicConfigAckSignature
      ) {
        state.publicConfigAckSignature = acknowledgementSignature;
        if (window.console && typeof console.info === "function") {
          console.info("PITCHPRINT_PUBLIC_CONFIG_READY", acknowledgement);
        }
        try {
          window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(
            "customhouse:pitchprint-public-config-ready",
            { detail: acknowledgement }
          ));
        } catch (error) {}
        postToHost("PITCHPRINT_PUBLIC_CONFIG_READY", acknowledgement);
      }
      return diagnostics;
    }

    function resolveSizeRows() {
      var variants = activeVariants();
      var rows = [];

      if (!state.sizeName) {
        if (variants[0]) {
          rows.push({
            label: "Quantity",
            variant: variants[0]
          });
        }
        return rows;
      }

      var seen = {};

      variants.forEach(function (variant) {
        var size = readOption(variant.options, state.sizeName);
        if (size == null || size === "") return;

        var key = normalizeName(size);
        if (!key || seen[key]) return;
        seen[key] = true;

        rows.push({
          label: String(size),
          variant: variant
        });
      });

      // Explicit product-page size list fallback.
      if (
        !rows.length &&
        state.config &&
        Array.isArray(state.config.sizeValues)
      ) {
        state.config.sizeValues.forEach(function (size) {
          var sizeKey = normalizeName(size);
          if (!sizeKey || seen[sizeKey]) return;

          var match = state.variants.find(function (variant) {
            if (!variant.available) return false;

            var colorMatches =
              !state.colorName ||
              state.selectedColor == null ||
              normalizeName(readOption(variant.options, state.colorName)) ===
                normalizeName(state.selectedColor);

            if (!colorMatches) return false;

            var mappedSize = readOption(variant.options, state.sizeName);
            if (
              mappedSize != null &&
              normalizeName(mappedSize) === sizeKey
            ) {
              return true;
            }

            return normalizeName(variant.title)
              .split(" ")
              .indexOf(sizeKey) >= 0;
          });

          if (match) {
            seen[sizeKey] = true;
            rows.push({
              label: String(size),
              variant: match
            });
          }
        });
      }

      return rows;
    }

    function clampQuantity(value) {
      var number = parseInt(value, 10);
      if (!isFinite(number) || number < 0) return 0;

      var max = state.config && state.config.maxQuantityPerVariant;
      if (max != null && number > Number(max)) return Number(max);

      return number;
    }

    function collectSelections() {
      if (publicMatrixMode()) return publicVariantSelections(true);
      return state.variants.map(function (variant) {
        var quantity = clampQuantity(
          state.quantities[variant.id] || 0
        );

        return {
          variantId: variant.id,
          variantGid: variant.gid || null,
          color: state.colorName
            ? String(
                readOption(
                  variant.options,
                  state.colorName
                ) || ""
              )
            : null,
          size: state.sizeName
            ? String(
                readOption(
                  variant.options,
                  state.sizeName
                ) || ""
              )
            : null,
          quantity: quantity,
          unitPrice: variant.price,
          available: variant.available !== false
        };
      }).filter(function (item) {
        return item.quantity > 0 && item.available;
      });
    }

    function selectionsForColor(color) {
      var target = normalizeName(color);

      return collectSelections().filter(function (item) {
        return normalizeName(item.color) === target;
      });
    }

    function quantityForColor(color) {
      return selectionsForColor(color).reduce(function (sum, item) {
        return sum + clampQuantity(item.quantity);
      }, 0);
    }

    function totalQuantity() {
      return collectSelections().reduce(function (sum, item) {
        return sum + item.quantity;
      }, 0);
    }

    function calculatePriceBreakdown() {
      var artwork = isPublicFlow()
        ? deriveCurrentArtworkSummary()
        : null;
      var method = effectiveSelectedMethod();
      var surchargeMinor = method && method.surchargeMinor != null && isFinite(Number(method.surchargeMinor))
        ? Math.round(Number(method.surchargeMinor))
        : (method && method.surcharge != null && isFinite(Number(method.surcharge))
            ? Math.round(Number(method.surcharge) * 100)
            : null);
      var selections = collectSelections();
      var placementCount = isPublicFlow()
        ? Math.max(0, Number(artwork && artwork.placementCount || 0))
        : 1;

      if (!selections.length) {
        return {
          baseTotalMinor: null,
          productionTotalMinor: null,
          totalPriceMinor: null,
          totalPrice: null
        };
      }

      // Display calculation uses integer minor units so 2-decimal Admin
      // surcharges do not accumulate JavaScript floating-point drift.
      // Checkout remains authoritative on the app/server side.
      var baseTotalMinor = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < selections.length; i += 1) {
        var item = selections[i];
        var unitPriceMinor = item.unitPriceMinor;

        if (unitPriceMinor == null || !isFinite(Number(unitPriceMinor))) {
          var unitPrice = item.unitPrice;
          if (unitPrice == null || !isFinite(Number(unitPrice))) {
            unitPrice = configBasePrice(state.config);
            if (unitPrice == null) {
              return {
                baseTotalMinor: null,
                productionTotalMinor: null,
                totalPriceMinor: null,
                totalPrice: null
              };
            }
          }
          unitPriceMinor = Math.round(Number(unitPrice) * 100);
        }
        baseTotalMinor += Math.round(Number(unitPriceMinor)) * item.quantity;
      }

      var pricingResolved = surchargeMinor != null && (!isPublicFlow() || placementCount > 0);
      var productionTotalMinor = pricingResolved
        ? surchargeMinor * totalQuantity() * placementCount
        : null;
      var totalPriceMinor = productionTotalMinor == null
        ? null
        : baseTotalMinor + productionTotalMinor;
      return {
        baseTotalMinor: baseTotalMinor,
        productionTotalMinor: productionTotalMinor,
        totalPriceMinor: totalPriceMinor,
        totalPrice: totalPriceMinor == null ? null : totalPriceMinor / 100
      };
    }

    function calculateTotalPrice() {
      return calculatePriceBreakdown().totalPrice;
    }

    function dimensionsValid() {
      var method = getSelectedMethod();
      var dims = state.designDimensionsCm;
      if (!method || !dims) return true;

      var width = Number(dims.width);
      var height = Number(dims.height);
      if (!isFinite(width) || !isFinite(height)) return true;

      return (!(Number(method.maxWidthCm) > 0) || width <= Number(method.maxWidthCm)) &&
        (!(Number(method.maxHeightCm) > 0) || height <= Number(method.maxHeightCm));
    }



    function postToHost(type, payload) {
      var targets = [];

      // PitchPrint may be embedded in an iframe OR opened as a separate popup
      // from the Creator Dashboard. In popup mode parent/top point back to this
      // same window, so the dashboard is reachable only through window.opener.
      // Include all valid host windows and de-duplicate them.
      try {
        if (
          window.opener &&
          window.opener !== window &&
          !window.opener.closed &&
          targets.indexOf(window.opener) === -1
        ) {
          targets.push(window.opener);
        }
      } catch (error) {}

      try {
        if (window.parent && window.parent !== window && targets.indexOf(window.parent) === -1) {
          targets.push(window.parent);
        }
      } catch (error) {}

      try {
        if (window.top && window.top !== window && targets.indexOf(window.top) === -1) {
          targets.push(window.top);
        }
      } catch (error) {}

      targets.forEach(function (target) {
        try {
          target.postMessage({
            type: type,
            payload: payload
          }, "*");
        } catch (error) {}
      });
      return targets.length;
    }

    function requestDesignDimensions() {
      var method = getSelectedMethod();
      var payload = method ? {
        method: method.id,
        maxWidthCm: method.maxWidthCm,
        maxHeightCm: method.maxHeightCm
      } : {};

      try {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(
          "customhouse:pitchprint-request-design-dimensions",
          { detail: payload }
        ));
      } catch (error) {}

      postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_REQUEST_DESIGN_DIMENSIONS", payload);
    }

    function requestDimensionEnforcement() {
      var method = getSelectedMethod();
      if (!method || !(method.maxWidthCm > 0 || method.maxHeightCm > 0)) return;

      var payload = {
        method: method.id,
        label: method.label,
        maxWidthCm: method.maxWidthCm,
        maxHeightCm: method.maxHeightCm
      };

      try {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(
          "customhouse:pitchprint-enforce-dimension-limit",
          { detail: payload }
        ));
      } catch (error) {}

      postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_ENFORCE_DIMENSION_LIMIT", payload);
    }

    function requestUnusedExampleTextCleanup() {
      var examples = (
        state.config &&
        Array.isArray(state.config.exampleTexts) &&
        state.config.exampleTexts.length
      ) ? state.config.exampleTexts : [
        "Your text here",
        "Your Text Here",
        "YOUR TEXT HERE"
      ];

      var payload = {
        examples: examples,
        removeIfUnedited: true
      };

      try {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(
          "customhouse:pitchprint-remove-unused-example-text",
          { detail: payload }
        ));
      } catch (error) {}

      postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_REMOVE_UNUSED_EXAMPLE_TEXT", payload);
    }

    function renderMethodButtons() {
      var list = document.getElementById("ch-production-list");
      if (!list) return;
      if (state.methodTrace) state.methodTrace.beforeRender = state.methods.map(function (method) { return method.id; });
      Array.prototype.slice.call(list.querySelectorAll("[data-method]")).forEach(function (button) {
        if (!state.methods.some(function (method) { return method.id === button.dataset.method; })) button.remove();
      });
      state.methods.forEach(function (method, index) {
        var id = method.id;
        var button = Array.prototype.find.call(list.querySelectorAll("[data-method]"), function (node) { return node.dataset.method === id; });
        if (!button) {
          button = document.createElement("button");
          button.type = "button";
          button.className = "ch-production-method";
          button.dataset.method = id;
          button.innerHTML = '<span class="ch-method-dot"></span><span class="ch-method-copy"><strong></strong><small></small></span><span class="ch-method-price"></span>';
          button.querySelector(".ch-method-price").setAttribute("data-method-price", id);
          list.appendChild(button);
        }
        if (list.children[index] !== button) list.insertBefore(button, list.children[index] || null);

        button.hidden = false;
        button.classList.toggle("ch-selected", id === state.selectedMethod);
        var artworkType = normalizeEmbroiderySubtype((state.creatorArtworkSummary || state.publicArtworkSummary || {}).artworkType);
        button.disabled = isEmbroidery(method) && !!artworkType && !embroiderySubtypeConfig(method, artworkType);
        button.setAttribute("aria-disabled", button.disabled ? "true" : "false");

        var title = button.querySelector("strong");
        var note = button.querySelector("small");
        var price = button.querySelector("[data-method-price]");
        var displayMethod = isEmbroidery(method)
          ? resolvedEmbroideryMethod(method, state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype)
          : method;

        if (title) title.textContent = method.label;
        if (note) {
          note.textContent = isPublicFlow() && isEmbroidery(method) && displayMethod.embroiderySubtype
            ? String(displayMethod.embroiderySubtypeLabel || (
                displayMethod.embroiderySubtype === "TEXT_ONLY" ? "Text only" : "Image / logo"
              ))
            : (method.maxWidthCm > 0 && method.maxHeightCm > 0
                ? "Maximum artwork " + method.maxWidthCm + " × " + method.maxHeightCm + " cm"
                : method.description);
        }

        if (price) {
          var unresolvedEmbroidery = isEmbroidery(method) && isPublicFlow() && (
            !displayMethod || displayMethod.pricingResolved === false || displayMethod.surchargeMinor == null
          );
          price.textContent = unresolvedEmbroidery
            ? (displayMethod && displayMethod.pricingUnavailable
                ? "Pricing unavailable. Please try again later."
                : "Pricing will be calculated from your artwork.")
            : "+" + formatMoney(Number(displayMethod.surcharge || 0)) + " / placement / item";
        }

        if (!button.__chBound) {
          button.__chBound = true;
          button.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();

            if (button.disabled || !state.methods.some(function (entry) { return entry.id === id; })) return;
            state.selectedMethod = id;
            state.userChangedMethod = true;
            render();

            var selected = getSelectedMethod();
            if (selected) {
              var payload = {
                method: selected.id,
                label: selected.label,
                maxWidthCm: selected.maxWidthCm,
                maxHeightCm: selected.maxHeightCm,
                surcharge: selected.surcharge,
                surchargeMinor: selected.surchargeMinor,
                pricingMode: "per_item_additive",
                adminPricingSource: state.config && state.config.adminPricingSource || null
              };

              try {
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(
                  "customhouse:pitchprint-production-method-changed",
                  { detail: payload }
                ));
              } catch (error) {}

              postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_DIMENSION_LIMIT_CHANGED", payload);

              requestDimensionEnforcement();
              requestDesignDimensions();
            }
          });
        }
      });

      var method = getSelectedMethod();
      var info = document.getElementById("ch-production-info");
      if (info) {
        info.textContent = !state.methodConfigReceived
          ? "Production methods are still loading."
          : !state.methods.length
          ? "No production methods are currently available for this product."
          : method
          ? method.description
          : "Select a production method to see more information.";
      }
    }

    function renderEmbroideryTypes() {
      var section = document.getElementById("ch-embroidery-type-section");
      var reviewRow = document.getElementById("ch-review-embroidery-row");
      var resultLabel = document.getElementById("ch-embroidery-type-label");
      var resultPrice = document.getElementById("ch-embroidery-type-price");
      var method = getSelectedMethod();
      var visible = isEmbroidery(method);
      var creatorDesign = isCreatorDesignFlow();

      if (section) section.hidden = !visible || creatorDesign;
      if (reviewRow) reviewRow.hidden = !visible;
      if (!visible) return;
      if (!creatorDesign) deriveCurrentArtworkSummary();
      var configured = embroiderySubtypeConfig(method, state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype);
      var subtype = normalizeEmbroiderySubtype(state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype);
      var configuredPriceMinor = embroiderySubtypePriceMinor(configured);
      var subtypeLabel = configured && configured.label
        ? String(configured.label)
        : (subtype === "TEXT_ONLY" ? "Text only" : (subtype === "IMAGE_OR_LOGO" ? "Image / logo" : null));
      if (resultLabel) {
        resultLabel.textContent = subtypeLabel || "No printable artwork detected";
      }
      if (resultPrice) {
        resultPrice.textContent = !subtype
          ? "Pricing will be calculated from your artwork."
          : (configuredPriceMinor == null
              ? "Pricing unavailable. Please try again later."
              : "+" + formatMoney(configuredPriceMinor / 100) + " / placement / item");
      }
    }

    function renderColors() {
      var list = document.getElementById("ch-color-list");
      if (!list) return;

      list.innerHTML = "";

      if (!state.active) {
        var pending = document.createElement("span");
        pending.className = "ch-empty-option";
        pending.textContent = "Loading product colors…";
        list.appendChild(pending);
        return;
      }

      var creatorDesign = isCreatorDesignFlow();
      var colors = isPublicFlow() && publicMatrixMode()
        ? publicMatrixColors()
        : configuredProductColors(state.config);

      if (state.colorName) {
        colors = colors.concat(state.variants.filter(function (variant) {
          return variant.available;
        }).map(function (variant) {
          return readOption(variant.options, state.colorName);
        }).filter(Boolean));
      }

      colors = unique(colors);

      if (!colors.length) {
        var empty = document.createElement("span");
        empty.className = "ch-empty-option";
        empty.textContent = "This product has no separate color option.";
        list.appendChild(empty);
        return;
      }

      var configuredCreatorColor = creatorDesign && state.config &&
        (state.config.fixedColor || state.config.selectedColor);
      if (
        configuredCreatorColor &&
        !state.userChangedColor &&
        !state.creatorNativeColorVerified
      ) {
        var matchingCreatorColor = colors.find(function (color) {
          return normalizeName(color) === normalizeName(configuredCreatorColor);
        });
        state.selectedColor = matchingCreatorColor ? String(matchingCreatorColor) : null;
        state.creatorNativeColorVerified = false;
        state.creatorNativeColorSource = null;
      }

      var currentPreviewColor = isPublicFlow()
        ? (state.activePreviewColor || state.selectedColor)
        : state.selectedColor;
      if (isPublicFlow() && !state.userChangedColor) {
        var nativePublicColor = nativeColorTemplateSelection(colors);
        if (nativePublicColor && nativePublicColor.name) {
          currentPreviewColor = nativePublicColor.name;
          state.selectedColor = nativePublicColor.name;
          state.activePreviewColor = nativePublicColor.name;
        }
      }
      var selectedColorExists = currentPreviewColor != null && colors.some(function (color) {
        return normalizeName(color) === normalizeName(currentPreviewColor);
      });
      if (!selectedColorExists) {
        // PitchPrint opens the garment with one product color already active.
        // Use the matching first option immediately, then let the native
        // garment-color synchronizer refine it from the checked swatch.
        state.selectedColor = String(colors[0]);
        if (isPublicFlow()) state.activePreviewColor = state.selectedColor;
        if (creatorDesign) {
          state.creatorNativeColorVerified = false;
          state.creatorNativeColorSource = null;
        }
      }

      colors.forEach(function (color) {
        var button = document.createElement("button");
        button.type = "button";
        button.className =
          "ch-choice" +
          (
            normalizeName(color) === normalizeName(state.selectedColor)
              ? " ch-selected"
              : ""
          );
        var label = document.createElement("span");
        label.textContent = String(color);
        button.appendChild(label);

        var colorQuantity = quantityForColor(color);
        if (isPublicFlow() && colorQuantity > 0) {
          var badge = document.createElement("span");
          badge.className = "ch-color-qty-badge";
          badge.textContent = String(colorQuantity);
          button.appendChild(badge);
        }

        button.setAttribute(
          "aria-label",
          isCreatorDesignFlow()
            ? "Select product color " + String(color)
            : "Edit sizes for color " + String(color)
        );

        button.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
          event.preventDefault();
          event.stopPropagation();

          if (
            normalizeName(isPublicFlow() ? state.activePreviewColor : state.selectedColor) === normalizeName(color)
          ) {
            return;
          }

          state.selectedColor = String(color);
          if (isPublicFlow()) {
            state.activePreviewColor = String(color);
            applyPublicPreviewColor(String(color), colors);
          }
          state.userChangedColor = true;

          if (isCreatorDesignFlow()) {
            // Creator products intentionally have exactly one fixed color and
            // never carry customer order quantities inside the designer.
            state.quantities = {};
          }

          // Preserve quantities from previously edited colors.
          // Quantities are keyed by real Shopify variant ID, so the customer
          // can order multiple colors and sizes with one PitchPrint design.
          render();
        });

        list.appendChild(button);
      });
    }

    function visibleNativeColorElement(element) {
      if (!element || !element.getBoundingClientRect) return false;
      var rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (rect.width < 14 || rect.height < 14) return false;
      var style = window.getComputedStyle ? window.getComputedStyle(element) : null;
      return !style || (style.display !== "none" && style.visibility !== "hidden");
    }

    function nativeColorTemplateRoot() {
      return document.querySelector(
        '#container [data-module="stage-parent"] [data-module="module-color-template"].active, ' +
        '#container [data-module="stage-parent"] [data-module="module-color-template"]'
      );
    }

    function nativeColorTemplateControls(root) {
      if (!root) return [];
      return Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll(
        '.main-div > .round-item[data-cmd="load"][data-id]'
      )).sort(function (left, right) {
        return Number(left.getAttribute("data-id")) - Number(right.getAttribute("data-id"));
      });
    }

    function semanticNativeColor(control, colors) {
      if (!control) return null;
      var values = [
        control.getAttribute("data-color"),
        control.getAttribute("data-value"),
        control.getAttribute("data-label"),
        control.getAttribute("aria-label"),
        control.getAttribute("title"),
        control.textContent
      ];
      for (var i = 0; i < values.length; i += 1) {
        var match = configuredColorMatch(values[i], colors);
        if (match) return match;
      }

      // PitchPrint's live color-template controls expose stable numeric
      // data-id values even when semantic labels are absent. Those IDs map
      // to the configured product color order; DOM position is not used.
      var nativeId = control.getAttribute("data-id");
      var nativeIndex = nativeId == null || nativeId === "" ? NaN : Number(nativeId);
      if (
        isFinite(nativeIndex) &&
        Math.floor(nativeIndex) === nativeIndex &&
        nativeIndex >= 0 &&
        nativeIndex < colors.length
      ) {
        return String(colors[nativeIndex]);
      }
      return null;
    }

    function nativeColorDetail(name, source, control, index, runtimeValue) {
      if (!name) return null;
      var id = control && (
        control.getAttribute("data-color-id") ||
        control.getAttribute("data-template-id") ||
        control.getAttribute("data-id")
      );
      if (!id && runtimeValue && typeof runtimeValue === "object") {
        id = runtimeValue.id || runtimeValue.colorId || runtimeValue.templateId || runtimeValue.key;
      }
      var stableIndex = id == null || id === "" ? NaN : Number(id);
      var resolvedIndex = isFinite(stableIndex) && Math.floor(stableIndex) === stableIndex
        ? stableIndex
        : index;
      return {
        name: String(name),
        value: String(name),
        key: normalizeName(name).replace(/\s+/g, "-"),
        nativeColorId: id == null || id === "" ? null : String(id),
        nativeColorIndex: resolvedIndex == null || resolvedIndex < 0 ? null : Number(resolvedIndex),
        source: source
      };
    }

    function nativeColorTemplateSelection(colors) {
      var root = nativeColorTemplateRoot();
      var controls = nativeColorTemplateControls(root);
      var selected = controls.find(function (item) {
        return !!item.querySelector(".icon-check.selected");
      });
      if (!selected && isCreatorDesignFlow()) {
        // The native swatch panel may be hidden during final review.
        var selectedControls = controls.filter(nativeGarmentColorIsSelected);
        if (selectedControls.length === 1) selected = selectedControls[0];
      }
      return selected
        ? nativeGarmentColorFromControl(selected, controls.indexOf(selected), colors)
        : null;
    }

    function applyPublicPreviewColor(color, colors) {
      if (!isPublicFlow()) return false;
      var controls = nativeColorTemplateControls(nativeColorTemplateRoot());
      var target = controls.find(function (control) {
        return normalizeName(semanticNativeColor(control, colors || [])) === normalizeName(color);
      });
      if (!target) return false;
      var alreadySelected = !!target.querySelector(".icon-check.selected");
      if (!alreadySelected && typeof target.click === "function") target.click();
      return true;
    }

    function runtimeSemanticNativeColor(colors) {
      var runtime = window.ppframe && window.ppframe.dModel && window.ppframe.dModel.runtime;
      if (!runtime) return null;
      var design = runtime.design || {};
      var product = runtime.product || runtime.productData || {};
      var candidates = [
        design.activeGarmentColor,
        design.selectedGarmentColor,
        design.garmentColor,
        design.selectedColor,
        design.color,
        product.activeColor,
        product.selectedColor,
        product.color,
        runtime.activeGarmentColor,
        runtime.selectedGarmentColor
      ];
      for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length; i += 1) {
        var runtimeValue = candidates[i];
        var candidate = runtimeValue;
        if (runtimeValue && typeof runtimeValue === "object") {
          candidate = runtimeValue.name || runtimeValue.label || runtimeValue.value || runtimeValue.title;
        }
        var match = configuredColorMatch(candidate, colors);
        if (match) return nativeColorDetail(match, "pitchprint-runtime", null, null, runtimeValue);
      }
      return null;
    }

    function nativeGarmentColorLabel() {
      if (!document.body || !document.createTreeWalker) return null;
      var walker = document.createTreeWalker(
        document.body,
        window.NodeFilter ? NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT : 4
      );
      var node;
      while ((node = walker.nextNode())) {
        var parent = node.parentElement;
        if (!parent || parent.closest("#ch-order-panel, #all-modules, [data-module=\"context\"]")) {
          continue;
        }
        var text = String(node.nodeValue || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
        if (
          visibleNativeColorElement(parent) &&
          /\b(select|choose|valj|välj)\b.{0,60}\b(color|colour|farg|färg)\b/i.test(text)
        ) {
          return parent;
        }
      }
      return null;
    }

    function nativeGarmentColorControls(label) {
      if (!label) return null;
      var node = label.parentElement;
      var selectors = "button, [role=\"button\"], [aria-pressed], [aria-selected], " +
        "[data-color], [data-value], .swatch, [class*=\"swatch\"], .color, [class*=\"color\"]";

      for (var depth = 0; node && node !== document.body && depth < 7; depth += 1) {
        var all = Array.prototype.slice.call(node.querySelectorAll(selectors)).filter(function (item) {
          if (item === label || item.contains(label)) return false;
          var rect = item.getBoundingClientRect ? item.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
          if (!rect || rect.width > 90 || rect.height > 90) return false;
          return visibleNativeColorElement(item);
        });
        var buttons = all.filter(function (item) {
          return item.matches("button, [role=\"button\"], [aria-pressed], [aria-selected]");
        });
        var controls = buttons.length >= 2 ? buttons : all.filter(function (item) {
          return !all.some(function (other) {
            return other !== item && item.contains(other);
          });
        });
        if (controls.length < 2) {
          var generic = Array.prototype.slice.call(node.querySelectorAll("*")).filter(function (item) {
            if (item === label || item.contains(label) || !visibleNativeColorElement(item)) return false;
            var rect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
            if (rect.width < 16 || rect.height < 16 || rect.width > 72 || rect.height > 72) return false;
            if (Math.abs(rect.width - rect.height) > 14) return false;
            var style = window.getComputedStyle ? window.getComputedStyle(item) : null;
            var interactive = item.matches("[onclick], [tabindex], input, label") ||
              (style && style.cursor === "pointer");
            var rounded = style && parseFloat(style.borderRadius || "0") >= Math.min(rect.width, rect.height) * 0.25;
            var colored = style && style.backgroundColor &&
              style.backgroundColor !== "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)" &&
              style.backgroundColor !== "transparent";
            return interactive || rounded || colored;
          });
          controls = generic.filter(function (item) {
            return !generic.some(function (other) {
              return other !== item && other.contains(item);
            });
          });
        }
        if (controls.length >= 2 && controls.length <= 30) {
          return { root: node, controls: controls };
        }
        node = node.parentElement;
      }
      return null;
    }

    function nativeGarmentColorIsSelected(control) {
      if (!control) return false;
      var controlSelected =
        ".selected, .active, .checked, .is-selected, .is-active, " +
        "[aria-pressed=\"true\"], [aria-selected=\"true\"], [aria-checked=\"true\"], " +
        "[data-selected=\"true\"], input:checked";
      var selectedMark =
        ".icon-check.selected, .selected, .checked, .is-selected, " +
        "[aria-pressed=\"true\"], [aria-selected=\"true\"], [aria-checked=\"true\"], " +
        "[data-selected=\"true\"], input:checked";
      if (control.matches && control.matches(controlSelected)) return true;
      if (control.querySelector && control.querySelector(selectedMark)) return true;
      if (/[✓✔]/.test(String(control.textContent || ""))) return true;
      if (window.getComputedStyle) {
        var after = window.getComputedStyle(control, "::after");
        if (after && /[✓✔]/.test(String(after.content || ""))) return true;
      }
      return false;
    }

    function nativeGarmentColorFromControl(control, index, colors) {
      return nativeColorDetail(
        semanticNativeColor(control, colors),
        "native-selected-control",
        control,
        index,
        null
      );
    }

    function configuredColorMatch(value, colors) {
      var valueName = normalizeName(value);
      if (!valueName) return null;
      var match = colors.find(function (color) {
        var colorName = normalizeName(color);
        return valueName === colorName ||
          valueName.slice(-colorName.length) === colorName ||
          valueName.indexOf(" " + colorName + " ") >= 0;
      });
      return match == null ? null : String(match);
    }

    function nativeGarmentColorIndexFromTarget(target, root) {
      var current = target && target.nodeType === 1 ? target : target && target.parentElement;
      for (var depth = 0; current && current !== root && depth < 7; depth += 1) {
        var parent = current.parentElement;
        if (!parent) break;
        var siblings = Array.prototype.slice.call(parent.children).filter(function (item) {
          if (!visibleNativeColorElement(item)) return false;
          var rect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
          return rect.width >= 16 && rect.height >= 16 &&
            rect.width <= 90 && rect.height <= 90 &&
            Math.abs(rect.width - rect.height) <= 18;
        });
        if (siblings.length >= 2 && siblings.length <= 30) {
          var index = siblings.findIndex(function (item) {
            return item === current || item.contains(target);
          });
          if (index >= 0) return index;
        }
        current = parent;
      }
      return -1;
    }

    function nativeGarmentColorFromEvent(event) {
      if (!event || !isCreatorDesignFlow()) return null;
      var colors = configuredProductColors(state.config);
      if (!colors.length) return null;

      var exactTarget = event.target && event.target.nodeType === 1
        ? event.target
        : event.target && event.target.parentElement;
      var exactControl = exactTarget && exactTarget.closest
        ? exactTarget.closest(
            '[data-module="module-color-template"] .round-item[data-cmd="load"][data-id]'
          )
        : null;
      if (exactControl) return semanticNativeColor(exactControl, colors);

      var label = nativeGarmentColorLabel();
      var group = nativeGarmentColorControls(label);
      var target = event.target && event.target.nodeType === 1
        ? event.target
        : event.target && event.target.parentElement;
      if (!target || !group || !group.root.contains(target)) return null;

      var path = event.composedPath ? event.composedPath() : [];
      if (!path.length) {
        var current = target;
        while (current && current !== group.root.parentElement) {
          path.push(current);
          current = current.parentElement;
        }
      }

      for (var i = 0; i < path.length; i += 1) {
        var item = path[i];
        if (!item || item.nodeType !== 1 || !group.root.contains(item)) continue;
        var values = [
          item.value,
          item.getAttribute && item.getAttribute("data-color"),
          item.getAttribute && item.getAttribute("data-value"),
          item.getAttribute && item.getAttribute("data-label"),
          item.getAttribute && item.getAttribute("aria-label"),
          item.getAttribute && item.getAttribute("title")
        ];
        for (var valueIndex = 0; valueIndex < values.length; valueIndex += 1) {
          var directMatch = configuredColorMatch(values[valueIndex], colors);
          if (directMatch) return directMatch;
        }
