      }

      var controlIndex = group.controls.findIndex(function (control) {
        return control === target || control.contains(target);
      });
      return controlIndex >= 0
        ? semanticNativeColor(group.controls[controlIndex], colors)
        : null;
    }

    function applyCreatorGarmentColor(selection, verifiedSource) {
      if (!selection) return false;
      var detail = typeof selection === "object"
        ? selection
        : nativeColorDetail(selection, verifiedSource || null, null, null, null);
      var selected = detail && detail.name;
      if (!selected) return false;
      verifiedSource = detail.source || verifiedSource || null;

      var verificationChanged = !!verifiedSource && !state.creatorNativeColorVerified;
      if (verifiedSource) {
        state.creatorNativeColorVerified = true;
        state.creatorNativeColorSource = verifiedSource;
        state.creatorNativeColorDetail = detail;
      }

      if (normalizeName(selected) === normalizeName(state.selectedColor)) {
        if (verificationChanged) updateValidation(false);
        return true;
      }

      state.selectedColor = selected;
      state.userChangedColor = true;
      state.quantities = {};
      render();
      return true;
    }

    function syncCreatorGarmentColor(options) {
      if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) return false;
      var requireVerified = options === true || !!(options && options.requireVerified);
      var colors = configuredProductColors(state.config);
      if (!colors.length) return false;

      if (requireVerified) {
        state.creatorNativeColorVerified = false;
        state.creatorNativeColorSource = null;
        state.creatorNativeColorDetail = null;
      }

      var exactSelection = nativeColorTemplateSelection(colors);
      if (exactSelection) return applyCreatorGarmentColor(exactSelection);

      var runtimeSelection = runtimeSemanticNativeColor(colors);
      if (runtimeSelection) return applyCreatorGarmentColor(runtimeSelection);

      var group = nativeGarmentColorControls(nativeGarmentColorLabel());
      if (group) {
        var selectedIndex = group.controls.findIndex(nativeGarmentColorIsSelected);
        if (selectedIndex >= 0) {
          return applyCreatorGarmentColor(nativeGarmentColorFromControl(
            group.controls[selectedIndex], selectedIndex, colors
          ));
        }
      }

      if (requireVerified) return false;

      // Launch/config color is initialization only. Once native/user color
      // state has existed, a generic refresh must never click a stale swatch.
      if (state.userChangedColor || state.creatorNativeColorVerified) return false;

      var desired = configuredColorMatch(
        state.config && (state.config.fixedColor || state.config.selectedColor),
        colors
      );
      var templateControls = nativeColorTemplateControls(nativeColorTemplateRoot());
      var desiredTemplateControl = templateControls.find(function (control) {
        return normalizeName(semanticNativeColor(control, colors)) === normalizeName(desired);
      });
      if (desired && desiredTemplateControl) {
        if (!nativeGarmentColorIsSelected(desiredTemplateControl)) desiredTemplateControl.click();
        var confirmedDesired = nativeColorTemplateSelection(colors);
        return confirmedDesired
          ? applyCreatorGarmentColor(confirmedDesired)
          : applyCreatorGarmentColor(desired, null);
      }
      return false;
    }

    var creatorGarmentColorSyncBound = false;
    var creatorGarmentColorSyncTimers = [];
    var creatorGarmentColorObserver = null;
    var creatorGarmentColorObserverRoot = null;
    var creatorGarmentColorObserverTimer = 0;

    function bindCreatorGarmentColorObserver() {
      if (!window.MutationObserver) return;
      var root = document.querySelector('#container [data-module="stage-parent"]') ||
        document.getElementById("container");
      if (!root || root === creatorGarmentColorObserverRoot) return;
      if (creatorGarmentColorObserver) creatorGarmentColorObserver.disconnect();
      creatorGarmentColorObserverRoot = root;
      creatorGarmentColorObserver = new MutationObserver(function () {
        if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) return;
        clearTimeout(creatorGarmentColorObserverTimer);
        creatorGarmentColorObserverTimer = setTimeout(syncCreatorGarmentColor, 50);
      });
      creatorGarmentColorObserver.observe(root, {
        attributes: true,
        childList: true,
        subtree: true,
        attributeFilter: [
          "class", "style", "aria-checked", "aria-pressed", "aria-selected",
          "data-selected", "data-color", "data-value", "checked", "src"
        ]
      });
    }

    function handleCreatorGarmentColorInteraction(event) {
      if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) return;
      var selected = nativeGarmentColorFromEvent(event);
      if (selected) applyCreatorGarmentColor(selected);
      [0, 60, 180].forEach(function (delay) {
        setTimeout(syncCreatorGarmentColor, delay);
      });
    }

    function scheduleCreatorGarmentColorSync() {
      if (!creatorGarmentColorSyncBound) {
        creatorGarmentColorSyncBound = true;
        ["pointerdown", "mousedown", "touchend", "click", "change"].forEach(function (name) {
          document.addEventListener(name, handleCreatorGarmentColorInteraction, true);
        });
      }
      if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) return;
      bindCreatorGarmentColorObserver();
      creatorGarmentColorSyncTimers.forEach(function (timer) {
        clearTimeout(timer);
      });
      creatorGarmentColorSyncTimers = [];
      [0, 100, 300, 700, 1400, 2500].forEach(function (delay) {
        creatorGarmentColorSyncTimers.push(
          setTimeout(syncCreatorGarmentColor, delay)
        );
      });
    }

    function renderPublicVariantMatrix(list) {
      var model = publicMatrixModel();
      if (!model.groups.length || !model.sizes.length) return false;

      var activeColor = state.activePreviewColor || state.selectedColor;
      var activeGroup = model.groups.find(function (group) {
        return normalizeName(group.color) === normalizeName(activeColor);
      }) || model.groups[0];
      state.activePreviewColor = activeGroup.color;
      state.selectedColor = activeGroup.color;

      [activeGroup].forEach(function (group) {
        var section = document.createElement("section");
        section.className = "ch-variant-color-group";
        section.setAttribute("data-color", group.color);

        var heading = document.createElement("div");
        heading.className = "ch-variant-color-preview";
        heading.textContent = group.color;
        heading.classList.add("ch-selected");
        section.appendChild(heading);

        var rows = document.createElement("div");
        rows.className = "ch-variant-color-rows";
        group.rows.forEach(function (row) {
          var wrapper = document.createElement("div");
          wrapper.className = "ch-variant-matrix-row" + (row.disabled ? " ch-order-unavailable" : "");
          wrapper.setAttribute("data-matrix-color", row.color);
          wrapper.setAttribute("data-matrix-size", row.size);
          if (row.variant) wrapper.setAttribute("data-variant-id", row.variant.id);

          var name = document.createElement("span");
          name.className = "ch-size-name";
          name.textContent = row.size;

          var qty = document.createElement("div");
          qty.className = "ch-qty";
          var minus = document.createElement("button");
          minus.type = "button";
          minus.textContent = "−";
          minus.disabled = row.disabled;
          minus.setAttribute("aria-label", "Decrease " + row.color + " / " + row.size);
          var input = document.createElement("input");
          input.type = "number";
          input.min = "0";
          input.step = "1";
          input.inputMode = "numeric";
          input.disabled = row.disabled;
          input.value = String(row.selection ? clampQuantity(row.selection.quantity) : 0);
          input.setAttribute("aria-label", row.color + " / " + row.size + " quantity");
          var plus = document.createElement("button");
          plus.type = "button";
          plus.textContent = "+";
          plus.disabled = row.disabled;
          plus.setAttribute("aria-label", "Increase " + row.color + " / " + row.size);

          function setQuantity(value) {
            if (!row.variant || !setPublicVariantQuantity(row.variant.id, value)) return;
            input.value = String(publicSelectionByVariantId(row.variant.id).quantity);
            updateValidation(false);
            renderColors();
            renderReview();
          }

          minus.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
            setQuantity((row.selection ? row.selection.quantity : 0) - 1);
          });
          plus.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
            setQuantity((row.selection ? row.selection.quantity : 0) + 1);
          });
          input.addEventListener("input", function (event) {
            event.stopPropagation();
            setQuantity(input.value);
          });

          qty.appendChild(minus);
          qty.appendChild(input);
          qty.appendChild(plus);
          wrapper.appendChild(name);
          wrapper.appendChild(qty);
          rows.appendChild(wrapper);
        });
        section.appendChild(rows);
        list.appendChild(section);
      });
      return true;
    }

    function renderSizes() {
      var list = document.getElementById("ch-size-list");
      if (!list) return;

      list.innerHTML = "";

      if (isCreatorDesignFlow()) {
        return;
      }

      if (publicMatrixMode() && renderPublicVariantMatrix(list)) {
        return;
      }

      if (!state.active) {
        var pending = document.createElement("div");
        pending.className = "ch-size-placeholder";
        pending.textContent = "Product sizes will load from the product page.";
        list.appendChild(pending);
        return;
      }

      var rows = resolveSizeRows();

      if (!rows.length) {
        var empty = document.createElement("div");
        empty.className = "ch-size-placeholder";
        empty.textContent = "No available size combination for the selected color.";
        list.appendChild(empty);
        return;
      }

      rows.forEach(function (row) {
        var wrapper = document.createElement("div");
        wrapper.className = "ch-size-row";

        var name = document.createElement("div");
        name.className = "ch-size-name";
        name.textContent = row.label;

        var qty = document.createElement("div");
        qty.className = "ch-qty";

        var minus = document.createElement("button");
        minus.type = "button";
        minus.textContent = "−";
        minus.setAttribute("aria-label", "Decrease " + row.label);

        var input = document.createElement("input");
        input.type = "number";
        input.min = "0";
        input.step = "1";
        input.inputMode = "numeric";
        input.value = String(clampQuantity(state.quantities[row.variant.id] || 0));
        input.setAttribute("aria-label", row.label + " quantity");

        var plus = document.createElement("button");
        plus.type = "button";
        plus.textContent = "+";
        plus.setAttribute("aria-label", "Increase " + row.label);

        function setQuantity(value) {
          state.quantities[row.variant.id] = clampQuantity(value);
          input.value = String(state.quantities[row.variant.id]);
          updateValidation(false);
          renderColors();
          renderReview();
        }

        minus.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
          event.preventDefault();
          event.stopPropagation();
          setQuantity(clampQuantity(state.quantities[row.variant.id] || 0) - 1);
        });

        plus.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
          event.preventDefault();
          event.stopPropagation();
          setQuantity(clampQuantity(state.quantities[row.variant.id] || 0) + 1);
        });

        input.addEventListener("input", function (event) {
          event.stopPropagation();
          setQuantity(input.value);
        });

        qty.appendChild(minus);
        qty.appendChild(input);
        qty.appendChild(plus);

        wrapper.appendChild(name);
        wrapper.appendChild(qty);
        list.appendChild(wrapper);
      });
    }

    function renderDimensions() {
      var target = document.getElementById("ch-design-dimensions");
      if (!target) return;

      var method = getSelectedMethod();

      if (!method) {
        target.textContent = "Select a production method.";
        target.classList.remove("ch-invalid");
        return;
      }

      var maximum = method.maxWidthCm > 0 && method.maxHeightCm > 0
        ? "Maximum: " + method.maxWidthCm + " × " + method.maxHeightCm + " cm"
        : (method.maxWidthCm > 0 ? "Maximum width: " + method.maxWidthCm + " cm"
          : (method.maxHeightCm > 0 ? "Maximum height: " + method.maxHeightCm + " cm" : ""));

      var value = maximum;

      if (
        state.designDimensionsCm &&
        isFinite(Number(state.designDimensionsCm.width)) &&
        isFinite(Number(state.designDimensionsCm.height))
      ) {
        value =
          "Current artwork: " +
          Number(state.designDimensionsCm.width).toFixed(1) +
          " × " +
          Number(state.designDimensionsCm.height).toFixed(1) +
          " cm" + (maximum ? " · " + maximum : "");
      }

      target.textContent = value;
      target.classList.toggle("ch-invalid", !dimensionsValid());
    }

    function bindAcknowledgementControls() {
      var copyrightInput = document.getElementById("ch-copyright-confirm");
      var nonReturnInput = document.getElementById("ch-nonreturn-confirm");

      if (copyrightInput) {
        copyrightInput.checked = !!state.copyrightConfirmed;
        if (!copyrightInput.__chBound) {
          copyrightInput.__chBound = true;
          copyrightInput.addEventListener("change", function () {
            state.copyrightConfirmed = !!copyrightInput.checked;
            updateValidation(false);
          });
        }
      }

      if (nonReturnInput) {
        nonReturnInput.checked = !!state.nonReturnConfirmed;
        if (!nonReturnInput.__chBound) {
          nonReturnInput.__chBound = true;
          nonReturnInput.addEventListener("change", function () {
            state.nonReturnConfirmed = !!nonReturnInput.checked;
            updateValidation(false);
          });
        }
      }
    }

    function renderReview() {
      var method = effectiveSelectedMethod();
      var selections = collectSelections();

      var methodTarget = document.getElementById("ch-review-method");
      var colorLabelTarget = document.getElementById("ch-review-color-label");
      var colorTarget = document.getElementById("ch-review-color");
      var sizesTarget = document.getElementById("ch-review-sizes");
      var quantityTarget = document.getElementById("ch-order-total");
      var totalPriceTarget = document.getElementById("ch-order-total-price");
      var embroideryTarget = document.getElementById("ch-review-embroidery");
      var placementsTarget = document.getElementById("ch-review-placements");

      if (methodTarget) {
        if (!method) {
          methodTarget.textContent = "—";
        } else if (isPublicFlow() && isEmbroidery(method) && method.pricingResolved === false) {
          methodTarget.textContent = method.label + " (" + (
            method.pricingUnavailable
              ? "Pricing unavailable. Please try again later."
              : "Pricing will be calculated from your artwork."
          ) + ")";
        } else if (isPublicFlow() && isEmbroidery(method)) {
          methodTarget.textContent = method.label + " — " + (
            method.embroiderySubtypeLabel ||
            (method.embroiderySubtype === "TEXT_ONLY" ? "Text only" : "Image / logo")
          );
        } else if (!isCreatorDesignFlow() && Number(method.surcharge || 0) > 0) {
          methodTarget.textContent =
            method.label +
            " (+" +
            formatMoney(method.surcharge) +
            " / placement / item)";
        } else {
          methodTarget.textContent = method.label;
        }
      }

      if (embroideryTarget) {
        var subtype = embroiderySubtypeConfig(method, state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype);
        var canonicalSubtype = normalizeEmbroiderySubtype(state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype);
        if (canonicalSubtype) {
          embroideryTarget.textContent = String(
            subtype && subtype.label || (canonicalSubtype === "TEXT_ONLY" ? "Text only" : "Image / logo")
          );
        } else if (isCreatorDesignFlow() && state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype === "TEXT_ONLY") {
          embroideryTarget.textContent = "Text only";
        } else if (isCreatorDesignFlow() && state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype === "IMAGE_OR_LOGO") {
          embroideryTarget.textContent = "Image / logo";
        } else {
          embroideryTarget.textContent = "—";
        }
      }

      var selectedColors = unique(
        selections.map(function (item) {
          return item.color;
        }).filter(Boolean)
      );

      if (colorTarget) {
        colorTarget.textContent = selectedColors.length
          ? selectedColors.join(", ")
          : (state.selectedColor || "—");
      }
      if (colorLabelTarget) {
        colorLabelTarget.textContent = selectedColors.length > 1 ? "Colors" : "Color";
      }

      if (sizesTarget) {
        if (!selections.length) {
          sizesTarget.textContent = "—";
        } else {
          var grouped = {};

          selections.forEach(function (item) {
            var color = item.color || "Selected";
            if (!grouped[color]) grouped[color] = [];

            grouped[color].push(
              (item.size || "Qty") +
              " × " +
              item.quantity
            );
          });

          sizesTarget.textContent = Object.keys(grouped)
            .map(function (color) {
              return color + ": " + grouped[color].join(", ");
            })
            .join(" | ");
        }
      }

      if (quantityTarget) {
        quantityTarget.textContent = String(totalQuantity());
      }

      if (placementsTarget) {
        var artwork = isPublicFlow() ? deriveCurrentArtworkSummary() : null;
        var activePlacements = artwork && artwork.placements
          ? artwork.placements.filter(function (placement) { return placement.hasArtwork; })
          : [];
        placementsTarget.textContent = activePlacements.length
          ? String(activePlacements.length) + " (" + activePlacements.map(function (placement) {
              return placement.side;
            }).join(", ") + ")"
          : "—";
      }

      if (totalPriceTarget) {
        var breakdown = calculatePriceBreakdown();
        totalPriceTarget.textContent = breakdown.totalPrice == null
          ? "Calculated at checkout"
          : formatMoney(breakdown.totalPrice);
      }

      renderDimensions();
    }

    function updateValidation(showErrors) {
      if (!state.active) {
        renderReview();
        if (!state.methodConfigReceived || !state.methods.length) {
          setNativeOkEnabled(false);
          return false;
        }
        return null;
      }

      var creatorDesign = isCreatorDesignFlow();
      var creatorArtwork = creatorDesign
        ? deriveCurrentCreatorArtworkSummary()
        : null;
      var publicArtwork = creatorDesign
        ? null
        : deriveCurrentArtworkSummary();
      var quantityValid = totalQuantity() > 0 && collectSelections().length > 0;
      var method = getSelectedMethod();
      var creatorMethodValid = !creatorDesign || !!(
        method && state.creatorFixedMethod && method.id === state.creatorFixedMethod
      );
      var creatorColorValid = !creatorDesign || !!(
        state.selectedColor && state.creatorNativeColorVerified
      );
      var creatorArtworkValid = !creatorDesign || !!(
        creatorArtwork && creatorArtwork.printableObjectCount > 0
      );
      var publicArtworkValid = creatorDesign || !!(
        publicArtwork && publicArtwork.printableObjectCount > 0
      );
      var publicProjectValid = creatorDesign || !!(
        state.config && (state.config.pitchprintProjectId || state.config.projectId)
      );
      var publicDesignValid = creatorDesign || !!(
        state.config && (state.config.pitchprintDesignId || state.config.designId)
      );
      var publicMethodValid = creatorDesign || !!method;
      var publicConfigValid = creatorDesign || publicOrderConfigReady();
      var dimensionValid = creatorDesign ? true : dimensionsValid();
      var exampleTextValid = !state.hasUnusedExampleText;
      var copyrightValid = !!state.copyrightConfirmed;
      var nonReturnValid = creatorDesign ? true : !!state.nonReturnConfirmed;
      var embroiderySubtypeValid = !isEmbroidery(method) || (
        !!normalizeEmbroiderySubtype(state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype) &&
        embroiderySubtypePriceMinor(embroiderySubtypeConfig(method, state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype)) != null
      );
      var creatorSetupValid = creatorMethodValid && creatorColorValid &&
        creatorArtworkValid && embroiderySubtypeValid;
      var publicOrderValid = publicConfigValid && quantityValid && publicArtworkValid &&
        publicProjectValid && publicDesignValid && publicMethodValid;
      var valid =
        (creatorDesign ? creatorSetupValid : publicOrderValid) &&
        embroiderySubtypeValid &&
        dimensionValid &&
        exampleTextValid &&
        copyrightValid &&
        nonReturnValid;

      var quantityError = document.getElementById("ch-order-error");
      var dimensionError = document.getElementById("ch-dimension-error");
      var exampleTextError = document.getElementById("ch-example-text-error");
      var legalError = document.getElementById("ch-legal-error");
      var embroideryError = document.getElementById("ch-embroidery-type-error");

      if (quantityError) {
        if (!state.methodConfigReceived) {
          quantityError.textContent = "Production methods are still loading.";
        } else if (!state.methods.length) {
          quantityError.textContent = "No production methods are currently available for this product.";
        } else if (!creatorDesign && state.publicCartHandoff.error) {
          quantityError.textContent = state.publicCartHandoff.error;
        } else if (creatorDesign && !creatorMethodValid) {
          quantityError.textContent = "The Creator printing method is unavailable. Reopen this design from the Creator Dashboard.";
        } else if (creatorDesign && !creatorColorValid) {
          quantityError.textContent = "The active garment color could not be confirmed. Please select the color again.";
        } else if (creatorDesign && !creatorArtworkValid) {
          quantityError.textContent = "Add printable artwork before submitting this Creator design.";
        } else if (creatorDesign && !embroiderySubtypeValid) {
          quantityError.textContent = "PitchPrint could not classify the embroidery artwork. Edit the artwork and try again.";
        } else {
          quantityError.textContent = creatorDesign
            ? "Complete the Creator design review before submitting."
            : (!publicConfigValid
                ? "Product options are still loading."
                : (!publicArtworkValid
                ? "Add printable text or artwork before continuing."
                : (!publicProjectValid || !publicDesignValid
                    ? "The customization project is incomplete. Reopen the product customizer and try again."
                    : (!publicMethodValid
                        ? "Select a production method before continuing."
                        : "Select at least one size and quantity."))));
        }
        quantityError.classList.toggle(
          "ch-show",
          !state.methodConfigReceived || !state.methods.length || (!creatorDesign && !!state.publicCartHandoff.error) ||
          (creatorDesign ? (showErrors || copyrightValid) && !creatorSetupValid : !!showErrors && !publicOrderValid)
        );
      }

      if (embroideryError) {
        var detectedSubtype = normalizeEmbroiderySubtype(state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype);
        embroideryError.textContent = detectedSubtype === "TEXT_ONLY"
          ? "Embroidery text pricing is currently unavailable."
          : (detectedSubtype === "IMAGE_OR_LOGO"
              ? "Embroidery image/logo pricing is currently unavailable."
              : "Add printable text or artwork so PitchPrint can determine the embroidery category.");
        embroideryError.classList.toggle(
          "ch-show",
          !!showErrors && !creatorDesign && !embroiderySubtypeValid
        );
      }

      if (dimensionError) {
        dimensionError.classList.toggle("ch-show", !!showErrors && !dimensionValid);
      }

      if (exampleTextError) {
        exampleTextError.classList.toggle(
          "ch-show",
          (showErrors || (creatorDesign && copyrightValid)) && !exampleTextValid
        );
      }

      if (legalError) {
        legalError.textContent = creatorDesign
          ? "Confirm that you own or have the necessary rights to use this artwork/design."
          : "Confirm the copyright/usage rights and custom-made product terms before continuing.";
        legalError.classList.toggle(
          "ch-show",
          !!showErrors && (!copyrightValid || !nonReturnValid)
        );
      }

      setNativeOkEnabled(valid);
      renderReview();
      return valid;
    }

    function buildPayload() {
      var publicArtwork = isPublicFlow()
        ? deriveCurrentArtworkSummary()
        : null;
      var method = effectiveSelectedMethod();
      var selections = collectSelections();
      var pricing = calculatePriceBreakdown();
      var selectedColors = isCreatorDesignFlow()
        ? (state.selectedColor ? [String(state.selectedColor)] : [])
        : unique(
            selections
              .map(function (item) { return item.color; })
              .filter(Boolean)
          );

      var cartItems = selections.map(function (item) {
        var properties = {
          _production_method: method ? method.label : null
        };

        if (state.config && state.config.pitchprintProjectId) {
          properties._pitchprint = state.config.pitchprintProjectId;
        }

        if (state.config && state.config.pitchprintDesignId) {
          properties._design_id = state.config.pitchprintDesignId;
        }

        return {
          id: item.variantId,
          quantity: item.quantity,
          properties: properties
        };
      });

      var payload = {
        flowMode: state.flowMode,
        productOrigin: isCreatorDesignFlow()
          ? ((state.config && (state.config.baseProductOrigin || state.config.productOrigin)) || "global")
          : "public",
        baseProductOrigin: isCreatorDesignFlow()
          ? ((state.config && (state.config.baseProductOrigin || state.config.productOrigin)) || "global")
          : null,
        designMode: isCreatorDesignFlow() ? "creator_design" : "customize",
        creatorContext: isCreatorDesignFlow(),
        launchContext: isCreatorDesignFlow()
          ? ((state.config && (state.config.launchContext || state.config.launchSource)) || "creator_dashboard")
          : null,
        isCreatorProduct: isCreatorDesignFlow(),
        creatorProductId: state.config && (
          state.config.creatorProductId ||
          state.config.creator_product_id
        ) || null,
        creatorPublicHandle: state.config && (
          state.config.creatorPublicHandle ||
          state.config.publicHandle ||
          state.config.creator_public_handle
        ) || null,
        productId: state.config && state.config.productId
          ? state.config.productId
          : null,
        productHandle: state.config && state.config.productHandle
          ? state.config.productHandle
          : null,
        baseProductId: state.config && (
          state.config.baseProductId || state.config.productId
        ) || null,
        projectId: state.config && (
          state.config.pitchprintProjectId || state.config.projectId
        ) || null,
        designId: state.config && (
          state.config.pitchprintDesignId || state.config.designId
        ) || null,
        selectedProductionMethod: method ? method.id : null,
        productionMethod: method,
        fixedProductionMethod: isCreatorDesignFlow() && method ? method.id : null,
        artworkType: publicArtwork ? publicArtwork.artworkType : null,
        embroiderySubtype: isEmbroidery(method) ? state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype : null,
        placementCount: publicArtwork ? publicArtwork.placementCount : null,
        placements: publicArtwork ? publicArtwork.placements : [],
        artworkSummary: publicArtwork ? {
          hasText: publicArtwork.hasText,
          hasImage: publicArtwork.hasImage,
          printableObjectCount: publicArtwork.printableObjectCount,
          objectTypes: publicArtwork.objectTypes
        } : null,
        productionMethodSurcharge: method ? method.surcharge : null,
        productionMethodSurchargeMinor: method ? method.surchargeMinor : null,
        productionFeeVariantId: method ? method.feeVariantId || null : null,
        productionFeeVariantGid: method ? method.feeVariantGid || null : null,
        pricingMode: isPublicFlow()
          ? "per_item_per_placement_additive"
          : "per_item_additive",
        adminPricingSource: state.config && state.config.adminPricingSource || null,
        selectedColor: state.selectedColor,
        activePreviewColor: isPublicFlow() ? state.activePreviewColor : null,
        selectedColors: selectedColors,
        designDimensionsCm: state.designDimensionsCm,
        exampleTextClean: !state.hasUnusedExampleText,
        copyrightConfirmed: !!state.copyrightConfirmed,
        nonReturnConfirmed: isCreatorDesignFlow() ? false : !!state.nonReturnConfirmed,
        acknowledgements: {
          copyrightConfirmed: !!state.copyrightConfirmed,
          nonReturnConfirmed: isCreatorDesignFlow() ? false : !!state.nonReturnConfirmed
        },
        selections: selections,
        variantSelections: isPublicFlow() ? publicVariantSelections(true) : selections,
        totalQuantity: totalQuantity(),
        productionFeeQuantityBasis: publicArtwork
          ? totalQuantity() * publicArtwork.placementCount
          : null,
        baseTotalMinor: pricing.baseTotalMinor,
        productionTotalMinor: pricing.productionTotalMinor,
        totalPriceMinor: pricing.totalPriceMinor,
        totalPrice: pricing.totalPrice,
        previews: state.config && Array.isArray(state.config.previews)
          ? state.config.previews.slice()
          : [],
        sidePreviews: state.config && state.config.sidePreviews &&
          typeof state.config.sidePreviews === "object"
            ? state.config.sidePreviews
            : {},
        timestamp: Date.now()
      };
      if (isPublicFlow()) {
        payload.displayMerchandiseTotalMinor = pricing.baseTotalMinor;
        payload.displayProductionTotalMinor = pricing.productionTotalMinor;
        payload.displayTotalMinor = pricing.totalPriceMinor;
        payload.currency = state.config && state.config.currency || "SEK";
      }
      if (isCreatorDesignFlow()) payload.cartItems = cartItems;
      return payload;
    }

    var publicCartAckTimer = 0;
    var publicCartHandoffSequence = 0;

    function publicCartError(message, stage) {
      var handoff = state.publicCartHandoff;
      clearTimeout(publicCartAckTimer);
      publicCartAckTimer = 0;
      handoff.inFlight = false;
      handoff.failureStage = stage || "PUBLIC_CART_HANDOFF_FAILED";
      handoff.error = String(message || "The cart handoff failed. Please try again.");
      updateValidation(false);
      var errorTarget = document.getElementById("ch-order-error");
      if (errorTarget) {
        errorTarget.textContent = handoff.error;
        errorTarget.classList.add("ch-show");
      }
      if (window.console && typeof console.info === "function") {
        console.info("PUBLIC_CART_READY_FAILURE_STAGE", handoff.failureStage);
      }
      return false;
    }

    function publicContinueValidationDiagnostics(validationResult) {
      var method = effectiveSelectedMethod();
      var artwork = deriveCurrentArtworkSummary();
      var variants = publicVariantSelections(true);
      var continueButton = document.getElementById("ch-continue-cart");
      var diagnostics = {
        BUTTON_ID: continueButton && continueButton.id || null,
        BUTTON_HANDLER: "window.chPitchPrintContinueToCart",
        BUTTON_DISABLED_STATE: !!(continueButton && continueButton.disabled),
        BUTTON_VALIDATION_RESULT: validationResult,
        HAS_PRODUCTION_METHOD: !!method,
        HAS_ARTWORK: !!(artwork && artwork.printableObjectCount > 0),
        ARTWORK_TYPE: artwork && artwork.artworkType || "NO_ARTWORK",
        HAS_EMBROIDERY_PRICE: !isEmbroidery(method) || method.pricingResolved !== false,
        VARIANT_SELECTION_COUNT: variants.length,
        TOTAL_QUANTITY: totalQuantity(),
        PLACEMENT_COUNT: artwork ? Number(artwork.placementCount || 0) : 0,
        HAS_PROJECT_ID: !!(state.config && (state.config.pitchprintProjectId || state.config.projectId)),
        HAS_DESIGN_ID: !!(state.config && (state.config.pitchprintDesignId || state.config.designId)),
        COPYRIGHT_CONFIRMED: !!state.copyrightConfirmed,
        NON_RETURN_CONFIRMED: !!state.nonReturnConfirmed,
        FINAL_PUBLIC_CART_VALID: validationResult === true
      };
      state.publicCartHandoff.validation = diagnostics;
      if (window.console && typeof console.info === "function") {
        Object.keys(diagnostics).forEach(function (key) {
          console.info(key, diagnostics[key]);
        });
      }
      return diagnostics;
    }

    function handlePublicCartReadyAck(detail) {
      if (!isPublicFlow()) return false;
      detail = detail || {};
      var handoff = state.publicCartHandoff;
      if (!handoff.inFlight || !handoff.handoffId) return false;
      if (String(detail.handoffId || "") !== String(handoff.handoffId)) return false;
      clearTimeout(publicCartAckTimer);
      publicCartAckTimer = 0;
      if (detail.ok !== true) {
        return publicCartError(
          detail.error || "CustomHouse could not add this customization to the cart. Please try again.",
          detail.stage || "HOST_ACK_FAILED"
        );
      }
      handoff.ackReceived = true;
      handoff.failureStage = null;
      handoff.error = null;
      if (window.console && typeof console.info === "function") {
        console.info("PUBLIC_CART_READY_ACK_RECEIVED", true);
      }
      return true;
    }

    function publishCartReady() {
      var handoff = state.publicCartHandoff;
      if (isPublicFlow() && handoff.inFlight) return null;
      var payload = buildPayload();
      if (isPublicFlow()) {
        var invalidSelection = (payload.variantSelections || []).find(function (selection) {
          return !(selection && (selection.variantId || selection.variantGid));
        });
        if (invalidSelection || !(payload.variantSelections || []).length) {
          publicCartError(
            "A selected product option is missing its Shopify variant ID. Reopen the customizer and try again.",
            "VARIANT_IDENTITY_MISSING"
          );
          return null;
        }
        publicCartHandoffSequence += 1;
        payload.handoffId = "public-cart-" + Date.now() + "-" + publicCartHandoffSequence;
        handoff.handoffId = payload.handoffId;
        handoff.built = true;
        handoff.ackReceived = false;
        handoff.failureStage = null;
        handoff.error = null;
        handoff.inFlight = true;
        if (window.console && typeof console.info === "function") {
          console.info("PUBLIC_CART_READY_BUILT", true);
        }
      }
      window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderSelection = payload;

      if (isPublicFlow() && window.console && typeof window.console.info === "function") {
        console.info("PUBLIC_SELECTED_VARIANTS", (payload.variantSelections || []).map(function (selection) {
          return {
            variantId: selection.variantId,
            color: selection.color,
            size: selection.size,
            quantity: selection.quantity
          };
        }));
        console.info("PUBLIC_TOTAL_QUANTITY", payload.totalQuantity);
      }

      try {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(
          "customhouse:pitchprint-cart-ready",
          { detail: payload }
        ));
      } catch (error) {}

      var targetCount = postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_CART_READY", payload);
      if (isPublicFlow()) {
        handoff.emittedCount += 1;
        handoff.target = targetCount > 0 ? "host-window" : "same-window-event";
        handoff.targetCount = targetCount;
        if (window.console && typeof console.info === "function") {
          console.info("PUBLIC_CART_READY_EMITTED", true);
          console.info("PUBLIC_CART_READY_EVENT_NAME", handoff.eventName);
          console.info("PUBLIC_CART_READY_TARGET", handoff.target);
        }
        setNativeOkEnabled(false);
        publicCartAckTimer = setTimeout(function () {
          publicCartError(
            "CustomHouse did not confirm the cart request. Please try again.",
            "HOST_ACK_TIMEOUT"
          );
        }, 15000);
      }

      return payload;
    }

    window.addEventListener("message", function (event) {
      var data = event && event.data;
      if (!data || data.type !== "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_CART_READY_ACK") return;
      handlePublicCartReadyAck(data.payload || null);
    });

    window.addEventListener("customhouse:pitchprint-cart-ready-ack", function (event) {
      handlePublicCartReadyAck(event && event.detail ? event.detail : null);
    });

    function creatorSaveError(code, message, diagnostics) {
      var detail = {
        flowMode: FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_DESIGN,
        code: code,
        message: message,
        recoverable: true,
        diagnostics: diagnostics || null
      };

      try {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(
          "customhouse:pitchprint-creator-save-error",
          { detail: detail }
        ));
      } catch (error) {}

      postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_CREATOR_SAVE_ERROR", detail);
      return null;
    }

    function savedEventData(event) {
      return event && event.data !== undefined ? event.data : event;
    }

    function savedSource(value) {
      if (!value) return null;
      if (typeof value === "object") return value;
      if (typeof value !== "string") return null;
      try {
        return JSON.parse(value);
      } catch (error) {
        return null;
      }
    }

    function savedPageObjects(page) {
      if (!page || typeof page !== "object") return [];
      if (Array.isArray(page.items)) return page.items;
      if (Array.isArray(page._items)) return page._items;
      if (Array.isArray(page.children)) return page.children;
      if (page.canvas && typeof page.canvas.getObjects === "function") {
        return page.canvas.getObjects();
      }
      if (page.canvas && Array.isArray(page.canvas._objects)) return page.canvas._objects;
      if (page.canvas && Array.isArray(page.canvas.objects)) return page.canvas.objects;
      if (typeof page.getObjects === "function") return page.getObjects();
      if (Array.isArray(page._objects)) return page._objects;
      return Array.isArray(page.objects) ? page.objects : [];
    }

    function isReferenceObject(object) {
      return !!(
        object &&
        (object.isReference === true || object.isReference === 1 || object.isReference === "1")
      );
    }

    function objectRawType(object) {
      return String(object && (object.type || object.objectType || object.kind) || "unknown").trim() || "unknown";
    }

    function objectDescriptor(object) {
      return normalizeName([
        objectRawType(object),
        object && object.name,
        object && object.id,
        object && object.role,
        object && object.sourceType,
        object && object.layerType,
        object && object.category,
        object && object.purpose
      ].filter(Boolean).join(" "));
    }

    function isPitchPrintInfrastructureObject(object) {
      if (!object) return true;
      if (
        isReferenceObject(object) ||
        object.isTemplate === true ||
        object.templateObject === true ||
        object.isBackground === true ||
        object.backgroundObject === true ||
        object.isHelper === true ||
        object.helper === true ||
        object.isGuide === true ||
        object.guide === true ||
        object.isPlaceholder === true ||
        object.placeholder === true ||
        object.isMask === true ||
        object.mask === true
      ) return true;

      return /(^| )(garment|base garment|base product|product mockup|mockup|template|background|print area|printarea|helper|guide|mask|clip|placeholder|reference|boundary|safe area)( |$)/.test(
        objectDescriptor(object)
      );
    }

    function excludedFromUserPrint(object) {
      return !object ||
        isPitchPrintInfrastructureObject(object) ||
        object.visible === false ||
        Number(object.opacity) === 0 ||
        object.excludeFromExport === true ||
        object.excludeFromPrint === true ||
        object.nonPrintable === true;
    }

    function artworkSourceIdentity(object) {
      if (!object) return null;
      var stableId = object.uploadId || object.assetId || object.fileId || object.photoId || object.imageId;
      if (stableId != null && stableId !== "") return "asset-id";
      var source = object.src || object._src || object.imageUrl || object.url;
      var element = object._element || object.element || object._originalElement;
      if ((!source || typeof source !== "string") && element && typeof element === "object") {
        source = element.currentSrc || element.src || element.href || null;
      }
      if (typeof source !== "string" || !source) return null;
      if (/^data:/i.test(source)) return "data-image";
      if (/^blob:/i.test(source)) return "blob-image";
      if (/^https?:/i.test(source)) {
        try { return "remote:" + new URL(source).hostname; } catch (error) { return "remote-image"; }
      }
      return "local-image";
    }

    function hasPositiveUserArtworkIdentity(object) {
      if (!object) return false;
      if (
        object.isUserArtwork === true ||
        object.userArtwork === true ||
        object.isUploaded === true ||
        object.uploaded === true ||
        object.printableArtwork === true ||
        object.isPrintableArtwork === true
      ) return true;
      return /(^| )(user artwork|artwork|uploaded|upload|logo|photo|design)( |$)/.test(
        objectDescriptor(object)
      );
    }

    function normalizedArtworkObjectType(object) {
      if (excludedFromUserPrint(object)) return null;
      var type = normalizeName(objectRawType(object)).replace(/\s+/g, "-");
      var textTypes = [
        "text", "i-text", "itext", "textbox", "curvedtext", "curved-text",
        "pp-text", "pitchprint-text"
      ];
      if (textTypes.indexOf(type) !== -1) return "text";

      var imageTypes = [
        "image", "photo", "logo", "svg", "svg-image", "vector", "vector-image",
        "raster", "raster-image", "uploaded-image", "pp-image", "pitchprint-image",
        "path", "path-group", "group"
      ];
      if (imageTypes.indexOf(type) === -1) return null;

      var hasSource = !!artworkSourceIdentity(object);
      var interactiveImage = !(object.selectable === false && object.evented === false);
      return hasPositiveUserArtworkIdentity(object) || (hasSource && interactiveImage)
        ? "image"
        : null;
    }

    var ARTWORK_CHILD_KEYS = ["objects", "_objects", "items", "_items", "children", "groups"];

    function artworkObjectChildren(object) {
      var collections = [];
      if (!object || typeof object !== "object") return collections;
      ARTWORK_CHILD_KEYS.forEach(function (key) {
        if (Array.isArray(object[key]) && object[key].length) {
          collections.push({ key: key, items: object[key] });
        }
      });
      return collections;
    }

    function pageArtworkRoots(page) {
      var roots = [];
      var seenCollections = [];
      function add(items, path) {
        if (!Array.isArray(items) || !items.length || seenCollections.indexOf(items) !== -1) return;
        seenCollections.push(items);
        roots.push({ items: items, path: path });
      }
      if (!page || typeof page !== "object") return roots;
      ARTWORK_CHILD_KEYS.forEach(function (key) { add(page[key], "page." + key); });
      if (page.canvas && typeof page.canvas === "object") {
        if (typeof page.canvas.getObjects === "function") {
          try { add(page.canvas.getObjects(), "page.canvas.getObjects"); } catch (error) {}
        }
        ARTWORK_CHILD_KEYS.forEach(function (key) {
          add(page.canvas[key], "page.canvas." + key);
        });
      }
      if (typeof page.getObjects === "function") {
        try { add(page.getObjects(), "page.getObjects"); } catch (error) {}
      }
      if (!roots.length) add(savedPageObjects(page), "page.objects");
      return roots;
    }

    function artworkRejectionReason(object, normalizedType, hasChildren) {
      if (!object) return "invalid-object";
      if (isPitchPrintInfrastructureObject(object)) return "pitchprint-infrastructure";
      if (object.visible === false || Number(object.opacity) === 0) return "not-visible";
      if (
        object.excludeFromExport === true ||
        object.excludeFromPrint === true ||
        object.nonPrintable === true
      ) return "excluded-from-print";
      if (hasChildren && /^(group|path-group)$/.test(normalizeName(objectRawType(object)))) {
        return "container";
      }
      if (!normalizedType) return "unknown-or-non-artwork-type";
      return null;
    }

    function collectPageArtworkEntries(page) {
      var entries = [];
      var seenObjects = [];
      var maxDepth = 30;
      var maxObjects = 250;
      function visit(object, path, depth) {
        if (
          !object || typeof object !== "object" ||
          depth > maxDepth || entries.length >= maxObjects ||
          seenObjects.indexOf(object) !== -1
        ) return;
        seenObjects.push(object);
        var childCollections = artworkObjectChildren(object);
        var type = normalizeName(objectRawType(object)).replace(/\s+/g, "-");
        var container = childCollections.length > 0 && (type === "group" || type === "path-group");
        var normalizedType = container ? null : normalizedArtworkObjectType(object);
        var reason = artworkRejectionReason(object, normalizedType, childCollections.length > 0);
        entries.push({
          object: object,
          path: path,
          normalizedType: normalizedType,
          included: !!normalizedType && !reason,
          rejectionReason: reason
        });
        childCollections.forEach(function (collection) {
          collection.items.forEach(function (child, childIndex) {
            visit(child, path + "." + collection.key + "[" + childIndex + "]", depth + 1);
          });
        });
      }
      pageArtworkRoots(page).forEach(function (root) {
        root.items.forEach(function (object, index) {
          visit(object, root.path + "[" + index + "]", 0);
        });
      });
      return entries;
    }

    function getPrintableUserArtworkObjects(page) {
      return collectPageArtworkEntries(page).filter(function (entry) {
        return entry.included;
      }).map(function (entry) {
        return entry.object;
      });
    }

    function sanitizedArtworkDiagnostic(page, index, entry) {
      var object = entry.object;
      function safeLabel(value) {
        if (value == null || value === "") return null;
        return String(value).replace(/[\r\n\t]+/g, " ").slice(0, 80);
      }
      return {
        page: normalizedSide(page, index),
        path: entry.path,
        type: objectRawType(object),
        name: safeLabel(object && object.name),
        id: safeLabel(object && (object.id || object._id)),
        isReference: isReferenceObject(object),
        excludeFromPrint: object && object.excludeFromPrint === true,
        excludeFromExport: object && object.excludeFromExport === true,
        nonPrintable: object && object.nonPrintable === true,
        selectable: object && object.selectable,
        evented: object && object.evented,
        visible: !(object && object.visible === false),
        opacity: object && object.opacity == null ? 1 : Number(object.opacity),
        sourceIdentity: artworkSourceIdentity(object),
        userPrintArtwork: !!entry.included,
        normalizedArtworkType: entry.included ? entry.normalizedType : null,
        rejectionReason: entry.rejectionReason
      };
    }

    function normalizedSide(page, index) {
      var title = String(
        page && page.pageData && page.pageData.title ||
        page && page.title ||
        ""
      ).trim();
      var name = normalizeName(title);
      if (/(^| )(front|framsida)( |$)/.test(name)) return "front";
      if (/(^| )(back|baksida|rear)( |$)/.test(name)) return "back";
      if (/(^| )(side|sida|sleeve)( |$)/.test(name)) return "side";
      return "side-" + String(index + 1);
    }

    function summarizeCreatorArtworkPages(pages) {
      var allObjects = [];
      var rawDiagnostics = [];
      var filteredDiagnostics = [];
      var placements = (Array.isArray(pages) ? pages : []).map(function (page, index) {
        var entries = collectPageArtworkEntries(page);
        var objects = entries.filter(function (entry) { return entry.included; }).map(function (entry) {
          return entry.object;
        });
        entries.forEach(function (entry) {
          var diagnostic = sanitizedArtworkDiagnostic(page, index, entry);
          rawDiagnostics.push(diagnostic);
          if (entry.included) filteredDiagnostics.push(diagnostic);
        });
        allObjects = allObjects.concat(objects);
        return {
          side: normalizedSide(page, index),
          sideId: page && (page.id || page._id) || null,
          title: page && page.pageData && page.pageData.title || page && page.title || normalizedSide(page, index),
          index: index,
          hasArtwork: objects.length > 0
        };
      });
      var objectTypes = unique(allObjects.map(normalizedArtworkObjectType));
      var hasText = objectTypes.indexOf("text") !== -1;
      var hasImage = objectTypes.indexOf("image") !== -1;
      var printableObjectCount = allObjects.length;
      return {
        hasText: hasText,
        hasImage: hasImage,
        printableObjectCount: printableObjectCount,
        objectTypes: objectTypes,
        artworkType: printableObjectCount
          ? (hasImage ? "IMAGE_OR_LOGO" : "TEXT_ONLY")
          : null,
        placementCount: placements.filter(function (placement) {
          return placement.hasArtwork;
        }).length,
        placements: placements,
        diagnostics: {
          rawTypes: unique(rawDiagnostics.map(function (item) { return item.type; })),
          filteredTypes: unique(filteredDiagnostics.map(function (item) { return item.type; })),
          rejectedReasons: unique(rawDiagnostics.filter(function (item) {
            return !item.userPrintArtwork && item.rejectionReason;
          }).map(function (item) { return item.rejectionReason; })),
          rawObjects: rawDiagnostics,
          filteredObjects: filteredDiagnostics
        }
      };
    }

    function currentCreatorRuntimePages() {
      var dModel = window.ppframe && window.ppframe.dModel;
      var runtime = dModel && dModel.runtime;
      var design = runtime && runtime.design;
      var candidates = [
        design && design.pages,
        runtime && runtime.pages,
        dModel && dModel.design && dModel.design.pages
      ];
      for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length; i += 1) {
        if (Array.isArray(candidates[i]) && candidates[i].length) return candidates[i];
      }
      return design && design.cPage ? [design.cPage] : [];
    }

    function logPublicArtworkDiagnostics(summary) {
      if (!isPublicFlow() || !summary || !window.console || typeof window.console.info !== "function") return;
      var diagnostics = summary.diagnostics || {};
      var rawObjects = (diagnostics.rawObjects || []).slice(0, 100);
      var signature = JSON.stringify({
        artworkType: summary.artworkType || "NO_ARTWORK",
        placementCount: Number(summary.placementCount || 0),
        raw: rawObjects.map(function (item) { return [item.type, item.path, item.rejectionReason]; }),
        filtered: diagnostics.filteredTypes || []
      });
      if (state.publicArtworkDiagnosticSignature === signature) return;
      state.publicArtworkDiagnosticSignature = signature;
      console.info("PUBLIC_CURRENT_PAGE", JSON.stringify(
        (summary.placements || []).map(function (placement) {
          return { side: placement.side, index: placement.index, hasArtwork: placement.hasArtwork };
        })
      ));
      console.info("PUBLIC_RAW_OBJECT_TYPES", JSON.stringify(rawObjects.map(function (item) { return item.type; })));
      console.info("PUBLIC_RAW_OBJECT_PATHS", JSON.stringify(rawObjects.map(function (item) { return item.path; })));
      console.info("PUBLIC_FILTERED_OBJECT_TYPES", JSON.stringify(diagnostics.filteredTypes || []));
      console.info("PUBLIC_RAW_ARTWORK_TYPES", JSON.stringify(diagnostics.rawTypes || []));
      console.info("PUBLIC_FILTERED_ARTWORK_TYPES", JSON.stringify(diagnostics.filteredTypes || []));
      console.info("PUBLIC_ARTWORK_TYPE", summary.artworkType || "NO_ARTWORK");
      console.info("PUBLIC_PLACEMENT_COUNT", Number(summary.placementCount || 0));
      console.info("PUBLIC_REJECTED_OBJECTS_WITH_REASON", JSON.stringify(
        rawObjects.filter(function (item) { return !item.userPrintArtwork; }).map(function (item) {
          return { type: item.type, path: item.path, reason: item.rejectionReason };
        })
      ));
    }

    // PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE and CREATOR_DESIGN intentionally share the same
    // canonical printable-object filter. Flow-specific code may consume the
    // result differently, but garment/template infrastructure is classified
    // exactly once here.
    function deriveCurrentArtworkSummary() {
      var summary = summarizeCreatorArtworkPages(currentCreatorRuntimePages());
      if (isCreatorDesignFlow()) {
        state.creatorArtworkSummary = summary;
        state.creatorArtworkDiagnostics = summary.diagnostics;
      } else {
        if (!summary.artworkType) summary.artworkType = "NO_ARTWORK";
        state.publicArtworkSummary = summary;
        state.publicArtworkDiagnostics = summary.diagnostics;
        logPublicArtworkDiagnostics(summary);
      }
      var method = getSelectedMethod();
      if (isEmbroidery(method)) {
        state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype = normalizeEmbroiderySubtype(summary.artworkType);
      } else if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) {
        state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype = null;
      }
      return summary;
    }

    function deriveCurrentCreatorArtworkSummary() {
      return deriveCurrentArtworkSummary();
    }

    function currentCreatorSelectedColorDetail() {
      if (!state.creatorNativeColorVerified || !state.selectedColor) return null;
      var detail = state.creatorNativeColorDetail || nativeColorDetail(
        state.selectedColor,
        state.creatorNativeColorSource,
        null,
        null,
        null
      );
      return {
        name: detail.name,
        value: detail.value,
        key: detail.key,
        nativeColorId: detail.nativeColorId,
        nativeColorIndex: detail.nativeColorIndex,
        source: detail.source
      };
    }

    function buildCurrentCreatorDesignSummary() {
      if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) return null;
      syncCreatorGarmentColor({ requireVerified: true });
      var artwork = deriveCurrentCreatorArtworkSummary();
      var colorDetail = currentCreatorSelectedColorDetail();
      return {
        selectedColor: colorDetail ? colorDetail.name : null,
        selectedColorDetail: colorDetail,
        artworkType: artwork.artworkType,
        embroiderySubtype: artwork.artworkType,
        artworkSummary: {
          hasText: artwork.hasText,
          hasImage: artwork.hasImage,
          printableObjectCount: artwork.printableObjectCount,
          objectTypes: artwork.objectTypes
        },
        placementCount: artwork.placementCount,
        placements: artwork.placements
      };
    }

    function prepareCreatorFinalSave() {
      if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) return null;
      var summary = buildCurrentCreatorDesignSummary();
      state.creatorPreSaveSummary = summary;
      renderEmbroideryTypes();
      renderReview();
      if (window.console && typeof window.console.info === "function") {
        console.info("CREATOR_SELECTED_METHOD_FINAL", state.selectedMethod || null);
        console.info("CREATOR_PRE_SAVE_ARTWORK_TYPE", summary.artworkType || null);
        console.info("CREATOR_PRE_SAVE_COLOR", summary.selectedColor || null);
        console.info("TEXT_ONLY_RAW_TYPES", state.creatorArtworkDiagnostics && state.creatorArtworkDiagnostics.rawTypes || []);
        console.info("TEXT_ONLY_FILTERED_TYPES", state.creatorArtworkDiagnostics && state.creatorArtworkDiagnostics.filteredTypes || []);
        console.info("CREATOR_ARTWORK_OBJECTS", state.creatorArtworkDiagnostics && state.creatorArtworkDiagnostics.rawObjects || []);
      }
      return summary;
    }

    function activeSavedSide(pages) {
      var current = window.ppframe &&
        window.ppframe.dModel &&
        window.ppframe.dModel.runtime &&
        window.ppframe.dModel.runtime.design &&
        window.ppframe.dModel.runtime.design.cPage;
      var index = -1;
      if (current) {
        index = pages.findIndex(function (page) {
          return page === current || page && current && page.id && page.id === current.id;
        });
      }
      if (index < 0) index = 0;
      return pages.length ? normalizedSide(current || pages[index], index) : null;
    }

    function canonicalPreviewUrls(saved) {
      var values = Array.isArray(saved && saved.previews)
        ? saved.previews
        : [];
      return values.filter(function (value) {
        return typeof value === "string" && /^https?:\/\//i.test(value.trim());
      }).map(function (value) { return value.trim(); });
    }

    function normalizeCreatorSavedResult(event) {
      var saved = savedEventData(event) || {};
      var source = savedSource(saved.source);
      var projectId = String(
        saved.projectId ||
        saved.project_id ||
        source && (source.projectId || source.project_id) ||
        ""
      ).trim();
      if (!projectId) {
        return { error: ["CREATOR_SAVE_PROJECT_UNAVAILABLE", "PitchPrint did not return the saved Creator project ID."] };
      }

      syncCreatorGarmentColor({ requireVerified: true });
      var postSaveColorDetail = currentCreatorSelectedColorDetail();
      var selectedColor = String(postSaveColorDetail && postSaveColorDetail.name || "").trim();
      if (!selectedColor) {
        return { error: [
          "CREATOR_SAVE_COLOR_UNAVAILABLE",
          "The active garment color could not be confirmed. Please select the color again."
        ] };
      }

      var pages = source && Array.isArray(source.pages) ? source.pages : null;
      if (!pages) {
        return { error: ["CREATOR_SAVE_ARTWORK_UNAVAILABLE", "PitchPrint did not return saved artwork data."] };
      }

      var canonicalPreviews = canonicalPreviewUrls(saved);
      if (!canonicalPreviews.length) {
        return { error: ["CREATOR_SAVE_PREVIEW_UNAVAILABLE", "PitchPrint has not produced the saved project preview yet."] };
      }

      var sidePreviews = {};
      var savedArtwork = summarizeCreatorArtworkPages(pages);
      var placements = savedArtwork.placements.map(function (placement, index) {
        var side = placement.side;
        var preview = canonicalPreviews[index] || null;
        if (sidePreviews[side] == null) sidePreviews[side] = preview;
        return {
          side: placement.side,
          sideId: placement.sideId,
          title: placement.title,
          index: placement.index,
          hasArtwork: placement.hasArtwork,
          preview: preview
        };
      });
      var artworkType = savedArtwork.artworkType;
      if (!artworkType) {
        return { error: ["CREATOR_SAVE_ARTWORK_EMPTY", "Add printable artwork before submitting this Creator design."] };
      }
      var preSave = state.creatorPreSaveSummary;
      var artworkMismatch = !!(
        preSave && preSave.artworkType && preSave.artworkType !== artworkType
      );
      var colorMismatch = !!(
        preSave && preSave.selectedColor &&
        normalizeName(preSave.selectedColor) !== normalizeName(selectedColor)
      );
      if (artworkMismatch || colorMismatch) {
        return { error: [
          "CREATOR_SAVE_CONTRACT_MISMATCH",
          "The saved design no longer matches the final review. Please review the artwork and color, then save again.",
          {
            preSaveArtworkType: preSave && preSave.artworkType || null,
            postSaveArtworkType: artworkType,
            preSaveColor: preSave && preSave.selectedColor || null,
            postSaveColor: selectedColor,
            rawTypes: savedArtwork.diagnostics.rawTypes,
            filteredTypes: savedArtwork.diagnostics.filteredTypes
          }
        ] };
      }
      var method = effectiveSelectedMethod();
      if (!method || (isEmbroidery(method) && (method.pricingResolved === false || !embroiderySubtypeConfig(method, artworkType)))) {
        return { error: ["CREATOR_SAVE_METHOD_UNAVAILABLE", "The production method or artwork tier is no longer available for this product. Reopen the design from the Creator Dashboard."] };
      }
      var payload = buildPayload();

      payload.flowMode = FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_DESIGN;
      payload.productOrigin = (state.config && (state.config.baseProductOrigin || state.config.productOrigin)) || "global";
      payload.baseProductOrigin = payload.productOrigin;
      payload.designMode = "creator_design";
      payload.creatorContext = true;
      payload.launchContext = (state.config && (state.config.launchContext || state.config.launchSource)) || "creator_dashboard";
      payload.isCreatorProduct = true;
      payload.projectId = projectId;
      payload.creatorProjectId = projectId;
      payload.designId = String(
        saved.designId ||
        saved.design_id ||
        source && (source.designId || source.design_id) ||
        state.config && state.config.pitchprintDesignId ||
        ""
      ).trim() || null;
      payload.templateProjectId = state.config && state.config.pitchprintProjectId || null;
      payload.fixedColor = selectedColor;
      payload.selectedColor = selectedColor;
      payload.selectedColors = [selectedColor];
      payload.selectedColorDetail = postSaveColorDetail;
      payload.selectedProductionMethod = method ? method.id : null;
      payload.productionMethod = method;
      payload.productionMethodSurcharge = method ? method.surcharge : 0;
      payload.productionMethodSurchargeMinor = method ? method.surchargeMinor : 0;
      payload.productionFeeVariantId = method ? method.feeVariantId || null : null;
      payload.productionFeeVariantGid = method ? method.feeVariantGid || null : null;
      payload.fixedProductionMethod = method ? method.id : null;
      payload.artworkType = artworkType;
      payload.embroiderySubtype = artworkType;
      state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype = artworkType;
      state.creatorArtworkSummary = savedArtwork;
      payload.placementCount = savedArtwork.placementCount;
      payload.activeSide = activeSavedSide(pages);
      payload.placements = placements;
      payload.previews = canonicalPreviews;
      payload.previewUrl = canonicalPreviews[0];
      payload.sidePreviews = sidePreviews;
      payload.artworkSummary = {
        hasText: savedArtwork.hasText,
        hasImage: savedArtwork.hasImage,
        printableObjectCount: savedArtwork.printableObjectCount,
        objectTypes: savedArtwork.objectTypes
      };
      payload.selections = [];
      payload.totalQuantity = 0;
      payload.totalPrice = null;
      payload.cartItems = [];
      return { payload: payload };
    }

    function publishCreatorSetupReady(event) {
      var result = normalizeCreatorSavedResult(event);
      if (!result || !result.payload) {
        var failure = result && result.error || [
          "CREATOR_SAVE_RESULT_UNAVAILABLE",
          "PitchPrint could not prepare the Creator save result."
        ];
        return creatorSaveError(failure[0], failure[1], failure[2]);
      }
      var payload = result.payload;

      window.CustomHousePitchPrintCreatorSetupSelection = payload;

      if (window.console && typeof window.console.info === "function") {
        console.info("CREATOR_SAVE_PROJECT", payload.projectId);
        console.info("CREATOR_SAVE_COLOR", payload.selectedColor);
        console.info("CREATOR_SAVE_ARTWORK_TYPE", payload.artworkType);
        console.info("CREATOR_SAVE_PLACEMENTS", payload.placements.map(function (placement) {
          return { side: placement.side, hasArtwork: placement.hasArtwork };
        }));
        console.info("CREATOR_SAVE_PREVIEWS", Object.keys(payload.sidePreviews));
      }

      try {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(
          "customhouse:pitchprint-creator-setup-ready",
          { detail: payload }
        ));
      } catch (error) {}

      postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_CREATOR_SETUP_READY", payload);

      return payload;
    }

    var creatorSaveBusBound = false;
    function bindCreatorSaveEventBus() {
      if (creatorSaveBusBound) return true;
      var bus = window.ppframe && window.ppframe.eventBus;
      if (!bus || typeof bus.on !== "function") return false;
      bus.on("project-saved", function (event) {
        var saved = savedEventData(event);
        if (!isCreatorDesignFlow() || saved && saved.autoSaved === true) return;
        publishCreatorSetupReady(event);
      });
      bus.on("save-failed", function () {
        if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) return;
        creatorSaveError(
          "CREATOR_SAVE_FAILED",
          "PitchPrint could not save the Creator project. Please try again."
        );
      });
      creatorSaveBusBound = true;
      return true;
    }

    bindCreatorSaveEventBus();
    [50, 200, 500, 1000, 2000, 4000].forEach(function (delay) {
      setTimeout(bindCreatorSaveEventBus, delay);
    });

    function refreshCreatorFinalReview() {
      var summary = buildCurrentCreatorDesignSummary();
      renderEmbroideryTypes();
      updateValidation(false);
      return summary;
    }

    var creatorReviewRefreshTimer = 0;
    function scheduleCreatorReviewRefresh() {
      if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) return;
      clearTimeout(creatorReviewRefreshTimer);
      creatorReviewRefreshTimer = setTimeout(function () {
        if (isCreatorDesignFlow() &&
            document.querySelector('#container [data-module="preview"].shown')) {
          refreshCreatorFinalReview();
        }
      }, 30);
    }

    var artworkBusBound = false;
    var publicArtworkRefreshTimer = 0;
    function refreshPublicArtworkSummary() {
      if (!isPublicFlow()) return null;
      var summary = deriveCurrentArtworkSummary();
      logPublicLaunchDiagnostics();
      renderMethodButtons();
      renderEmbroideryTypes();
      renderReview();
      updateValidation(false);
      return summary;
    }

    function schedulePublicArtworkRefresh() {
      if (!isPublicFlow()) return;
      clearTimeout(publicArtworkRefreshTimer);
      publicArtworkRefreshTimer = setTimeout(refreshPublicArtworkSummary, 30);
    }

    function bindArtworkEventBus() {
      if (artworkBusBound) return true;
      var bus = window.ppframe && window.ppframe.eventBus;
      if (!bus || typeof bus.on !== "function") return false;
      [
        "object-added", "object-removed", "object-modified", "object-updated",
        "canvas-modified", "history-change", "history-changed", "page-changed",
        "design-changed"
      ].forEach(function (name) {
        bus.on(name, schedulePublicArtworkRefresh);
      });
      [
        "preview-shown", "page-render-complete", "canvas-object-added",
        "canvas-object-removed", "canvas-object-modified", "canvas-text-changed"
      ].forEach(function (name) {
        bus.on(name, scheduleCreatorReviewRefresh);
      });
      artworkBusBound = true;
      return true;
    }

    bindArtworkEventBus();
    [50, 200, 500, 1000, 2000, 4000].forEach(function (delay) {
      setTimeout(bindArtworkEventBus, delay);
    });
    ["click", "input", "change"].forEach(function (name) {
      document.addEventListener(name, schedulePublicArtworkRefresh, true);
    });

    function render() {
      if (isPublicFlow()) deriveCurrentArtworkSummary();
      renderMethodButtons();
      renderEmbroideryTypes();
      renderColors();
      renderSizes();
      renderReview();
      updateValidation(false);
      applyFlowSpecificUi(state.config);
    }

    function methodArrayFromSource(methods) {
      methods = parsePossibleJson(methods);
      if (!methods) return [];

      if (Array.isArray(methods)) return methods.map(function (method) {
        return typeof method === "string" ? { code: method } : method;
      }).filter(function (method) { return method && typeof method === "object"; });

      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(methods, "productionMethods")) {
        return methodArrayFromSource(methods.productionMethods);
      }

      // Accept the Shopify JSON metafield contract directly:
      // { version, currency, methods: { EMBROIDERY: {...}, DTF: {...}, DTG: {...} } }
      if (methods.methods && typeof methods.methods === "object") {
        return methodArrayFromSource(methods.methods);
      }

      if (methods.code || methods.id || methods.key) return [methods];

      if (typeof methods === "object") {
        return Object.keys(methods).filter(function (key) {
          return ["version", "currency", "revision"].indexOf(key) === -1;
        }).map(function (key) {
          var value = methods[key];
          if (value == null) value = {};
          if (typeof value === "boolean") value = { enabled: value };
          else if (typeof value !== "object") value = { surcharge: value };

          var copy = {};
          Object.keys(value).forEach(function (field) {
            copy[field] = value[field];
          });
          if (!copy.id && !copy.key && !copy.code) copy.code = key;
          return copy;
        });
      }

      return [];
    }

    function extractProductionMethods(config) {
      if (!config) return null;
      var fields = ["productionMethods", "productionMethodPricing", "production_method_pricing", "productionPricing"];
      for (var i = 0; i < fields.length; i += 1) {
        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(config, fields[i]) || config[fields[i]] == null) continue;
        var source = config[fields[i]];
        // The bridge supplies availability in the array and richer Embroidery
        // tiers in the keyed map. Enrich listed codes only; never append map keys.
        if (fields[i] === "productionMethods") {
          var pricing = methodArrayFromSource(config.productionMethodPricing || config.production_method_pricing);
          return methodArrayFromSource(source).map(function (method) {
            var detail = pricing.find(function (entry) { return sameMethod(entry, method); });
            return Object.assign({}, detail || {}, method);
          });
        }
        return source;
      }
      return null;
    }

    function normalizeMethods(methods) {
      var incoming = methodArrayFromSource(methods);
      var source = incoming;

      return source.map(function (method) {
        var rawId = methodCode(method) || method.name;
        var id = String(rawId || "").trim();

        var hasMinor = method.surchargeMinor != null &&
          method.surchargeMinor !== "" &&
          isFinite(Number(method.surchargeMinor));

        var surcharge = hasMinor
          ? Math.round(Number(method.surchargeMinor)) / 100
          : Number(
              method.surcharge != null ? method.surcharge :
              method.additionalPrice != null ? method.additionalPrice :
              method.extraPrice != null ? method.extraPrice :
              0
            );

        if (!isFinite(surcharge) || surcharge < 0) surcharge = 0;

        return Object.assign({}, method, {
          id: id,
          label: String(method.label || method.name || id.replace(/[_-]+/g, " ")),
          description: String(method.description || method.info || ""),
          // price is retained only for backwards-compatible diagnostics.
          // V10.21 total calculation NEVER treats it as a replacement unit price.
          price: method.price == null || method.price === "" ? null : Number(method.price),
          surcharge: surcharge,
          surchargeMinor: hasMinor
            ? Math.round(Number(method.surchargeMinor))
            : Math.round(surcharge * 100),
          feeVariantId: String(
            method.feeVariantId != null ? method.feeVariantId :
            method.productionFeeVariantId != null ? method.productionFeeVariantId :
            method.shopifyFeeVariantId != null ? method.shopifyFeeVariantId :
            method.fee_variant_id != null ? method.fee_variant_id :
            method.feeVariantGid != null ? method.feeVariantGid :
            ""
          ).replace(/^gid:\/\/shopify\/ProductVariant\//, ""),
          feeVariantGid: String(
            method.feeVariantGid != null ? method.feeVariantGid :
            (
              String(
                method.feeVariantId != null ? method.feeVariantId :
                method.productionFeeVariantId != null ? method.productionFeeVariantId :
                method.shopifyFeeVariantId != null ? method.shopifyFeeVariantId :
                ""
              ).indexOf("gid://") === 0
                ? String(
                    method.feeVariantId != null ? method.feeVariantId :
                    method.productionFeeVariantId != null ? method.productionFeeVariantId :
                    method.shopifyFeeVariantId != null ? method.shopifyFeeVariantId :
                    ""
                  )
                : ""
            )
          ),
          embroideryTypes: method.embroideryTypes ||
            method.embroiderySubtypes ||
            method.embroidery_types ||
            method.embroidery_subtypes ||
            null,
          maxWidthCm: Number(
            method.maxWidthCm != null ? method.maxWidthCm :
            method.maxWidth != null ? method.maxWidth :
            0
          ),
          maxHeightCm: Number(
            method.maxHeightCm != null ? method.maxHeightCm :
            method.maxHeight != null ? method.maxHeight :
            0
          ),
          enabled: method.enabled === false ? false : true
        });
      }).filter(function (method) {
        if (!method.id || !method.label || method.enabled === false) return false;
        if (isEmbroidery(method) && method.embroideryTypes) {
          return ["TEXT_ONLY", "IMAGE_OR_LOGO"].some(function (code) {
            return !!embroiderySubtypeConfig(method, code);
          });
        }
        return true;
      });
    }

    function hasExplicitMethodSurcharge(method) {
      if (!method || typeof method !== "object") return false;

      var keys = [
        "surchargeMinor",
        "surcharge",
        "additionalPrice",
        "extraPrice"
      ];

      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i += 1) {
        var key = keys[i];
        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(method, key)) continue;

        var value = method[key];
        if (value === "" || value == null) continue;
        if (isFinite(Number(value))) return true;
      }

      return false;
    }

    function mergeProductionMethods(currentMethods, incomingSource) {
      var current = Array.isArray(currentMethods) ? currentMethods : [];
      if (incomingSource == null) return current.slice();
      var hadConfiguration = state.methodConfigReceived;
      state.methodConfigReceived = true;
      state.methodConfigSource = incomingSource;
      // Replace availability, retaining metadata only for codes still present.
      // Omitted lists are partial refreshes; an explicit [] is authoritative.
      return normalizeMethods(methodArrayFromSource(incomingSource).map(function (raw) {
        var previous = hadConfiguration && current.find(function (method) { return sameMethod(method, raw); });
        var next = Object.assign({}, previous || {}, raw);
        if (hasExplicitMethodSurcharge(raw) && raw.surchargeMinor == null) delete next.surchargeMinor;
        var tierFields = ["embroideryTypes", "embroiderySubtypes", "embroidery_types", "embroidery_subtypes"];
        tierFields.forEach(function (field) {
          if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw, field)) next.embroideryTypes = raw[field];
        });
        return next;
      }));
    }

    function parsePossibleJson(value) {
      var current = value;

      for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt += 1) {
        if (current == null) return null;
        if (typeof current !== "string") return current;

        var trimmed = current.trim();
        if (!trimmed) return null;

        try {
          current = JSON.parse(trimmed);
        } catch (error) {
          return null;
        }
      }

      return current;
    }

    function variantArrayFrom(value) {
      var parsed = parsePossibleJson(value);

      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) return parsed;

      if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object") {
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.variants)) return parsed.variants;
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.nodes)) return parsed.nodes;
        if (
          parsed.edges &&
          Array.isArray(parsed.edges)
        ) {
          return parsed.edges.map(function (edge) {
            return edge && edge.node ? edge.node : edge;
          }).filter(Boolean);
        }
      }

      return [];
    }

    function extractFullVariantArray(config) {
      if (!config) return [];

      var candidates = [
        config.variants,
        config.allVariants,
        config.productVariants,
        config.shopifyVariants,
        config.baseProductVariants,
        config.baseProductVariantsJson,
        config.variantsJson,
        config.product && config.product.variants,
        config.product && config.product.variantsJson
      ];

      for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length; i += 1) {
        var variants = variantArrayFrom(candidates[i]);
        if (variants.length) return variants;
      }

      return [];
    }

    function extractOptionDefinitions(config, variants) {
      if (!config) return deriveOptionDefinitions(config, variants);

      var candidates = [
        config.options,
        config.productOptions,
        config.optionDefinitions,
        config.product && config.product.options
      ];
