      for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length; i += 1) {
        var parsed = parsePossibleJson(candidates[i]);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length) {
          return parsed;
        }
      }

      return deriveOptionDefinitions(config, variants);
    }

    function productIdentity(config) {
      if (!config) return "";

      if (config.productId != null && config.productId !== "") {
        return "id:" + String(config.productId);
      }

      if (config.productHandle) {
        return "handle:" + normalizeName(config.productHandle);
      }

      return "";
    }

    function sameProductConfig(previous, incoming) {
      if (!previous || !incoming) return true;

      var previousId = previous.productId != null
        ? String(previous.productId)
        : "";
      var incomingId = incoming.productId != null
        ? String(incoming.productId)
        : "";

      if (previousId && incomingId) return previousId === incomingId;

      var previousHandle = normalizeName(previous.productHandle);
      var incomingHandle = normalizeName(incoming.productHandle);

      if (previousHandle && incomingHandle) {
        return previousHandle === incomingHandle;
      }

      // A pricing-only or metadata-only message may intentionally omit product
      // identity. Treat that as a refresh for the already active product.
      return true;
    }

    function rawVariantId(variant) {
      return normalizeVariantId(
        variant && (
          variant.id ||
          variant.variantId ||
          variant.legacyResourceId
        )
      );
    }

    function mergeRawVariants(existing, incoming) {
      var oldList = Array.isArray(existing) ? existing : [];
      var newList = Array.isArray(incoming) ? incoming : [];

      if (!newList.length) return oldList.slice();
      if (!oldList.length) return newList.slice();

      var order = [];
      var byId = {};

      oldList.forEach(function (variant) {
        var id = rawVariantId(variant);
        if (!id) return;
        if (!byId[id]) order.push(id);
        byId[id] = variant;
      });

      newList.forEach(function (variant) {
        var id = rawVariantId(variant);
        if (!id) return;

        if (!byId[id]) order.push(id);

        var previous = byId[id] || {};
        var merged = {};

        Object.keys(previous).forEach(function (key) {
          merged[key] = previous[key];
        });

        Object.keys(variant || {}).forEach(function (key) {
          merged[key] = variant[key];
        });

        byId[id] = merged;
      });

      return order.map(function (id) {
        return byId[id];
      }).filter(Boolean);
    }

    function mergeConfigPreservingProductState(previous, incoming) {
      if (!previous) return incoming || null;
      if (!incoming) return previous;

      if (!sameProductConfig(previous, incoming)) {
        return incoming;
      }

      var merged = {};

      Object.keys(previous).forEach(function (key) {
        merged[key] = previous[key];
      });

      Object.keys(incoming).forEach(function (key) {
        merged[key] = incoming[key];
      });

      // Critical V10.23 rule:
      // Pricing bridge refreshes can be intentionally narrow. They must not
      // delete the richer variant/color/size snapshot already loaded earlier.
      var previousVariants = extractFullVariantArray(previous);
      var incomingVariants = extractFullVariantArray(incoming);

      merged.variants = mergeRawVariants(
        previousVariants,
        incomingVariants
      );

      if (
        (!Array.isArray(incoming.options) || !incoming.options.length) &&
        Array.isArray(previous.options)
      ) {
        merged.options = previous.options;
      }

      [
        "colorValues",
        "colorOptionValues",
        "colors",
        "availableColors",
        "productColors",
        "sizeValues",
        "sizeOptionValues",
        "sizes",
        "optionGroups"
      ].forEach(function (key) {
        if (
          (!Array.isArray(incoming[key]) || !incoming[key].length) &&
          Array.isArray(previous[key])
        ) {
          merged[key] = previous[key];
        }
      });

      if (!incoming.optionNames && previous.optionNames) {
        merged.optionNames = previous.optionNames;
      }

      if (
        incoming.basePrice == null &&
        incoming.basePriceMinor == null &&
        previous.basePrice != null
      ) {
        merged.basePrice = previous.basePrice;
      }

      if (
        incoming.basePrice == null &&
        incoming.basePriceMinor == null &&
        previous.basePriceMinor != null
      ) {
        merged.basePriceMinor = previous.basePriceMinor;
      }

      return merged;
    }

    function resetProductSelectionState(resetMethods) {
      state.initializedFromProduct = false;
      if (resetMethods) {
        state.methods = [];
        state.methodConfigReceived = false;
        state.methodListReceived = false;
        state.methodConfigSource = null;
        state.methodConfigRevision = null;
        state.methodConfigProductId = null;
        state.methodConfigChannel = null;
        state.methodTrace = null;
        state.selectedMethod = null;
        state.creatorFixedMethod = null;
      }
      state.userChangedColor = false;
      state.userChangedMethod = false;
      state.userChangedEmbroiderySubtype = false;
      state.copyrightConfirmed = false;
      state.nonReturnConfirmed = false;
      state.selectedColor = null;
      state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype = null;
      state.creatorArtworkSummary = null;
      state.creatorArtworkDiagnostics = null;
      state.publicArtworkSummary = null;
      state.publicArtworkDiagnostics = null;
      state.publicArtworkDiagnosticSignature = null;
      state.creatorPreSaveSummary = null;
      state.creatorNativeColorVerified = false;
      state.creatorNativeColorSource = null;
      state.creatorNativeColorDetail = null;
      state.quantities = {};
      state.variantSelections = [];
      state.activePreviewColor = null;
      state.publicVariantMatrixSource = null;
      state.publicLaunchDiagnostics = null;
      state.publicConfigAckSignature = null;
      state.variants = [];
      state.options = [];
      state.colorName = null;
      state.sizeName = null;
    }

    function configuredProductionMethodId(config) {
      if (!config) return "";
      var value = config.fixedProductionMethod ||
        config.selectedProductionMethod ||
        config.productionMethod ||
        "";
      if (value && typeof value === "object") {
        value = methodCode(value);
      }
      return methodCode(value);
    }

    function lockCreatorProductionMethod(config) {
      if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) return configuredProductionMethodId(config);
      var launchMethod = methodCode(
        state.launchContext && state.launchContext.fixedProductionMethod
      );
      var configuredMethod = launchMethod || configuredProductionMethodId(config);
      if (!state.creatorFixedMethod && configuredMethod) {
        state.creatorFixedMethod = configuredMethod;
      }
      if (state.creatorFixedMethod) {
        var allowed = state.methods.find(function (method) { return sameMethod(method.id, state.creatorFixedMethod); });
        state.creatorFixedMethod = allowed ? allowed.id : null;
        state.selectedMethod = allowed ? allowed.id : null;
        state.userChangedMethod = false;
      }
      if (window.console && typeof window.console.info === "function") {
        console.info("CREATOR_FIXED_METHOD_CONFIG", state.creatorFixedMethod || null);
        console.info("CREATOR_SELECTED_METHOD_FINAL", state.selectedMethod || null);
      }
      return state.creatorFixedMethod || configuredMethod;
    }

    function reconcileProductionMethod(config) {
      var selected = state.methods.find(function (method) { return sameMethod(method.id, state.selectedMethod); });
      var configured = state.methods.find(function (method) { return sameMethod(method.id, configuredProductionMethodId(config)); });
      state.selectedMethod = (selected || configured || state.methods[0] || {}).id || null;
      lockCreatorProductionMethod(config);
      if (isCreatorDesignFlow() && !state.creatorFixedMethod) {
        if (state.methods.length === 1) {
          state.selectedMethod = state.methods[0].id;
          state.creatorFixedMethod = state.selectedMethod;
        }
        else state.selectedMethod = null;
      }
    }

    function receiveProductionMethods(config, channel) {
      var hasList = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(config, "productionMethods") && config.productionMethods != null;
      var source = extractProductionMethods(hasList ? Object.assign({}, state.config || {}, config) : config);
      if (source == null) return;
      state.methodTrace = {
        raw: methodArrayFromSource(source).map(function (method) {
          return {
            code: methodCode(method),
            label: String(method.label || method.name || "").slice(0, 120),
            enabled: typeof method.enabled === "boolean" ? method.enabled : null,
            surchargeMinor: method.surchargeMinor != null && isFinite(Number(method.surchargeMinor)) ? Number(method.surchargeMinor) : null,
            currency: String(method.currency || config.currency || "").slice(0, 3)
          };
        }),
        afterNormalize: normalizeMethods(source).map(function (method) { return method.id; }),
        afterMerge: state.methods.map(function (method) { return method.id; }),
        beforeRender: state.methods.map(function (method) { return method.id; }),
        ignoredOlderRevision: false
      };
      // A pricing catalog is not a product availability list, even before the
      // first list arrives. Keep waiting rather than enabling its catalog keys.
      if (!hasList && !state.methodListReceived) return;
      if (!hasList && state.methodListReceived) {
        var pricingOnly = methodArrayFromSource(source);
        source = state.methods.map(function (method) {
          var detail = pricingOnly.find(function (entry) { return sameMethod(entry, method); });
          return detail ? Object.assign({}, detail, { id: method.id, code: method.code || method.id }) : method;
        });
      }
      var raw = config.productionMethods != null ? parsePossibleJson(config.productionMethods)
        : parsePossibleJson(config.productionMethodPricing || config.production_method_pricing || config.productionPricing);
      // `version` is the bridge schema version, not a freshness counter.
      var revision = config.revision != null ? config.revision : raw && raw.revision;
      var comparable = revision != null && revision !== "" && isFinite(Number(revision));
      if (comparable && state.methodConfigRevision != null && Number(revision) < state.methodConfigRevision) {
        state.methodTrace.ignoredOlderRevision = true;
        if (state.config) state.config.productionMethods = state.methods.slice();
        return;
      }
      state.methods = mergeProductionMethods(state.methods, source);
      state.methodTrace.afterMerge = state.methods.map(function (method) { return method.id; });
      state.methodConfigProductId = config.productId || state.config && state.config.productId || null;
      state.methodConfigChannel = channel || "production-method API";
      if (hasList) state.methodListReceived = true;
      if (comparable) state.methodConfigRevision = Number(revision);
      // Keep the common config snapshot in sync with standalone method events,
      // so a later launch-context refresh cannot replay an obsolete method list.
      if (state.config) state.config.productionMethods = state.methods.slice();
    }

    function productionMethodDiagnostics() {
      var method = getSelectedMethod();
      return {
        PP_FLOW_MODE: state.flowMode,
        PP_PRODUCT_ID: state.config && state.config.productId || null,
        PP_CONFIG_RECEIVED: !!state.config,
        PP_RAW_PRODUCTION_METHODS: state.methodTrace ? state.methodTrace.raw : [],
        PP_AUTHORITATIVE_METHOD_CODES: state.methods.map(function (entry) { return entry.id; }),
        PP_AUTHORITATIVE_METHOD_COUNT: state.methods.length,
        PP_CONFIG_REVISION: state.methodConfigRevision,
        PP_METHOD_PRODUCT_ID: state.methodConfigProductId,
        PP_METHOD_CONFIG_CHANNEL: state.methodConfigChannel,
        METHODS_AFTER_RECEIVE: state.methodTrace ? state.methodTrace.raw.map(function (method) { return method.code; }) : [],
        METHODS_AFTER_NORMALIZE: state.methodTrace ? state.methodTrace.afterNormalize : [],
        METHODS_AFTER_MERGE: state.methodTrace ? state.methodTrace.afterMerge : [],
        METHODS_BEFORE_RENDER: state.methodTrace ? state.methodTrace.beforeRender : [],
        PP_IGNORED_OLDER_METHOD_REVISION: !!(state.methodTrace && state.methodTrace.ignoredOlderRevision),
        PP_METHOD_CONFIG_RECEIVED: state.methodConfigReceived,
        PP_ENABLED_METHOD_CODES: state.methods.map(function (entry) { return entry.id; }),
        PP_METHOD_COUNT: state.methods.length,
        PP_SELECTED_METHOD: state.selectedMethod,
        PP_EMBROIDERY_TEXT_ENABLED: state.methods.some(function (entry) { return isEmbroidery(entry) && !!embroiderySubtypeConfig(entry, "TEXT_ONLY"); }),
        PP_EMBROIDERY_IMAGE_ENABLED: state.methods.some(function (entry) { return isEmbroidery(entry) && !!embroiderySubtypeConfig(entry, "IMAGE_OR_LOGO"); }),
        PP_ARTWORK_TYPE: (state.creatorArtworkSummary || state.publicArtworkSummary || {}).artworkType || null,
        PP_RESOLVED_EMBROIDERY_TIER: isEmbroidery(method) ? state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype : null
      };
    }

    function configuredEmbroiderySubtype(config) {
      if (!config) return null;
      return normalizeEmbroiderySubtype(
        config.embroiderySubtype ||
        config.embroideryType ||
        config.embroidery_type ||
        config.selectedEmbroiderySubtype
      );
    }

    function configBasePrice(config) {
      if (!config) return null;

      var fromMinor = normalizeMoneyFromMinor(config.basePriceMinor);
      if (fromMinor != null) return fromMinor;

      var value = config.basePrice;
      if (value && typeof value === "object") {
        if (value.amount != null) value = value.amount;
        else if (value.value != null) value = value.value;
      }

      if (value == null || value === "" || !isFinite(Number(value))) {
        return null;
      }

      return Number(value);
    }


    function applyFlowSpecificUi(config) {
      var container = document.getElementById("container");
      if (!container) return;

      var creatorDesign = state.flowMode === FLOW_MODES.CREATOR_DESIGN;

      container.dataset.chFlowMode = state.flowMode;
      container.classList.toggle("ch-flow-public", state.flowMode === FLOW_MODES.PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE);
      container.classList.toggle("ch-flow-creator", creatorDesign);
      window.CustomHousePitchPrintFlowMode = state.flowMode;

      var kicker = document.getElementById("ch-order-kicker");
      var heading = document.getElementById("ch-order-title");
      var subtitle = document.getElementById("ch-order-subtitle");
      var colorHelp = document.getElementById("ch-color-help");
      var productionSection = document.getElementById("ch-production-section");
      var colorSection = document.getElementById("ch-color-section");
      var sizeSection = document.getElementById("ch-size-section");
      var methodReviewRow = document.getElementById("ch-review-method-row");
      var sizesReviewRow = document.getElementById("ch-review-sizes-row");
      var quantityReviewRow = document.getElementById("ch-review-quantity-row");
      var placementsReviewRow = document.getElementById("ch-review-placements-row");
      var priceReviewRow = document.getElementById("ch-review-price-row");
      var reviewLabel = document.getElementById("ch-order-review-label");
      var creatorNote = document.getElementById("ch-creator-setup-note");
      var copyrightText = document.getElementById("ch-copyright-text");
      var nonReturnRow = document.getElementById("ch-nonreturn-row");
      var continueButton = document.getElementById("ch-continue-cart");
      var hint = document.getElementById("ch-order-hint");
      var quantityError = document.getElementById("ch-order-error");

      if (kicker) kicker.textContent = "Final step";
      if (heading) heading.textContent = creatorDesign ? "Submit Design" : "Order details";
      if (subtitle) {
        subtitle.textContent = creatorDesign
          ? "Review your artwork and confirm your rights before submitting this Creator design for admin review."
          : "Review your artwork and choose production, color, sizes and quantities before continuing.";
      }
      if (colorHelp) {
        colorHelp.textContent = creatorDesign
          ? "The selected garment color is added to this Creator design automatically."
          : "Choose a color, set its size quantities, then switch to another color if needed. Previous quantities will stay selected.";
      }

      if (productionSection) productionSection.hidden = creatorDesign;
      if (colorSection) colorSection.hidden = creatorDesign;
      if (sizeSection) sizeSection.hidden = creatorDesign;
      if (methodReviewRow) {
        methodReviewRow.hidden = creatorDesign ? !getSelectedMethod() : false;
      }
      if (sizesReviewRow) sizesReviewRow.hidden = creatorDesign;
      if (quantityReviewRow) quantityReviewRow.hidden = creatorDesign;
      if (placementsReviewRow) placementsReviewRow.hidden = creatorDesign;
      if (priceReviewRow) priceReviewRow.hidden = creatorDesign;
      if (creatorNote) creatorNote.hidden = true;
      if (copyrightText) {
        copyrightText.textContent = creatorDesign
          ? "I confirm that I own or have the necessary rights to use this artwork/design."
          : "I confirm that I have the necessary rights/copyrights to use the image or design I provided.";
      }
      if (nonReturnRow) nonReturnRow.hidden = creatorDesign;
      if (reviewLabel) reviewLabel.textContent = creatorDesign ? "Design review" : "Order review";

      if (continueButton) {
        continueButton.textContent = creatorDesign
          ? "Submit Design"
          : "Continue to cart";
      }

      if (hint) {
        hint.textContent = creatorDesign
          ? "Your design will be submitted for admin review with the active garment color. Customer order options are handled later on the product page."
          : "Your final artwork, selected production method, color, sizes and quantities will be sent to the product/cart flow.";
      }

      if (quantityError && !creatorDesign) {
        quantityError.textContent = "Select at least one size and quantity.";
      }

      bindAcknowledgementControls();

      if (typeof window.chPitchPrintSyncFinalOrderPanel === "function") {
        window.chPitchPrintSyncFinalOrderPanel();
      }
    }

    function applyEditorUiConfig(config) {
      var ui = config && config.editorUi ? config.editorUi : null;
      var container = document.getElementById("container");
      var guide = document.getElementById("ch-design-next-step-guide");
      if (!container || !guide) return;

      if (typeof window.chPitchPrintMountNextStepGuideInToolbar === "function") {
        window.chPitchPrintMountNextStepGuideInToolbar();
      }

      var enabled = !!(ui && ui.enabled !== false);
      var desktopSideNavigation = !!(
        enabled &&
        ui.desktopSideNavigation !== false
      );

      container.classList.toggle("ch-client-review-ui", enabled);
      container.classList.toggle(
        "ch-client-desktop-side-nav",
        desktopSideNavigation
      );

      syncNextStepGuideCopy();

      applyFlowSpecificUi(config);

      var requestedSize = Number(
        ui && ui.desktopSideNavigationSize
      );

      if (isFinite(requestedSize) && requestedSize >= 88 && requestedSize <= 128) {
        container.style.setProperty(
          "--ch-client-nav-thumb-width",
          Math.round(requestedSize) + "px"
        );
      }
    }

    function setConfig(config, channel) {
      // The dashboard sends Creator intent in ORDER_CONFIG_DATA as well as
      // dedicated context messages. Lock either entry point before merging.
      var incomingContext = normalizeLaunchContext(config);
      if (incomingContext && incomingContext.flowMode !== FLOW_MODES.PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE) {
        var lockedContext = state.launchContext || {};
        Object.keys(incomingContext).forEach(function (key) {
          if (
            key === "fixedProductionMethod" &&
            state.launchContextLocked &&
            lockedContext.fixedProductionMethod
          ) return;
          if (incomingContext[key] != null && incomingContext[key] !== "") {
            lockedContext[key] = incomingContext[key];
          }
        });
        state.launchContext = lockedContext;
        state.launchContextLocked = true;
        state.launchContextResolved = true;
      }
      config = mergeLaunchContextIntoConfig(config);

      var wasActive = state.active;
      var previousConfig = state.config;
      var productChanged =
        !!previousConfig &&
        !!config &&
        !sameProductConfig(previousConfig, config);

      if (productChanged) {
        resetProductSelectionState(true);
      }

      var effectiveConfig = mergeConfigPreservingProductState(
        productChanged ? null : previousConfig,
        config
      );

      var nextFlowMode = resolveFlowMode(effectiveConfig);
      if (previousConfig && state.flowMode !== nextFlowMode) {
        resetProductSelectionState();
      }

      state.config = effectiveConfig || null;
      state.flowMode = nextFlowMode;
      state.active = isSupportedEditorConfig(effectiveConfig);
      receiveProductionMethods(config, channel);
      applyEditorUiConfig(effectiveConfig);

      // V10.41: the parent config commonly arrives after init().
      // applyEditorUiConfig() can hide the native desktop navigator, so
      // immediately render/sync the external clickable proxy in the same turn.
      scheduleDesktopProxyNavigatorSync();

      if (!state.active) {
        // If no full public-product snapshot has ever arrived, there is
        // genuinely nothing to render. But never erase an already-loaded
        // variant matrix just because a pricing-only message arrived.
        if (!wasActive) {
          state.variants = [];
          state.options = [];
          state.colorName = null;
          state.sizeName = null;
        }

        if (!state.initializedFromProduct) {
          state.selectedColor = null;
          state.quantities = {};
        }

        if (!state.userChangedMethod) {
          state.selectedMethod =
            configuredProductionMethodId(effectiveConfig) ||
            (state.methods[0] && state.methods[0].id) ||
            null;
        }
        if (isCreatorDesignFlow() && !state.userChangedEmbroiderySubtype) {
          state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype = configuredEmbroiderySubtype(effectiveConfig);
        } else if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) {
          state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype = null;
        }
        reconcileProductionMethod(effectiveConfig);

        render();
        scheduleCreatorGarmentColorSync();
        return false;
      }

      var fullRawVariants = extractFullVariantArray(effectiveConfig);

      state.options = extractOptionDefinitions(
        effectiveConfig,
        fullRawVariants
      );

      state.variants = fullRawVariants.map(function (variant) {
        return normalizeVariant(variant, state.options);
      }).filter(function (variant) {
        return !!variant.id;
      });

      state.colorName =
        (
          effectiveConfig.optionNames &&
          effectiveConfig.optionNames.color
        ) ||
        findOptionName(
          state.options,
          ["Color", "Colour", "Färg", "Farg"]
        );

      state.sizeName =
        (
          effectiveConfig.optionNames &&
          effectiveConfig.optionNames.size
        ) ||
        findOptionName(
          state.options,
          ["Size", "Storlek"]
        );

      var shouldApplyInitialPublicSelection = !state.initializedFromProduct;

      // Product-page selections are used only as INITIAL values.
      // Once the customer changes Color/Method/Quantity on the save page,
      // config refreshes must not overwrite their choices.
      if (!state.initializedFromProduct) {
        state.selectedColor = isCreatorDesignFlow()
          ? (effectiveConfig.fixedColor || effectiveConfig.selectedColor || null)
          : (
              effectiveConfig.selectedColor ||
              (
                Array.isArray(effectiveConfig.colorValues) &&
                effectiveConfig.colorValues[0]
              ) ||
              null
            );
        if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) state.activePreviewColor = state.selectedColor;

        if (!state.userChangedMethod) {
          state.selectedMethod =
            configuredProductionMethodId(effectiveConfig) ||
            (state.methods[0] && state.methods[0].id) ||
            null;
        }
        if (isCreatorDesignFlow() && !state.userChangedEmbroiderySubtype) {
          state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype = configuredEmbroiderySubtype(effectiveConfig);
        } else if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) {
          state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype = null;
        }

        state.quantities = {};

        if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) {
          var initialVariantId = normalizeVariantId(
            effectiveConfig.initialVariantId || ""
          );
          var initialQuantity = clampQuantity(
            effectiveConfig.initialQuantity || 1
          );

          if (initialVariantId && initialQuantity > 0) {
            state.quantities[initialVariantId] = initialQuantity;
          } else if (effectiveConfig.selectedSize) {
            var initialVariant = state.variants.find(function (variant) {
              var colorMatches =
                !state.colorName ||
                !state.selectedColor ||
                normalizeName(readOption(variant.options, state.colorName)) ===
                  normalizeName(state.selectedColor);

              var sizeMatches =
                state.sizeName &&
                normalizeName(readOption(variant.options, state.sizeName)) ===
                  normalizeName(effectiveConfig.selectedSize);

              return variant.available && colorMatches && sizeMatches;
            });

            if (initialVariant) {
              state.quantities[initialVariant.id] = initialQuantity;
            }
          }
        }

        state.initializedFromProduct = true;
      }

      if (isPublicFlow()) {
        initializePublicVariantSelections(
          effectiveConfig,
          shouldApplyInitialPublicSelection
        );
        if (!state.activePreviewColor) {
          state.activePreviewColor = state.selectedColor || publicMatrixColors()[0] || null;
        }
        state.selectedColor = state.activePreviewColor;
        logPublicLaunchDiagnostics();
      }

      reconcileProductionMethod(effectiveConfig);

      state.designDimensionsCm =
        effectiveConfig.currentDesignDimensionsCm ||
        state.designDimensionsCm ||
        null;

      state.hasUnusedExampleText =
        effectiveConfig.hasUnusedExampleText != null
          ? !!effectiveConfig.hasUnusedExampleText
          : state.hasUnusedExampleText;

      var orderError = document.getElementById("ch-order-error");
      if (orderError) {
        orderError.classList.remove("ch-show");
        orderError.textContent = "Select at least one size and quantity.";
      }

      render();
      scheduleCreatorGarmentColorSync();
      return true;
    }

    window.CustomHousePitchPrintOrderConfig = {
      setConfig: setConfig,
      setDimensions: function (dimensions) {
        state.designDimensionsCm = dimensions || null;
        updateValidation(false);
      },
      setExampleTextState: function (hasUnusedExampleText) {
        state.hasUnusedExampleText = !!hasUnusedExampleText;
        updateValidation(false);
      },
      requestExampleTextCleanup: requestUnusedExampleTextCleanup,
      requestDimensionEnforcement: requestDimensionEnforcement,
      requestDesignDimensions: requestDesignDimensions,
      setProductionMethods: function (methods) {
        receiveProductionMethods({ productionMethods: methods });
        reconcileProductionMethod(state.config);
        render();
      },
      getSelection: buildPayload,
      validate: function () {
        return updateValidation(true);
      },
      requestConfig: requestProductConfig,
      requestFullProductConfig: requestFullProductConfig,
      syncCreatorGarmentColor: syncCreatorGarmentColor,
      deriveCurrentArtworkSummary: deriveCurrentArtworkSummary,
      refreshPublicArtworkSummary: refreshPublicArtworkSummary,
      deriveCurrentCreatorArtworkSummary: deriveCurrentCreatorArtworkSummary,
      buildCurrentCreatorDesignSummary: buildCurrentCreatorDesignSummary,
      refreshCreatorFinalReview: refreshCreatorFinalReview,
      syncNextStepGuideCopy: syncNextStepGuideCopy,
      debugState: function () {
        return {
          active: state.active,
          flowMode: state.flowMode,
          launchContextLocked: state.launchContextLocked,
          launchContextResolved: state.launchContextResolved,
          launchContext: state.launchContext,
          colorName: state.colorName,
          sizeName: state.sizeName,
          selectedColor: state.selectedColor,
          activePreviewColor: state.activePreviewColor,
          creatorNativeColorVerified: state.creatorNativeColorVerified,
          creatorNativeColorSource: state.creatorNativeColorSource,
          creatorNativeColorDetail: state.creatorNativeColorDetail,
          selectedMethod: state.selectedMethod,
          methodConfiguration: productionMethodDiagnostics(),
          creatorFixedMethod: state.creatorFixedMethod,
          selectedEmbroiderySubtype: state.selectedEmbroiderySubtype,
          creatorArtworkSummary: state.creatorArtworkSummary,
          creatorArtworkDiagnostics: state.creatorArtworkDiagnostics,
          publicArtworkSummary: state.publicArtworkSummary,
          publicArtworkDiagnostics: state.publicArtworkDiagnostics,
          creatorPreSaveSummary: state.creatorPreSaveSummary,
          quantities: Object.assign({}, state.quantities),
          variantSelections: state.variantSelections.map(function (selection) {
            return Object.assign({}, selection);
          }),
          publicVariantMatrixSource: state.publicVariantMatrixSource,
          publicLaunchDiagnostics: state.publicLaunchDiagnostics,
          publicCartHandoff: Object.assign({}, state.publicCartHandoff, {
            validation: state.publicCartHandoff.validation
              ? Object.assign({}, state.publicCartHandoff.validation)
              : null
          }),
          selections: collectSelections(),
          colors: configuredProductColors(state.config),
          sizes: state.config && state.config.sizeValues,
          adminPricingSource:
            state.config && state.config.adminPricingSource,
          layoutVersion: window.ppLayoutVersion,
          productIdentity: productIdentity(state.config),
          variantCount: state.variants.length,
          needsFullVariantRecovery: needsFullVariantRecovery(),
          totalQuantity: totalQuantity(),
          totalPrice: calculateTotalPrice(),
          pricingPayloadPresent: state.methodConfigReceived,
          productionMethods: state.methods.map(function (method) {
            return {
              id: method.id,
              label: method.label,
              surcharge: method.surcharge,
              surchargeMinor: method.surchargeMinor,
              maxWidthCm: method.maxWidthCm,
              maxHeightCm: method.maxHeightCm
            };
          }),
          variants: state.variants.map(function (variant) {
            return {
              id: variant.id,
              available: variant.available,
              price: variant.price,
              options: variant.options
            };
          })
        };
      },
      publishCartReady: publishCartReady,
      publishCreatorSetupReady: publishCreatorSetupReady
    };

    var creatorFinalSubmitRequest = null;
    function requestCreatorFinalSubmit(nativeOk) {
      var creatorProductId = String(
        state.config && state.config.creatorProductId || ""
      ).trim();
      if (!creatorProductId) {
        return creatorSaveError(
          "CREATOR_PRODUCT_ID_UNAVAILABLE",
          "CustomHouse could not identify this Creator Product. Reopen the designer and try again."
        );
      }
      if (creatorFinalSubmitRequest) return true;
      var requestId = "creator-submit-" + String(Date.now()) + "-" +
        Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
      creatorFinalSubmitRequest = {
        creatorProductId: creatorProductId,
        requestId: requestId,
        nativeOk: nativeOk,
        timeout: setTimeout(function () {
          creatorFinalSubmitRequest = null;
          creatorSaveError(
            "CREATOR_FINAL_SUBMIT_INTENT_TIMEOUT",
            "CustomHouse could not start the final submission. Please try again."
          );
        }, 5000)
      };
      var targetCount = postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_CREATOR_FINAL_SUBMIT_INTENT", {
        creatorProductId: creatorProductId,
        requestId: requestId
      });
      if (!targetCount) {
        clearTimeout(creatorFinalSubmitRequest.timeout);
        creatorFinalSubmitRequest = null;
        return creatorSaveError(
          "CREATOR_FINAL_SUBMIT_HOST_UNAVAILABLE",
          "CustomHouse could not start the final submission. Reopen the designer and try again."
        );
      }
      return true;
    }

    function acknowledgeCreatorFinalSubmit(payload) {
      if (!creatorFinalSubmitRequest || !payload) return false;
      if (
        String(payload.creatorProductId || "") !== creatorFinalSubmitRequest.creatorProductId ||
        String(payload.requestId || "") !== creatorFinalSubmitRequest.requestId
      ) return false;
      var request = creatorFinalSubmitRequest;
      creatorFinalSubmitRequest = null;
      clearTimeout(request.timeout);
      request.nativeOk.click();
      return true;
    }

    window.chPitchPrintContinueToCart = function (event) {
      if (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
      }

      if (isPublicFlow() && state.publicCartHandoff.inFlight) return false;
      if (isPublicFlow()) {
        state.publicCartHandoff.clickedCount += 1;
        if (window.console && typeof console.info === "function") {
          console.info("PUBLIC_CONTINUE_CLICKED");
        }
      }

      if (isCreatorDesignFlow()) prepareCreatorFinalSave();
      requestUnusedExampleTextCleanup();
      requestDesignDimensions();
      if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) {
        requestDimensionEnforcement();
      }

      var validationResult = updateValidation(true);
      if (isPublicFlow()) {
        state.publicCartHandoff.valid = validationResult === true;
        publicContinueValidationDiagnostics(validationResult);
        if (window.console && typeof console.info === "function") {
          console.info("PUBLIC_CONTINUE_VALID", validationResult === true);
        }
      }

      if (validationResult === null) {
        requestProductConfig();
        var loadingError = document.getElementById("ch-order-error");
        if (loadingError) {
          loadingError.textContent = "Product options are still loading.";
          loadingError.classList.add("ch-show");
        }
        return false;
      }

      if (validationResult === false) return false;

      if (isCreatorDesignFlow()) {
        var nativeOk = document.querySelector(
          '#container [data-module="preview"] [data-cmd="ok"]'
        );
        if (nativeOk) {
          return !!requestCreatorFinalSubmit(nativeOk);
        }
        creatorSaveError("CREATOR_SAVE_CONTROL_UNAVAILABLE", "PitchPrint's native save control is unavailable. Please reopen the designer and try again.");
      } else {
        return !!publishCartReady();
      }
      return true;
    };

    window.addEventListener(
      "customhouse:pitchprint-launch-context-data",
      function (event) {
        applyLaunchContext(event && event.detail ? event.detail : null);
      }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
      "customhouse:pitchprint-creator-context-data",
      function (event) {
        applyLaunchContext(event && event.detail ? event.detail : null);
      }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
      "customhouse:pitchprint-order-config-data",
      function (event) {
        setConfig(event && event.detail ? event.detail : null, "customhouse:pitchprint-order-config-data");
      }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
      "customhouse:pitchprint-example-text-state",
      function (event) {
        state.hasUnusedExampleText = !!(
          event && event.detail && event.detail.hasUnusedExampleText
        );
        updateValidation(false);
      }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
      "customhouse:pitchprint-design-dimensions",
      function (event) {
        state.designDimensionsCm = event && event.detail
          ? event.detail
          : null;
        updateValidation(false);
      }
    );

    window.addEventListener("message", function (event) {
      var data = event && event.data;
      if (!data || !data.type) return;

      if (data.type === "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_CREATOR_FINAL_SUBMIT_INTENT_ACK") {
        acknowledgeCreatorFinalSubmit(data.payload || null);
        return;
      }

      if (
        data.type === "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_LAUNCH_CONTEXT_DATA" ||
        data.type === "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_CREATOR_CONTEXT_DATA"
      ) {
        applyLaunchContext(data.payload || null);
        requestProductConfig();
        return;
      }

      if (
        data.type === "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_CREATOR_CONFIG_DATA" ||
        data.type === "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_ORDER_CONFIG_DATA" ||
        data.type === "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_FULL_PRODUCT_CONFIG" ||
        data.type === "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_FULL_PRODUCT_CONFIG_DATA"
      ) {
        setConfig(data.payload || null, data.type);
        return;
      }

      if (data.type === "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_VARIANTS_DATA") {
        var variantPayload = data.payload || {};
        setConfig({
          productId: variantPayload.productId ||
            (state.config && state.config.productId) ||
            null,
          productHandle: variantPayload.productHandle ||
            (state.config && state.config.productHandle) ||
            null,
          variants: variantPayload.variants ||
            variantPayload.allVariants ||
            variantPayload.productVariants ||
            [],
          options: variantPayload.options ||
            variantPayload.productOptions ||
            null,
          optionNames: variantPayload.optionNames || null,
          colorValues: variantPayload.colorValues || null,
          colorOptionValues: variantPayload.colorOptionValues || null,
          colors: variantPayload.colors || null,
          availableColors: variantPayload.availableColors || null,
          productColors: variantPayload.productColors || null,
          optionGroups: variantPayload.optionGroups || null,
          sizeValues: variantPayload.sizeValues || null,
          selectedColor: variantPayload.selectedColor,
          selectedSize: variantPayload.selectedSize,
          initialVariantId: variantPayload.initialVariantId,
          initialQuantity: variantPayload.initialQuantity
        });
        return;
      }

      if (data.type === "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_DESIGN_DIMENSIONS") {
        state.designDimensionsCm = data.payload || null;
        updateValidation(false);
        return;
      }

      if (data.type === "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_PRODUCTION_METHODS") {
        var methodsPayload = data.payload;
        if (methodsPayload && (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(methodsPayload, "productionMethods") ||
            Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(methodsPayload, "productionMethodPricing"))) {
          receiveProductionMethods(methodsPayload);
        } else {
          receiveProductionMethods({ productionMethods: methodsPayload });
        }
        reconcileProductionMethod(state.config);
        render();
        return;
      }

      if (data.type === "CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_EXAMPLE_TEXT_STATE") {
        state.hasUnusedExampleText = !!(
          data.payload && data.payload.hasUnusedExampleText
        );
        updateValidation(false);
        return;
      }
    });

    document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
      var target = event.target;
      var button = target && target.closest
        ? target.closest(
            '#container [data-module="preview"] [data-cmd="ok"]'
          )
        : null;

      if (!button) return;

      if (isCreatorDesignFlow()) prepareCreatorFinalSave();
      requestUnusedExampleTextCleanup();
      requestDesignDimensions();
      if (!isCreatorDesignFlow()) {
        requestDimensionEnforcement();
      }

      var validationResult = updateValidation(true);

      if (validationResult === false) {
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopImmediatePropagation();
        event.stopPropagation();
        return false;
      }

      if (validationResult === true) {
        if (isCreatorDesignFlow()) {
          bindCreatorSaveEventBus();
        } else if (isPublicFlow()) {
          publishCartReady();
        }
      }
    }, true);
    function needsFullVariantRecovery() {
      if (!state.active) return true;
      if (state.variants.length <= 1) return true;

      var hasColor =
        !!state.colorName ||
        (
          state.config &&
          Array.isArray(state.config.colorValues) &&
          state.config.colorValues.length > 1
        );

      var hasSize =
        !!state.sizeName ||
        (
          state.config &&
          Array.isArray(state.config.sizeValues) &&
          state.config.sizeValues.length > 1
        );

      return !hasColor && !hasSize;
    }

    function requestFullProductConfig() {
      var payload = {
        productId: state.config && state.config.productId
          ? state.config.productId
          : null,
        productHandle: state.config && state.config.productHandle
          ? state.config.productHandle
          : null,
        reason: "full-variant-matrix-required",
        currentVariantCount: state.variants.length
      };

      try {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(
          "customhouse:pitchprint-full-product-config-request",
          { detail: payload }
        ));
      } catch (error) {}

      postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_FULL_PRODUCT_CONFIG_REQUEST", payload);
      postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_VARIANTS_REQUEST", payload);
    }

    function requestLaunchContext() {
      var payload = {
        requestedBy: "pitchprint-template",
        layoutVersion: window.ppLayoutVersion,
        productId: state.config && state.config.productId
          ? state.config.productId
          : null,
        productHandle: state.config && state.config.productHandle
          ? state.config.productHandle
          : null
      };

      try {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(
          "customhouse:pitchprint-launch-context-request",
          { detail: payload }
        ));
      } catch (error) {}

      postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_LAUNCH_CONTEXT_REQUEST", payload);
      postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_CREATOR_CONTEXT_REQUEST", payload);
    }

    function requestProductConfig() {
      try {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(
          "customhouse:pitchprint-order-config-request"
        ));
      } catch (error) {}

      postToHost("CUSTOMHOUSE_PP_ORDER_CONFIG_REQUEST", null);

      if (needsFullVariantRecovery()) {
        requestFullProductConfig();
      }
    }


    function scheduleConfigRetries() {
      [0, 200, 500, 1000, 1800, 3000, 5000].forEach(function (delay) {
        setTimeout(function () {
          if (!state.launchContextResolved) {
            requestLaunchContext();
          }
          if (!state.active || needsFullVariantRecovery()) {
            requestProductConfig();
          }
        }, delay);
      });
    }




    var chClientProxyNavObserver = null;
    var chClientProxyNavFrame = 0;

    function scheduleDesktopProxyNavigatorSync() {
      if (chClientProxyNavFrame) return;
      chClientProxyNavFrame = requestAnimationFrame(function () {
        chClientProxyNavFrame = 0;
        syncDesktopProxyNavigator();
      });
    }

    function ensureDesktopProxyNavigator() {
      var proxy = document.getElementById("ch-client-nav-proxy");
      if (proxy) return proxy;

      proxy = document.createElement("div");
      proxy.id = "ch-client-nav-proxy";
      proxy.setAttribute("role", "navigation");
      proxy.setAttribute("aria-label", "Garment sides");
      var stageParent = document.querySelector('#container [data-module="stage-parent"]');
      (stageParent || document.body).appendChild(proxy);
      return proxy;
    }

    function readablePageLabel(nativeThumb, index) {
      var fallback = ["FRONT", "BACK", "SIDE"];
      var raw = nativeThumb && nativeThumb.lastElementChild
        ? String(nativeThumb.lastElementChild.textContent || "").trim()
        : "";

      if (!raw || raw.indexOf("{") !== -1 || raw.indexOf("}") !== -1) {
        return fallback[index] || ("SIDE " + (index + 1));
      }

      return raw.toUpperCase();
    }

    function syncDesktopProxyNavigator() {
      var container = document.getElementById("container");
      if (!container) return;

      if (window.innerWidth < 900) {
        var mobileProxy = document.getElementById("ch-client-nav-proxy");
        if (mobileProxy) {
          mobileProxy.classList.remove("is-visible");
          mobileProxy.innerHTML = "";
        }
        container.classList.remove("ch-responsive-has-nav");
        return;
      }

      var nativeNav = container.querySelector('[data-module="navigator"]');
      var proxy = ensureDesktopProxyNavigator();

      var preview = container.classList.contains("ch-save-preview-open");
      // V10.42: this public layout must always show first-step side navigation.
      var enabled = true;

      // V10.59: CSS reserves a navigator track alongside/below the real canvas.
      // Keep the same native page delegates in both editing and review modes.
      if (!nativeNav || !enabled) {
        proxy.classList.remove("is-visible");
        proxy.innerHTML = "";
        return;
      }

      var nativeThumbs = Array.prototype.slice.call(
        nativeNav.querySelectorAll(".nav-thumb")
      );

      var focusedIndex = proxy.contains(document.activeElement)
        ? document.activeElement.getAttribute('data-index') : null;
      proxy.innerHTML = "";

      nativeThumbs.forEach(function (nativeThumb, index) {
        var button = document.createElement("button");
        var image = document.createElement("span");
        var label = document.createElement("span");
        var nativeImage = nativeThumb.firstElementChild;

        button.type = "button";
        button.className = "ch-client-proxy-thumb";
        button.setAttribute("data-index", String(index));
        button.setAttribute("aria-label", readablePageLabel(nativeThumb, index));

        if (nativeThumb.classList.contains("selected")) {
          button.classList.add("is-selected");
          button.setAttribute("aria-current", "true");
        }

        image.className = "ch-client-proxy-image";
        label.className = "ch-client-proxy-label";
        label.textContent = readablePageLabel(nativeThumb, index);

        if (nativeImage) {
          var nativeStyle = getComputedStyle(nativeImage);
          image.style.backgroundImage = nativeStyle.backgroundImage || "";
          image.style.backgroundColor = nativeStyle.backgroundColor || "#fff";
        }

        button.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
          event.preventDefault();
          event.stopPropagation();

          /* Proven by live diagnostics:
             nativeThumb.click() changes selected page correctly. */
          nativeThumb.click();

          setTimeout(scheduleDesktopProxyNavigatorSync, 0);
          setTimeout(scheduleDesktopProxyNavigatorSync, 80);
          setTimeout(scheduleDesktopProxyNavigatorSync, 240);
        });

        button.appendChild(image);
        button.appendChild(label);
        proxy.appendChild(button);
      });

      proxy.classList.toggle("is-visible", nativeThumbs.length > 0);
      container.classList.toggle("ch-responsive-has-nav", nativeThumbs.length > 0);
      if (focusedIndex != null && proxy.children[Number(focusedIndex)]) {
        proxy.children[Number(focusedIndex)].focus({ preventScroll: true });
      }

      if (!chClientProxyNavObserver) {
        chClientProxyNavObserver = new MutationObserver(function () {
          scheduleDesktopProxyNavigatorSync();
        });

        chClientProxyNavObserver.observe(nativeNav, {
          attributes: true,
          childList: true,
          subtree: true,
          characterData: true,
          attributeFilter: ["class", "style"]
        });

        chClientProxyNavObserver.observe(container, {
          attributes: true,
          attributeFilter: ["class"]
        });
      }
    }

    function init() {
      var preset = window.CustomHousePublicPitchPrintConfig || window.CustomHouseCreatorPitchPrintConfig || null;

      if (preset) {
        setConfig(preset, window.CustomHousePublicPitchPrintConfig ? "window.CustomHousePublicPitchPrintConfig" : "window.CustomHouseCreatorPitchPrintConfig");
      } else {
        reconcileProductionMethod(state.config);
        render();
      }

      // V10.36: config may arrive before #container is parsed.
      // Re-apply the stored editor UI now that DOMContentLoaded has completed.
      applyEditorUiConfig(state.config || preset);
      syncDesktopProxyNavigator();

      // Ask the host/dashboard for authoritative launch intent before generic
      // product config retries. Creator mode is determined by launch context,
      // not by whether the base Shopify product itself is a Creator product.
      requestLaunchContext();
      requestProductConfig();
      scheduleConfigRetries();
      scheduleCreatorGarmentColorSync();
    }

    window.addEventListener("resize", syncDesktopProxyNavigator);

    if (document.readyState === "loading") {
      document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", init);
    } else {
      init();
    }
  })();






  /* Mobile Upload cleanup keeps the native entry point but removes the large
     upload drawer. The native entry opens the file picker directly. */
  (function () {
    var chMobileNativeUploadObserver = null;
    var chMobileNativeUploadBusy = false;

    function chMobileHideNativeUploadUi() {
      if (chMobileNativeUploadBusy) return;
      if (window.innerWidth >= 768) {
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-ch-mobile-upload-hidden]').forEach(function (node) {
          var original = node.__chMobileUploadOriginal;
          if (!original) return;
          Object.keys(original.styles).forEach(function (key) {
            var value = original.styles[key];
            if (value[0]) node.style.setProperty(key, value[0], value[1]);
            else node.style.removeProperty(key);
          });
          ['aria-hidden', 'tabindex'].forEach(function (key) {
            if (original[key] == null) node.removeAttribute(key);
            else node.setAttribute(key, original[key]);
          });
          node.removeAttribute('data-ch-mobile-upload-hidden');
          delete node.__chMobileUploadOriginal;
        });
        var desktopContainer = document.getElementById('container');
        if (desktopContainer && desktopContainer.classList.contains('ch-mobile-native-upload-active')) {
          desktopContainer.classList.remove('ch-mobile-native-upload-active');
        }
        return;
      }
      chMobileNativeUploadBusy = true;

      try {
        var container = document.getElementById('container');
        var uploadSections = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(
          '#container [data-module="uploads"], #all-modules [data-module="uploads"]'
        ));
        var uploadWasSelected = false;

        uploadSections.forEach(function (section) {
          if (section.classList && section.classList.contains('selected')) {
            uploadWasSelected = true;
          }

          hideMobileUploadNode(section, true);
        });

        if (container) {
          var otherSelectedPanel = document.querySelector(
            '#container [data-module="panels"] > section.selected:not([data-module="uploads"])'
          );
          container.classList.toggle(
            'ch-mobile-native-upload-active',
            !!uploadWasSelected && !otherSelectedPanel
          );
        }
      } finally {
        chMobileNativeUploadBusy = false;
      }
    }

    function hideMobileUploadNode(node, section) {
      var styles = { display: 'none', visibility: 'hidden', 'pointer-events': 'none' };
      if (section) styles.opacity = '0';
      if (!node.__chMobileUploadOriginal) {
        var original = { styles: {}, 'aria-hidden': node.getAttribute('aria-hidden'), tabindex: node.getAttribute('tabindex') };
        Object.keys(styles).forEach(function (key) {
          original.styles[key] = [node.style.getPropertyValue(key), node.style.getPropertyPriority(key)];
        });
        node.__chMobileUploadOriginal = original;
        node.setAttribute('data-ch-mobile-upload-hidden', '');
      }
      Object.keys(styles).forEach(function (key) {
        if (node.style.getPropertyValue(key) !== styles[key] || node.style.getPropertyPriority(key) !== 'important') {
          node.style.setProperty(key, styles[key], 'important');
        }
      });
      if (node.getAttribute('aria-hidden') !== 'true') node.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      if (!section && node.getAttribute('tabindex') !== '-1') node.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1');
    }

    window.chMobileHideNativeUploadUi = chMobileHideNativeUploadUi;
    window.addEventListener('resize', chMobileHideNativeUploadUi);

    function chStartMobileNativeUploadCleanup() {
      chMobileHideNativeUploadUi();

      if (!chMobileNativeUploadObserver) {
        chMobileNativeUploadObserver = new MutationObserver(function () {
          chMobileHideNativeUploadUi();
        });

        chMobileNativeUploadObserver.observe(document.documentElement, {
          childList: true,
          subtree: true,
          attributes: true,
          attributeFilter: ['class', 'style']
        });
      }
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', chStartMobileNativeUploadCleanup);
    } else {
      chStartMobileNativeUploadCleanup();
    }
  })();
</script>
