</style>

<script>
(function () {
  var frame = 0;
  var mountedContainer = null;
  var lastStageSize = '';
  var mobileUploadSession = null;
  var toolTrigger = null;
  var mobileImageFilterBackend = null;

  function setPopoverProperty(node, name, value) {
    var pixels = Math.round(value) + 'px';
    if (node.style.getPropertyValue(name) !== pixels) node.style.setProperty(name, pixels);
  }

  function syncMobileImageFilterPanels(container) {
    var viewport = window.visualViewport;
    var width = viewport ? viewport.width : window.innerWidth;
    var height = viewport ? viewport.height : window.innerHeight;
    var top = viewport ? viewport.offsetTop : 0;
    var left = viewport ? viewport.offsetLeft : 0;
    var menus = container.querySelector('[data-module="menus"]');
    var toolbar = container.querySelector('[data-module="context"]');
    var lower = Math.max(top + 8, menus ? menus.getBoundingClientRect().bottom + 8 : top + 8);
    var upper = top + height - 8;
    if (visibleControl(toolbar) && toolbar.getBoundingClientRect().top > lower + 48) {
      upper = Math.min(upper, toolbar.getBoundingClientRect().top - 8);
    }
    container.querySelectorAll('[data-module="sidebar"]').forEach(function (sidebar) {
      var active = width < 768 && !!sidebar.querySelector('[data-module="image-editor"]');
      sidebar.classList.toggle('ch-mobile-image-filter-panel', active);
      if (!active) return;
      var panelHeight = Math.min(240, height * .32, Math.max(0, upper - lower));
      setPopoverProperty(sidebar, '--ch-image-filter-height', panelHeight);
      setPopoverProperty(sidebar, '--ch-image-filter-top', upper - panelHeight);
      setPopoverProperty(sidebar, '--ch-image-filter-left', left + 8);
      setPopoverProperty(sidebar, '--ch-image-filter-width', Math.max(0, width - 16));
    });
  }

  function syncToolPopovers() {
    var container = mountedContainer;
    if (!container) return;
    var toolbar = container.querySelector('[data-module="context"]');
    var menus = container.querySelector('[data-module="menus"]');
    var viewport = window.visualViewport;
    var left = viewport ? viewport.offsetLeft : 0;
    var top = viewport ? viewport.offsetTop : 0;
    var width = viewport ? viewport.width : window.innerWidth;
    var height = viewport ? viewport.height : window.innerHeight;
    var phoneLayout = width < 768;
    var creatorPhoneLayout = phoneLayout && container.classList.contains('ch-flow-creator');
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-module="popup"]').forEach(function (popup) {
      var isDirectLivePopup = popup.parentElement && popup.parentElement.id === 'all-modules';
      if (popup.closest('#all-modules') && !(phoneLayout && isDirectLivePopup)) {
        if (popup.classList.contains('ch-tool-popover')) popup.classList.remove('ch-tool-popover');
        popup.classList.remove('ch-image-editor-popover');
        return;
      }
      var module = popup.querySelector('[data-module="fonts"], [data-module="more-text"], [data-module="size"], [data-module="opacity"], [data-module="stroke-width"], [data-module="transform"], [data-module="alignment"], [data-module="depth"], [data-module="colors"], [data-module="image-editor"]');
      popup.classList.toggle('ch-mobile-adjustment-popover', phoneLayout && !!module && ['more-text', 'size', 'opacity', 'stroke-width', 'transform', 'alignment', 'depth'].indexOf(module.dataset.module) !== -1);
      if (!module || container.classList.contains('ch-save-preview-open')) {
        if (popup.classList.contains('ch-tool-popover')) popup.classList.remove('ch-tool-popover');
        popup.classList.remove('ch-image-editor-popover');
        return;
      }
      if (module.dataset.module === 'image-editor') bindMobileImageEditorTapGuard(module);
      if (!popup.getClientRects().length || getComputedStyle(popup).visibility === 'hidden') return;
      var anchor = toolTrigger && toolTrigger.isConnected ? toolTrigger : toolbar;
      if (!anchor || !anchor.getClientRects().length) return;
      var rect = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
      var onTop = !!anchor.closest('[data-module="menus"]');
      var toolbarRect = toolbar && toolbar.getClientRects().length ? toolbar.getBoundingClientRect() : rect;
      var tabs = container.querySelector('[data-module="tabs"]');
      var tabsRect = tabs && tabs.getClientRects().length ? tabs.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
      var lower = Math.max(top + 8, menus ? menus.getBoundingClientRect().bottom + 8 : top + 8);
      var upper;
      if (phoneLayout && !onTop && toolbarRect.top < height * .45) {
        lower = Math.max(lower, toolbarRect.bottom + 8);
        upper = Math.min(top + height - 8, tabsRect ? tabsRect.top - 8 : top + height - 8);
      } else {
        upper = onTop ? top + height - 8 : Math.min(top + height - 8, toolbarRect.top - 8);
      }
      if (creatorPhoneLayout && !onTop) {
        var navigator = document.getElementById('ch-client-nav-proxy');
        if (navigator && navigator.classList.contains('is-visible') && navigator.getClientRects().length) {
          upper = Math.min(upper, navigator.getBoundingClientRect().top - 8);
        }
      }
      var available = Math.min(module.dataset.module === 'fonts' ? 420 : 560, Math.max(0, upper - lower));
      if (phoneLayout && module.dataset.module === 'image-editor') available = Math.min(available, 240, height * .32);
      var desiredWidth = module.dataset.module === 'image-editor' ? 600 : 300;
      var popupWidth = Math.min(desiredWidth, width - 16);
      setPopoverProperty(popup, '--ch-popover-width', popupWidth);
      setPopoverProperty(popup, '--ch-popover-height', available);
      setPopoverProperty(popup, '--ch-popover-left', Math.max(left + 8, Math.min(rect.left + rect.width / 2 - popupWidth / 2, left + width - popupWidth - 8)));
      if (!popup.classList.contains('ch-tool-popover')) popup.classList.add('ch-tool-popover');
      popup.classList.toggle('ch-image-editor-popover', module.dataset.module === 'image-editor');
      var popupHeight = popup.getBoundingClientRect().height;
      var popupTop = onTop
        ? lower
        : (creatorPhoneLayout
          ? Math.max(lower, upper - popupHeight)
          : phoneLayout
          ? lower + Math.max(0, (upper - lower - popupHeight) / 2)
          : Math.max(lower, upper - popupHeight));
      setPopoverProperty(popup, '--ch-popover-top', popupTop);
    });
  }

  function text(node, value) {
    if (node && node.textContent !== value) node.textContent = value;
  }

  function schedule() {
    if (frame) return;
    frame = requestAnimationFrame(function () { frame = 0; sync(); });
  }

  function prepareReview(panel) {
    if (document.getElementById('ch-order-scroll')) return;
    var scroll = document.createElement('div');
    scroll.id = 'ch-order-scroll';
    scroll.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');
    scroll.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Design and order details');
    var footer = document.createElement('div');
    footer.id = 'ch-order-footer';
    while (panel.firstChild) scroll.appendChild(panel.firstChild);
    panel.appendChild(scroll);
    panel.appendChild(footer);
    // Move existing nodes, preserving their validation and event listeners.
    ['ch-order-error', 'ch-dimension-error', 'ch-example-text-error', 'ch-continue-cart', 'ch-order-hint'].forEach(function (id) {
      var node = document.getElementById(id);
      if (node) footer.appendChild(node);
    });
    scroll.querySelectorAll('#ch-order-content > .ch-order-section').forEach(function (section, index) {
      var label = section.querySelector('.ch-order-label');
      if (!label) return;
      if (!section.id) section.id = 'ch-review-section';
      var trigger = document.createElement('button');
      trigger.type = 'button';
      trigger.className = 'ch-accordion-trigger';
      trigger.id = section.id + '-trigger';
      var summary = document.createElement('span');
      summary.className = 'ch-accordion-summary';
      var chevron = document.createElement('span');
      chevron.className = 'ch-accordion-chevron';
      chevron.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      trigger.appendChild(label);
      trigger.appendChild(summary);
      trigger.appendChild(chevron);
      var body = document.createElement('div');
      body.className = 'ch-accordion-body';
      body.id = section.id + '-body';
      body.setAttribute('role', 'region');
      body.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', trigger.id);
      while (section.firstChild) body.appendChild(section.firstChild);
      section.appendChild(trigger);
      section.appendChild(body);
      trigger.setAttribute('aria-controls', body.id);
      section.__chExpanded = false;
      trigger.addEventListener('click', function () {
        section.__chExpanded = !section.__chExpanded;
        syncAccordions();
      });
    });
  }

  function syncAccordions() {
    var container = document.getElementById('container');
    if (!container) return;
    var mobilePublic = window.innerWidth <= 767 && container.dataset.chFlowMode === 'PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE';
    var values = {
      'ch-production-section': 'ch-review-method',
      'ch-color-section': 'ch-review-color',
      'ch-size-section': 'ch-review-sizes',
      'ch-review-section': 'ch-order-total-price'
    };
    container.querySelectorAll('#ch-order-content > .ch-order-section').forEach(function (section) {
      var trigger = section.querySelector('.ch-accordion-trigger');
      var body = section.querySelector('.ch-accordion-body');
      if (!trigger || !body) return;
      var target = document.getElementById(values[section.id]);
      var summary = target ? target.textContent.trim() : '';
      if (section.id === 'ch-legal-section') {
        var copyright = document.getElementById('ch-copyright-confirm');
        var nonReturn = document.getElementById('ch-nonreturn-confirm');
        summary = (Number(!!(copyright && copyright.checked)) + Number(!!(nonReturn && nonReturn.checked))) + ' of 2 complete';
      }
      if (!summary || summary === '\u2014') summary = 'Choose';
      text(trigger.querySelector('.ch-accordion-summary'), summary);
      if (body.querySelector('.ch-order-error.ch-show')) section.__chExpanded = true;
      var expanded = !mobilePublic || section.__chExpanded;
      if (body.hidden === expanded) body.hidden = !expanded;
      if (trigger.disabled === mobilePublic) trigger.disabled = !mobilePublic;
      if (trigger.getAttribute('aria-expanded') !== String(expanded)) trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(expanded));
    });
  }

  function stopMobileUpload() {
    var session = mobileUploadSession;
    if (!session) return;
    mobileUploadSession = null;
    session.observer.disconnect();
    clearTimeout(session.timeout);
    document.removeEventListener('change', session.fileEvent, true);
    document.removeEventListener('cancel', session.fileEvent, true);
  }

  function mobileUploadIsEditing(session) {
    return session.container.isConnected && window.innerWidth <= 767 &&
      !session.container.classList.contains('ch-save-preview-open') &&
      !session.container.querySelector('[data-module="preview"].shown');
  }

  function uploadCandidate(session, thumb, create) {
    var id = thumb.getAttribute('data-id');
    if (session.existingNodes.has(thumb) || (id && session.existingIds.has(id))) return null;
    var candidate = session.nodes.get(thumb) || (id && session.ids.get(id));
    if (!candidate && create) {
      candidate = { added: false };
      session.pending.add(candidate);
      session.remainingFiles = Math.max(0, session.remainingFiles - 1);
    }
    if (candidate) {
      candidate.thumb = thumb;
      session.nodes.set(thumb, candidate);
      if (id) session.ids.set(id, candidate);
    }
    return candidate;
  }

  function scanMobileUploads(session) {
    if (mobileUploadSession !== session) return;
    if (!mobileUploadIsEditing(session)) { stopMobileUpload(); return; }
    session.section.querySelectorAll('.thumb[data-cmd="image-click"]').forEach(function (thumb) {
      if (mobileUploadSession !== session) return;
      var pending = thumb.classList.contains('not-selectable') || !!thumb.querySelector('.circle-progress');
      var candidate = uploadCandidate(session, thumb, pending);
      var id = thumb.getAttribute('data-id');
      // The native remove control replaces upload progress only on completion.
      if (pending || !id || !thumb.querySelector('[data-cmd="remove"]')) return;
      if (thumb.hidden || thumb.style.display === 'none' || thumb.getAttribute('aria-hidden') === 'true') return;
      if (!candidate && session.remainingFiles > 0) candidate = uploadCandidate(session, thumb, true);
      if (!candidate) {
        session.existingNodes.add(thumb);
        session.existingIds.add(id);
        return;
      }
      if (candidate.added) return;
      // Mark before delegation: native handlers can synchronously rerender the list.
      candidate.added = true;
      session.pending.delete(candidate);
      var add = thumb.querySelector('[data-cmd="add"]');
      (add || thumb).click();
    });
    session.pending.forEach(function (candidate) {
      if (!session.section.contains(candidate.thumb)) session.pending.delete(candidate);
    });
    if (!session.awaitingFiles && !session.remainingFiles && !session.pending.size) stopMobileUpload();
  }

  function armMobileUpload(container) {
    if (window.innerWidth > 767) return;
    var section = container.querySelector('[data-module="uploads"]');
    if (!section) return;
    var session = mobileUploadSession;
    if (session && (session.container !== container || session.section !== section)) {
      stopMobileUpload();
      session = null;
    }
    if (!session) {
      session = {
        container: container, section: section, existingIds: new Set(), existingNodes: new WeakSet(),
        ids: new Map(), nodes: new WeakMap(), pending: new Set(), remainingFiles: 0, awaitingFiles: false
      };
      section.querySelectorAll('.thumb[data-cmd="image-click"]').forEach(function (thumb) {
        session.existingNodes.add(thumb);
        var id = thumb.getAttribute('data-id');
        if (id) session.existingIds.add(id);
      });
      session.observer = new MutationObserver(function (records) {
        // Also capture uploads that start and finish within one native render turn.
        records.forEach(function (record) {
          var thumb = record.target.closest && record.target.closest('.thumb[data-cmd="image-click"]');
          var wasPending = record.attributeName === 'class' && /\bnot-selectable\b/.test(record.oldValue || '');
          if (record.type === 'childList') {
            wasPending = Array.prototype.some.call(record.removedNodes, function (node) {
              return node.nodeType === 1 && node.classList.contains('circle-progress');
            });
          }
          if (thumb && wasPending) uploadCandidate(session, thumb, true);
        });
        scanMobileUploads(session);
      });
      session.fileEvent = function (event) {
        if (!session.awaitingFiles || !event.target || event.target.type !== 'file') return;
        // Capture selection before native change handlers render a fast upload.
        scanMobileUploads(session);
        session.awaitingFiles = false;
        session.remainingFiles = event.type === 'change' && event.target.files ? event.target.files.length : 0;
        if (!session.remainingFiles && !session.pending.size) stopMobileUpload();
      };
      mobileUploadSession = session;
      session.observer.observe(section, {
        subtree: true, childList: true, attributes: true, attributeOldValue: true,
        attributeFilter: ['class', 'data-id', 'style', 'hidden', 'aria-hidden']
      });
      document.addEventListener('change', session.fileEvent, true);
      document.addEventListener('cancel', session.fileEvent, true);
    }
    // Keep earlier pending uploads when another file picker is opened.
    session.awaitingFiles = true;
    session.remainingFiles = 0;
    clearTimeout(session.timeout);
    session.timeout = setTimeout(stopMobileUpload, 10 * 60 * 1000);
  }

  function isNativeUploadTab(trigger) {
    if (!trigger || trigger.closest('[data-module="image-source-picker"]')) return false;
    var label = String(trigger.textContent || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    var hasUploadIcon = !!trigger.querySelector(
      '.icon-upload, .icon-upload-cloud, [class*="icon-upload"]'
    );
    return hasUploadIcon || label.indexOf('LADDA UPP') !== -1 || label.indexOf('UPLOAD') !== -1;
  }

  function syncMobileUploadPlacement(container) {
    var toolbar = container.querySelector('[data-module="menus"]');
    var nativeUploadButton = container.querySelector('[data-module="uploads"] [data-cmd="upload"]');
    if (toolbar && nativeUploadButton && !toolbar.querySelector('.ch-mobile-upload-shortcut')) {
      var button = document.createElement('button');
      button.type = 'button';
      button.className = 'btn ch-main-upload-shortcut ch-mobile-upload-shortcut';
      button.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Upload Image');
      button.title = 'Upload Image';
      var icon = document.createElement('i');
      icon.className = 'icon-upload-cloud';
      icon.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      button.appendChild(icon);
      button.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
        armMobileUpload(container);
        window.chPitchPrintOpenUpload(event);
      });
      var textButton = toolbar.querySelector('[data-cmd="text"]');
      toolbar.insertBefore(button, textButton ? textButton.nextSibling : toolbar.querySelector('.spacer'));
    }
    if (toolbar && !toolbar.querySelector('.ch-mobile-delete-shortcut')) {
      var deleteButton = document.createElement('button');
      deleteButton.type = 'button';
      deleteButton.className = 'btn ch-mobile-delete-shortcut';
      deleteButton.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Delete selected item');
      deleteButton.title = 'Delete selected item';
      deleteButton.hidden = true;
      var deleteIcon = document.createElement('i');
      deleteIcon.className = 'icon-trash-2';
      deleteIcon.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      deleteButton.appendChild(deleteIcon);
      deleteButton.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
        if (window.innerWidth > 767) return;
        var context = container.querySelector('[data-module="context"]');
        var deleteTargets = context ? Array.prototype.slice.call(context.querySelectorAll(':scope > [data-cmd="delete"]')) : [];
        var nativeDeleteButton = deleteTargets.find(function (target) {
          return target.classList.contains('wide-menus');
        }) || deleteTargets[0];
        if (!nativeDeleteButton) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        nativeDeleteButton.click();
        schedule();
      });
      var uploadButton = toolbar.querySelector('.ch-mobile-upload-shortcut');
      toolbar.insertBefore(deleteButton, uploadButton ? uploadButton.nextSibling : toolbar.querySelector('.spacer'));
    }
    var context = container.querySelector('[data-module="context"]');
    var mobileDeleteButton = toolbar && toolbar.querySelector('.ch-mobile-delete-shortcut');
    if (mobileDeleteButton) {
      var deleteTargets = context ? Array.prototype.slice.call(context.querySelectorAll(':scope > [data-cmd="delete"]')) : [];
      var nativeDeleteButton = deleteTargets.find(function (target) {
        return target.classList.contains('wide-menus');
      }) || deleteTargets[0];
      var canDelete = window.innerWidth <= 767 &&
        !container.classList.contains('ch-save-preview-open') &&
        context && context.classList.contains('shown') &&
        nativeDeleteButton && !nativeDeleteButton.classList.contains('disabled') &&
        nativeDeleteButton.getAttribute('aria-disabled') !== 'true';
      mobileDeleteButton.hidden = !canDelete;
      mobileDeleteButton.disabled = !canDelete;
    }

    container.querySelectorAll('[data-module="tabs"] button').forEach(function (tab) {
      tab.toggleAttribute('data-ch-mobile-upload-tab', isNativeUploadTab(tab));
    });
  }

  function syncNativeMobileUpload(container) {
    var triggers = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(
      '#container [data-module="image-source-picker"] [data-cmd="uploads"], ' +
      '#all-modules [data-module="image-source-picker"] [data-cmd="uploads"], ' +
      '#container [data-module="tabs"] button'
    )).filter(function (trigger) {
      if (trigger.closest('[data-module="image-source-picker"]')) return true;
      var label = String(trigger.textContent || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      var hasUploadIcon = !!trigger.querySelector(
        '.icon-upload, .icon-upload-cloud, [class*="icon-upload"]'
      );
      return hasUploadIcon || label.indexOf('LADDA UPP') !== -1 || label.indexOf('UPLOAD') !== -1;
    });

    triggers.forEach(function (trigger) {
      if (trigger.__chNativeMobileUploadBound) return;
      trigger.__chNativeMobileUploadBound = true;
      trigger.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
        if (window.innerWidth > 767) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        event.stopImmediatePropagation();
        armMobileUpload(container);
        window.chPitchPrintOpenUpload(event);
      }, true);
    });
  }

  function visibleControl(node) {
    if (!node || node.hidden || node.getAttribute('aria-hidden') === 'true') return false;
    if (!node.getClientRects || !node.getClientRects().length) return false;
    var style = window.getComputedStyle(node);
    return style.display !== 'none' && style.visibility !== 'hidden';
  }

  function mobileFilterImages() {
    var model = window.dModel || (window.designer && window.designer.model);
    var runtime = model && model.runtime;
    if (!runtime || !runtime.design || !runtime.act || !runtime.page) return [];
    var page = runtime.design.cPage;
    if (page && (page._bgMode || runtime.act.forcePhotoToBG) && page.canvas.backgroundImage) {
      return [page.canvas.backgroundImage];
    }
    var images = (runtime.page.cItems || []).filter(function (image) { return image.type === 'image'; });
    return images.length ? images : runtime.act.photoTarget ? [runtime.act.photoTarget] : [];
  }

  function prepareMobileImageFilter(image) {
    var engine = window.fabric;
    if (!engine || !engine.Canvas2dFilterBackend || !image || typeof image.applyFilters !== 'function') return;
    if (image.__chMobileFilterTransaction) return;
    var nativeApply = image.applyFilters;
    var ownApply = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(image, 'applyFilters');
    var previousFilters = (image.filters || []).map(function (filter) {
      var Filter = filter && engine.Image.filters[filter.type];
      return Filter && typeof filter.toObject === 'function' ? new Filter(filter.toObject()) : filter;
    });
    var previous = {};
    ['_element', '_originalElement', '_filteredEl', '_filterScalingX', '_filterScalingY', '_lastScaleX', '_lastScaleY', 'dirty'].forEach(function (key) {
      previous[key] = image[key];
    });
    function release() {
      if (image.applyFilters === apply) {
        if (ownApply) image.applyFilters = nativeApply;
        else delete image.applyFilters;
      }
      delete image.__chMobileFilterTransaction;
    }
    var apply = function () {
      var backend = engine.filterBackend;
      try {
        // PitchPrint's synchronous Fabric 3.4 filter path commits _element before
        // processing. Use a fresh CPU output so a failure cannot blank the last image.
        if (!mobileImageFilterBackend) mobileImageFilterBackend = new engine.Canvas2dFilterBackend();
        engine.filterBackend = mobileImageFilterBackend;
        image._element = image._originalElement;
        image._filteredEl = null;
        var result = nativeApply.apply(image, arguments);
        if (!image._element || !image._element.width || !image._element.height) {
          throw new Error('Mobile image filter produced an empty image');
        }
        release.applied = true;
        return result;
      } catch (error) {
        image.filters = previousFilters;
        Object.keys(previous).forEach(function (key) { image[key] = previous[key]; });
        if (image.canvas) image.canvas.requestRenderAll();
        if (window.app && window.app.events) {
          window.app.events.fire('show-notify', { text: 'This filter could not be applied. Your image has been preserved.', icon: 'icon-alert-triangle' });
        }
        document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('customhouse:mobile-image-filter-error', { detail: { message: String(error.message || error) } }));
        return image;
      } finally {
        engine.filterBackend = backend;
        release();
      }
    };
    image.__chMobileFilterTransaction = true;
    image.applyFilters = apply;
    return release;
  }

  function bindMobileImageEditorTapGuard(editor) {
    if (!editor || editor.__chMobileImageEditorTapGuardBound) return;
    editor.__chMobileImageEditorTapGuardBound = true;
    var start = window.PointerEvent ? 'pointerdown' : 'touchstart';
    editor.addEventListener(start, function (event) {
      if (window.innerWidth <= 767) event.stopPropagation();
    });
    var releases = [];
    function releaseUnused() {
      releases.forEach(function (release) { if (release) release(); });
      releases = [];
    }
    ['click', 'input'].forEach(function (type) {
      editor.addEventListener(type, function (event) {
        if (window.innerWidth > 767) return;
        releaseUnused();
        var target = event.target;
        var filter = type === 'input' ? target.matches('input[type="range"]') : target.closest('[data-cmd="preset"], [data-cmd="reset"]');
        if (filter) releases = mobileFilterImages().map(prepareMobileImageFilter);
      }, true);
      editor.addEventListener(type, function (event) {
        if (window.innerWidth <= 767) {
          event.stopPropagation();
          var preset = type === 'click' && event.target.closest('[data-cmd="preset"]');
          if (preset && releases.length && releases.every(function (release) { return release && release.applied; })) {
            var sidebar = editor.closest('[data-module="sidebar"]');
            var popup = editor.closest('[data-module="popup"]');
            if (window.app && window.app.events) {
              if (sidebar) window.app.events.fire('hide-sidebar');
              else if (popup) window.app.events.fire('hide-popup');
            }
          }
        }
      });
    });
  }

  function contextDeleteButton(container) {
    var context = container.querySelector('[data-module="context"]');
    if (!context || !context.classList.contains('shown')) return null;
    var buttons = Array.prototype.slice.call(context.querySelectorAll(':scope > [data-cmd="delete"]'))
      .filter(visibleControl);
    return buttons.find(function (button) {
      return button.classList.contains('wide-menus');
    }) || buttons[0] || null;
  }

  function commandControlAvailable(node) {
    return !!(node && node.isConnected && !node.disabled && node.getAttribute('aria-disabled') !== 'true');
  }

  function resetMobileDismissedToolPopovers() {
    var styleNames = [
      'display', 'visibility', 'pointer-events', 'width', 'height', 'min-width',
      'min-height', 'max-width', 'max-height', 'margin', 'padding', 'border',
      'background', 'box-shadow', 'overflow'
    ];
    document.querySelectorAll('.ch-mobile-font-dismissed, .ch-mobile-color-dismissed').forEach(function (node) {
      node.classList.remove('ch-mobile-font-dismissed', 'ch-mobile-color-dismissed');
      if (node.dataset && node.dataset.chMobilePickerDismissed === '1') {
        styleNames.forEach(function (name) { node.style.removeProperty(name); });
        delete node.dataset.chMobilePickerDismissed;
      }
    });
  }

  function nativeImageEditButton(context) {
    if (!context || !context.classList.contains('shown')) return null;
    var buttons = Array.prototype.slice.call(context.querySelectorAll(':scope > [data-cmd="edit-image"]:not(.ch-image-edit-shortcut)'))
      .filter(function (button) {
        return commandControlAvailable(button);
      });
    return buttons[0] || null;
  }

  function nativePhotoFilterButton() {
    var buttons = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(
      '#all-modules [data-module="module-photo-3"] [data-cmd="filters"], ' +
      '#all-modules [data-module="module-photo-2"] [data-cmd="filters"], ' +
      '#container [data-module="module-photo-3"] [data-cmd="filters"], ' +
      '#container [data-module="module-photo-2"] [data-cmd="filters"]'
    )).filter(commandControlAvailable);
    return buttons.find(function (button) {
      return visibleControl(button) && !!button.closest('[data-module="module-photo-3"].selected, [data-module="module-photo-2"].selected');
    });
  }

  function bindMobileContextScroll(context) {
    if (!context || context.__chMobileContextScrollBound) return;
    context.__chMobileContextScrollBound = true;
    var startX = 0;
    var startLeft = 0;
    var tracking = false;
    context.addEventListener('touchstart', function (event) {
      if (window.innerWidth > 767 || event.touches.length !== 1) return;
      tracking = context.scrollWidth > context.clientWidth + 2;
      startX = event.touches[0].clientX;
      startLeft = context.scrollLeft;
    }, { passive: true });
    context.addEventListener('touchmove', function (event) {
      if (!tracking || window.innerWidth > 767 || event.touches.length !== 1) return;
      var deltaX = event.touches[0].clientX - startX;
      if (Math.abs(deltaX) < 4) return;
      context.scrollLeft = startLeft - deltaX;
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
    }, { passive: false });
    context.addEventListener('touchend', function () { tracking = false; }, { passive: true });
    context.addEventListener('touchcancel', function () { tracking = false; }, { passive: true });
  }

  function ensureImageEditShortcut(context) {
    var shortcut = context.querySelector(':scope > .ch-image-edit-shortcut');
    if (!shortcut) {
      shortcut = document.createElement('button');
      shortcut.type = 'button';
      shortcut.className = 'btn ch-image-edit-shortcut';
      shortcut.setAttribute('data-balloon-pos', 'down');
      shortcut.setAttribute('data-balloon', 'Edit image');
      shortcut.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Edit image');
      shortcut.title = 'Edit image';
      var icon = document.createElement('i');
      icon.className = 'icon-p-wand';
      icon.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      shortcut.appendChild(icon);
      context.insertBefore(shortcut, context.firstChild);
    }
    shortcut.removeAttribute('data-cmd');
    shortcut.setAttribute('data-ch-proxy-cmd', 'edit-image');
    if (!shortcut.__chImageEditShortcutBound) {
      shortcut.__chImageEditShortcutBound = true;
      shortcut.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
        if (window.innerWidth > 767) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        event.stopImmediatePropagation();
        var filterButton = nativePhotoFilterButton();
        var nativeEdit = nativeImageEditButton(context);
        var target = nativeEdit || filterButton;
        if (!target) {
          shortcut.toggleAttribute('hidden', true);
          shortcut.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
          return;
        }
        resetMobileDismissedToolPopovers();
        toolTrigger = shortcut;
        if (typeof target.click === 'function') target.click();
        else {
          target.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click', {
            bubbles: true,
            cancelable: true,
            view: window
          }));
        }
        syncToolPopovers();
      }, true);
    }
    return shortcut;
  }

  function syncImageEditShortcut(container) {
    var context = container.querySelector('[data-module="context"]');
    if (!context) return;
    bindMobileContextScroll(context);

    var shortcut = context.querySelector(':scope > .ch-image-edit-shortcut');
    var shortcutWasVisible = visibleControl(shortcut);
    var visibleControls = context.classList.contains('shown')
      ? Array.prototype.filter.call(context.children, visibleControl)
      : [];
    var nativeEdit = nativeImageEditButton(context);
    var filterButton = nativePhotoFilterButton();
    var textMode = context.classList.contains('ch-context-text-mode');
    var imageMode = !textMode && visibleControls.some(function (child) {
      if (child.classList && child.classList.contains('ch-image-edit-shortcut')) return false;
      return child.matches && child.matches(
        '[data-cmd="scale-less"], [data-cmd="scale-more"], [data-cmd="position-background-image"], ' +
        '[data-cmd="undock-background-image"], [data-cmd="edit-image"], [data-cmd="delete"], ' +
        '[data-cmd="clone"], [data-cmd="alignment"], [data-cmd="depth"], [data-cmd="opacity"], ' +
        '[data-cmd="transform"]'
      );
    });
    var shouldShow = window.innerWidth <= 767 && context.classList.contains('shown') && (filterButton || nativeEdit) && imageMode;

    if (!shortcut && shouldShow) shortcut = ensureImageEditShortcut(context);
    if (shortcut) {
      shortcut.hidden = false;
      shortcut.toggleAttribute('hidden', !shouldShow);
      if (shouldShow) {
        shortcut.style.setProperty('display', 'inline-flex', 'important');
        shortcut.style.setProperty('visibility', 'visible', 'important');
        shortcut.style.setProperty('pointer-events', 'auto', 'important');
        shortcut.style.setProperty('opacity', '1', 'important');
        if (window.innerWidth <= 767 && !shortcutWasVisible) {
          requestAnimationFrame(function () {
            if (context.isConnected) context.scrollLeft = 0;
          });
        }
      } else {
        shortcut.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
        shortcut.style.setProperty('visibility', 'hidden', 'important');
        shortcut.style.setProperty('pointer-events', 'none', 'important');
      }
    }
  }

  function syncTopDeleteButton(container) {
    var toolbar = container.querySelector('[data-module="menus"]');
    if (!toolbar) return;
    var target = contextDeleteButton(container);
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(toolbar.querySelectorAll(':scope > [data-cmd="delete"]'), function (button) {
      var canDelete = !!target && !container.classList.contains('ch-save-preview-open');
      button.classList.toggle('disabled', !canDelete);
      button.toggleAttribute('disabled', !canDelete);
      button.setAttribute('aria-disabled', canDelete ? 'false' : 'true');
      if (canDelete) {
        button.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
        button.style.opacity = '';
      }
    });
  }

  function syncContextVisibility(container) {
    var context = container.querySelector('[data-module="context"]');
    if (!context) return;
    var visibleControls = context.classList.contains('shown')
      ? Array.prototype.filter.call(context.children, function (child) {
        if (child.hidden || child.getAttribute('aria-hidden') === 'true') return false;
        var style = window.getComputedStyle(child);
        return style.display !== 'none' && style.visibility !== 'hidden';
      })
      : [];
    var textMode = visibleControls.some(function (child) {
      if (child.classList && child.classList.contains('ch-artwork-color-control')) return false;
      return child.matches && child.matches(
        '[data-cmd="fonts"], [data-cmd="colors"], [data-cmd="size"], [data-cmd="bold"], ' +
        '[data-cmd="italic"], [data-cmd="align"], [data-cmd="more-text"], .font-display'
      );
    });
    context.classList.toggle('ch-context-empty', !visibleControls.length);
    context.classList.toggle('ch-context-text-mode', textMode);
  }

  function syncMobileChangeCounters() {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-module="flash"]').forEach(function (flash) {
      var message = flash.querySelector('span');
      var value = message ? String(message.textContent || '').trim() : '';
      var numeric = /^[+\-\u2212]?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?(?:\s*x\s*[+\-\u2212]?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)?\s*(?:%|\u00b0|px|cm|mm|in)?$/i.test(value);
      flash.classList.toggle('ch-mobile-change-counter', numeric && !flash.classList.contains('is-type') && !flash.querySelector('textarea'));
    });
  }

  function sync() {
    bindImagePinchLifecycle();
    syncMobileChangeCounters();
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-module="image-editor"]').forEach(bindMobileImageEditorTapGuard);
    syncToolPopovers();
    if (mobileUploadSession && !mobileUploadIsEditing(mobileUploadSession)) stopMobileUpload();
    var container = document.getElementById('container');
    if (!container) return;
    syncMobileImageFilterPanels(container);
    syncContextVisibility(container);
    syncImageEditShortcut(container);
    syncTopDeleteButton(container);
    var panel = document.getElementById('ch-public-order-config');
    if (!panel) return;
    prepareReview(panel);
    syncMobileUploadPlacement(container);
    syncNativeMobileUpload(container);
    syncAccordions();
    var selectedPanel = container.querySelector('[data-module="panels"] > section.selected:not([data-module="uploads"])');
    container.classList.toggle('ch-responsive-panel-open', !!selectedPanel);
    var color = document.getElementById('ch-review-color');
    if (color) {
      var value = color.textContent.trim();
      var validColor = /^(#[0-9a-f]{3,8}|[a-z]+)$/i.test(value) && CSS.supports('color', value);
      color.classList.toggle('ch-known-color', validColor);
      if (validColor && color.style.getPropertyValue('--ch-fixed-color') !== value) color.style.setProperty('--ch-fixed-color', value);
    }
    if (window.chPitchPrintMountNextStepGuideInToolbar) window.chPitchPrintMountNextStepGuideInToolbar();
  }

  function handleSidebarClick(event) {
    var container = mountedContainer;
    var target = event.target && event.target.closest ? event.target : null;
    if (target && target.closest('[data-module="menus"] [data-cmd="close"], [data-module="menus"] [data-cmd="submit"]')) stopMobileUpload();
    if (!container || !target || container.classList.contains('ch-save-preview-open')) return;
    var tab = target.closest('[data-module="tabs"] [data-cmd="tab"]');
    var collapsed = container.classList.contains('ch-editor-panel-collapsed');
    if (window.innerWidth >= 768 && target.closest('#panels-close')) {
      // Collapse only the shell; retain PitchPrint's selected module and artwork.
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopImmediatePropagation();
      container.classList.add('ch-editor-panel-collapsed');
      var activeTab = container.querySelector('[data-module="tabs"] [data-cmd="tab"].selected');
      if (activeTab) activeTab.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      schedule();
    } else if (tab && collapsed) {
      if (window.innerWidth >= 768 && tab.classList.contains('selected')) {
        // Reopening the active tool must not toggle its native selection off.
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopImmediatePropagation();
      }
      container.classList.remove('ch-editor-panel-collapsed');
      schedule();
    }
  }

  function currentImageInteractionCanvas() {
    var model = window.dModel || (window.designer && window.designer.model) ||
      (window.ppframe && window.ppframe.dModel);
    var page = model && model.runtime && model.runtime.design && model.runtime.design.cPage;
    return page && page.canvas;
  }

  function bindImagePinchLifecycle() {
    var canvas = currentImageInteractionCanvas();
    bindImagePinchResize(canvas);
    if (!canvas || canvas.__chImagePinchBound ||
        typeof canvas.__onTransformGesture !== 'function' ||
        typeof canvas.__gesturesRenderer !== 'function' ||
        typeof canvas._transformObject !== 'function') return;
    canvas.__chImagePinchBound = true;
    var nativeGesture = canvas.__onTransformGesture;
    var nativeTransform = canvas._transformObject;
    var gesture = null;
    var doc = canvas.upperCanvasEl.ownerDocument;

    function preventSafariPagePinch(event) {
      if (rememberImageZoomView(canvas)) {
        // Safari also sends native GestureEvents. Cancel browser-page zoom only
        // here; the existing touch recognizer still owns the artwork transform.
        if (event.cancelable) event.preventDefault();
      }
    }
    ['gesturestart', 'gesturechange', 'gestureend'].forEach(function (name) {
      canvas.upperCanvasEl.addEventListener(name, preventSafariPagePinch, { passive: false });
    });

    function finishImagePinch(event) {
      if (!gesture || (event.type !== 'touchcancel' && event.touches && event.touches.length >= 2)) return;
      var transform = canvas._currentTransform;
      if (transform && transform.target === gesture.target) {
        // Commit once at the two-to-one-finger transition. The remaining finger
        // must not drag using offsets captured before the image was pinched.
        transform.action = 'scale';
        transform.actionPerformed = transform.actionPerformed || gesture.changed;
        canvas._finalizeCurrentTransform(event);
        canvas._currentTransform = null;
      }
      canvas.__gesturesParams = null;
      gesture = null;
      doc.removeEventListener('touchend', finishImagePinch, true);
      doc.removeEventListener('touchcancel', finishImagePinch, true);
      if (event.type === 'touchcancel' && !event.touches.length) canvas._onTouchEnd(event);
    }

    canvas.__onTransformGesture = function (event, self) {
      var transform = this._currentTransform;
      var target = transform && transform.target;
      if (event.touches && event.touches.length === 2 && self.gesture === 'gesture') {
        // Empty-stage pinches use PitchPrint's native viewport zoom. Remember
        // that view too, before selection/toolbar changes can trigger a refit.
        rememberImageZoomView(this);
      }
      if (!this.isDrawingMode && event.touches && event.touches.length === 2 &&
          self.gesture === 'gesture' && target && target.type === 'image' &&
          this.getActiveObject() === target && !(this.page && this.page._bgMode)) {
        if (!gesture) {
          gesture = { target: target, changed: false };
          doc.addEventListener('touchend', finishImagePinch, true);
          doc.addEventListener('touchcancel', finishImagePinch, true);
        }
        gesture.changed = gesture.changed || self.scale !== 1 || self.rotation !== 0;
        this.__gesturesParams = { e: event, self: self, target: target };
        // Fabric owns this image transform. Do not also emit PitchPrint's
        // touch:gesture canvas-zoom path, which scales the same pixels twice.
        this.__gesturesRenderer();
        return;
      }
      return nativeGesture.apply(this, arguments);
    };

    canvas._transformObject = function (event) {
      // The recognizer and Fabric's single-pointer move handler receive the same
      // touchmove. Only the recognizer may write during an image pinch.
      if (gesture && event.touches && event.touches.length >= 2) return;
      return nativeTransform.apply(this, arguments);
    };
  }

  function rememberImageZoomView(canvas) {
    if (!canvas || currentImageInteractionCanvas() !== canvas || canvas.isDrawingMode ||
        (canvas.page && canvas.page._bgMode) ||
        mountedContainer.classList.contains('ch-save-preview-open')) return false;
    var active = canvas.getActiveObject();
    if (active ? active.type !== 'image' : !canvas.getObjects().some(function (object) {
      return object.type === 'image';
    })) return false;
    canvas.__chImagePinchWidth = canvas.upperCanvasEl.ownerDocument.documentElement.clientWidth;
    return true;
  }

  function bindImagePinchResize(canvas) {
    var page = canvas && canvas.page;
    var bus = (window.app && window.app.events) ||
      (window.ppframe && window.ppframe.eventBus);
    if (!page || typeof page.center !== 'function' || !bus || typeof bus.fire !== 'function') return;
    if (!mountedContainer.__chImageZoomControlsBound) {
      mountedContainer.__chImageZoomControlsBound = true;
      var rememberSliderView = function (event) {
        if (event.target.matches && event.target.matches('[data-cmd="zoom"]')) {
          // Capture both native range events before PitchPrint's zoom module.
          // This also covers slider zoom while the artwork is deselected.
          rememberImageZoomView(currentImageInteractionCanvas());
        }
      };
      mountedContainer.addEventListener('input', rememberSliderView, true);
      mountedContainer.addEventListener('change', rememberSliderView, true);
    }
    if (!bus.__chPinchResizeBound) {
      bus.__chPinchResizeBound = true;
      var nativeFire = bus.fire;
      bus.fire = function (type) {
        var previous = this.__chPinchWindowResize;
        this.__chPinchWindowResize = type === 'window-resized';
        try { return nativeFire.apply(this, arguments); }
        finally { this.__chPinchWindowResize = previous; }
      };
    }
    if (page.__chPinchResizeBound) return;
    page.__chPinchResizeBound = true;
    var nativeCenter = page.center;
    page.center = function () {
      var width = canvas.upperCanvasEl.ownerDocument.documentElement.clientWidth;
      if (bus.__chPinchWindowResize && canvas.__chImagePinchWidth === width &&
          width <= 767 && currentImageInteractionCanvas() === canvas && !this._bgMode &&
          !mountedContainer.classList.contains('ch-save-preview-open') && this.parent &&
          this.parent.parentElement) {
        // Safari's browser bars/viewport can resize after fingers lift. Keep the
        // user's view, while still resizing the backing canvas and the other UI.
        resizeImageCanvasBacking(canvas, this.parent.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect());
        return;
      }
      // Width/orientation changes and explicit Reset/center-page remain native.
      canvas.__chImagePinchWidth = null;
      return nativeCenter.apply(this, arguments);
    };
  }

  function resizeImageCanvasBacking(canvas, rect) {
    if (rect.width > 0 && rect.height > 0 &&
        (canvas.width !== rect.width || canvas.height !== rect.height)) {
      canvas.setDimensions({ width: rect.width, height: rect.height });
      canvas.forEachObject(function (object) { object.setCoords(); });
    }
  }

  function resizeSelectedImageCanvas(rect) {
    var canvas = currentImageInteractionCanvas();
    var page = canvas && canvas.page;
    if (!canvas || typeof canvas.getActiveObject !== 'function' ||
        typeof canvas.setDimensions !== 'function') return false;
    var active = canvas.getActiveObject();
    var editingImage = active ? active.type === 'image' :
      canvas.getObjects().some(function (object) { return object.type === 'image'; });
    if (!editingImage || page._bgMode ||
        mountedContainer.classList.contains('ch-save-preview-open')) return false;

    // Selection only changes the toolbar track. A synthetic window resize calls
    // Page.center(), resetting the user's zoom/pan even though the image is intact.
    // Resize the backing canvas in place; Fabric retains its viewport transform.
    resizeImageCanvasBacking(canvas, rect);
    return true;
  }

  function mount() {
    var container = document.getElementById('container');
    if (!container || mountedContainer === container) return;
    mountedContainer = container;
    sync();
    container.addEventListener('click', handleSidebarClick, true);
    container.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      var headerDelete = event.target.closest && event.target.closest('[data-module="menus"] > [data-cmd="delete"]');
      if (!headerDelete) return;
      var target = contextDeleteButton(container);
      if (!target) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      event.stopImmediatePropagation();
      target.click();
      schedule();
    }, true);
    container.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      var trigger = event.target.closest && event.target.closest('[data-module="context"] > [data-cmd], [data-module="menus"] > [data-cmd]');
      if (trigger) {
        toolTrigger = trigger;
        schedule();
        setTimeout(schedule, 40);
        setTimeout(schedule, 120);
      }
    }, true);
    document.addEventListener('scroll', schedule, true);
    // Native tool popups and numeric feedback can mount outside #container.
    new MutationObserver(function (records) {
      if (records.some(function (record) {
        var target = record.target.nodeType === 1 ? record.target : record.target.parentElement;
        return (target && target.closest('[data-module="popup"], [data-module="flash"]')) ||
          Array.prototype.some.call(record.addedNodes, function (node) {
            return node.nodeType === 1 && (
              node.matches('[data-module="popup"], [data-module="flash"]') ||
              node.querySelector('[data-module="popup"], [data-module="flash"]')
            );
          });
      })) schedule();
    }).observe(document.body, {subtree: true, childList: true, characterData: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class', 'style']});
    container.addEventListener('input', schedule);
    container.addEventListener('change', schedule);
    container.addEventListener('click', schedule);
    // Text summaries reflect existing rendered values; no pricing or payload logic.
    new MutationObserver(function (records) {
      if (records.some(function (record) {
        var node = record.target.nodeType === 1 ? record.target : record.target.parentElement;
        return node && !node.closest('.ch-accordion-summary, #ch-client-nav-proxy, #ch-design-next-step-guide');
      })) schedule();
    }).observe(container, { childList: true, characterData: true, subtree: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-ch-flow-mode', 'class'] });
    if (window.ResizeObserver) {
      new ResizeObserver(function (entries) {
        var rect = entries[0].contentRect;
        var size = Math.round(rect.width) + 'x' + Math.round(rect.height);
        if (size !== lastStageSize) {
          var initialSize = !lastStageSize;
          lastStageSize = size;
          if (!initialSize && resizeSelectedImageCanvas(rect)) return;
          // Ask PitchPrint's existing resize handler to fit its canvas to its track.
          requestAnimationFrame(function () { window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize')); });
        }
      }).observe(document.getElementById('stage'));
    }
  }

  window.chPitchPrintSyncResponsiveUi = schedule;
  window.addEventListener('pagehide', stopMobileUpload);
  window.addEventListener('resize', schedule);
  if (window.visualViewport) {
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize', schedule);
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll', schedule);
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', mount);
  else mount();
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  if (window.chMobileTextEntryGuardInstalled) return;
  window.chMobileTextEntryGuardInstalled = true;
  var mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 767px)');
  var frame = 0;
  var activeTextEntryNodes = [];

  function isTextEntry(node) {
    if (!node || node === document.body || node === document.documentElement) return false;
    if (node.isContentEditable) return true;
    var name = node.tagName ? node.tagName.toLowerCase() : '';
    if (name === 'textarea') return true;
    if (name !== 'input') return false;
    var type = String(node.getAttribute('type') || 'text').toLowerCase();
    return ['button', 'checkbox', 'color', 'file', 'hidden', 'image', 'radio', 'range', 'reset', 'submit'].indexOf(type) === -1;
  }

  function clearActiveTextEntryNodes() {
    activeTextEntryNodes.forEach(function (node) {
      if (!node || !node.classList) return;
      node.classList.remove('ch-mobile-text-entry-popup', 'ch-mobile-text-entry-control');
      node.style.removeProperty('--ch-mobile-text-entry-top');
    });
    activeTextEntryNodes = [];
  }

  function textEntryHost(node) {
    return node.closest && (
      node.closest('[data-module="popup"], .popup, [data-module="dialog"], .dialog, [data-module="ai-text"]')
    ) || node;
  }

  function placeTextEntry(node, container) {
    var host = textEntryHost(node);
    var toolbar = container.querySelector('[data-module="context"]');
    var toolbarRect = toolbar && toolbar.getClientRects().length ? toolbar.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
    var menus = container.querySelector('[data-module="menus"]');
    var menuRect = menus && menus.getClientRects().length ? menus.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
    var stage = container.querySelector('#stage');
    var stageRect = stage && stage.getClientRects().length ? stage.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
    var viewport = window.visualViewport;
    var visualTop = viewport ? viewport.offsetTop : 0;
    var visualHeight = viewport ? viewport.height : window.innerHeight;
    var lowerEdge = toolbarRect ? toolbarRect.bottom : (menuRect ? menuRect.bottom : visualTop + 96);
    var mobileTextGap = Math.max(58, Math.min(86, visualHeight * .08));
    var toolbarIsBottom = toolbarRect && toolbarRect.top > visualTop + visualHeight * .45;
    var top = toolbarIsBottom
      ? Math.max(visualTop + 8, toolbarRect.top - Math.max(116, Math.min(168, visualHeight * .2)))
      : Math.max(visualTop + 8, lowerEdge + mobileTextGap);
    if (stageRect) {
      var stageOffset = Math.max(58, Math.min(96, stageRect.height * .16));
      top = toolbarIsBottom
        ? Math.max(visualTop + 8, Math.min(top, toolbarRect.top - 92))
        : Math.max(top, stageRect.top + stageOffset);
    }
    top = Math.min(top, visualTop + Math.max(96, visualHeight - 180));
    host.classList.add('ch-mobile-text-entry-popup');
    host.style.setProperty('--ch-mobile-text-entry-top', Math.round(top) + 'px');
    activeTextEntryNodes.push(host);
    if (host !== node && node.classList) {
      node.classList.add('ch-mobile-text-entry-control');
      activeTextEntryNodes.push(node);
    }
  }

  function sync() {
    frame = 0;
    var container = document.getElementById('container');
    if (!container) return;
    clearActiveTextEntryNodes();
    var active = mobile.matches &&
      !container.classList.contains('ch-save-preview-open') &&
      isTextEntry(document.activeElement);
    container.classList.toggle('ch-mobile-text-entry-active', active);
    if (active) placeTextEntry(document.activeElement, container);
  }

  function schedule() {
    if (frame) cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
    frame = requestAnimationFrame(sync);
    setTimeout(sync, 80);
  }

  document.addEventListener('focusin', schedule, true);
  document.addEventListener('focusout', schedule, true);
  document.addEventListener('click', schedule, true);
  document.addEventListener('input', schedule, true);
  window.addEventListener('resize', schedule);
  if (window.visualViewport) {
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize', schedule);
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll', schedule);
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', schedule);
  else schedule();
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  var desktopTrigger = null;
  var desktopFrame = 0;
  var relevantModuleSelector = [
    '[data-module="fonts"]', '[data-module="fonts-list"]', '[data-module="more-text"]',
    '[data-module="alignment"]', '[data-module="depth"]', '[data-module="size"]',
    '[data-module="opacity"]', '[data-module="stroke-width"]', '[data-module="transform"]',
    '[data-module="colors"]', '[data-module="image-editor"]'
  ].join(', ');

  function setPixels(node, name, value) { node.style.setProperty(name, Math.round(value) + 'px'); }
  function clearDesktopPopupState() {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-module="popup"].ch-v1060-desktop-context-popup').forEach(function (popup) {
      popup.classList.remove('ch-v1060-desktop-context-popup');
      ['--ch-v1060-popup-top', '--ch-v1060-popup-left', '--ch-v1060-popup-width', '--ch-v1060-popup-max-height'].forEach(function (name) { popup.style.removeProperty(name); });
    });
  }
  function relevantPopup() {
    var result = null;
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-module="popup"]').forEach(function (popup) {
      if (result || popup.closest('#all-modules') || !popup.querySelector(relevantModuleSelector)) return;
      if (!popup.getClientRects().length || getComputedStyle(popup).visibility === 'hidden') return;
      result = popup;
    });
    return result;
  }

  window.chDesktopKeepContextPopupInViewport = function () {
    if (window.innerWidth < 1200) { clearDesktopPopupState(); return; }
    var container = document.getElementById('container');
    var toolbar = container && container.querySelector('[data-module="context"]');
    var stage = container && container.querySelector('#stage');
    if (!container || !toolbar || !stage || container.classList.contains('ch-save-preview-open')) { clearDesktopPopupState(); return; }

    var stageRect = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
    setPixels(toolbar, '--ch-v1060-context-left', stageRect.left + stageRect.width / 2);
    setPixels(toolbar, '--ch-v1060-context-max-width', Math.max(280, stageRect.width - 24));

    var popup = relevantPopup();
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-module="popup"].ch-v1060-desktop-context-popup').forEach(function (node) {
      if (node !== popup) node.classList.remove('ch-v1060-desktop-context-popup');
    });
    if (!popup) return;

    var module = popup.querySelector(relevantModuleSelector);
    var toolbarRect = toolbar.getBoundingClientRect();
    var anchor = desktopTrigger && desktopTrigger.isConnected ? desktopTrigger : toolbar;
    var anchorRect = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
    var safeMargin = 16;
    var gap = 16;
    var popupWidth = module.dataset.module === 'image-editor' ? 600 : 320;
    popupWidth = Math.min(popupWidth, window.innerWidth - safeMargin * 2);
    var availableHeight = Math.max(0, toolbarRect.top - gap - safeMargin);

    popup.classList.add('ch-v1060-desktop-context-popup');
    setPixels(popup, '--ch-v1060-popup-width', popupWidth);
    setPixels(popup, '--ch-v1060-popup-max-height', availableHeight);
    var left = anchorRect.left + anchorRect.width / 2 - popupWidth / 2;
    var minLeft = Math.max(safeMargin, stageRect.left + safeMargin);
    var maxLeft = Math.min(window.innerWidth - popupWidth - safeMargin, stageRect.right - popupWidth - safeMargin);
    left = Math.max(minLeft, Math.min(left, Math.max(minLeft, maxLeft)));
    setPixels(popup, '--ch-v1060-popup-left', left);
    var popupHeight = Math.min(popup.getBoundingClientRect().height, availableHeight);
    setPixels(popup, '--ch-v1060-popup-top', Math.max(safeMargin, toolbarRect.top - gap - popupHeight));
  };

  function scheduleDesktopPopup(anchor) {
    if (window.innerWidth < 1200) return;
    if (anchor) desktopTrigger = anchor;
    if (desktopFrame) cancelAnimationFrame(desktopFrame);
    desktopFrame = requestAnimationFrame(function () {
      desktopFrame = 0;
      window.chDesktopKeepContextPopupInViewport();
      requestAnimationFrame(window.chDesktopKeepContextPopupInViewport);
    });
  }
  function mountDesktopToolbarFix() {
    var container = document.getElementById('container');
    if (!container || container.__chV1060DesktopToolbarBound) return;
    container.__chV1060DesktopToolbarBound = true;
    container.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      var trigger = event.target.closest && event.target.closest('[data-module="context"] > [data-cmd], [data-module="context"] > .font-display');
      if (!trigger) return;
      scheduleDesktopPopup(trigger);
      setTimeout(function () { window.chDesktopKeepContextPopupInViewport(); }, 60);
    }, true);
    container.addEventListener('input', function () { scheduleDesktopPopup(); }, true);
    container.addEventListener('change', function () { scheduleDesktopPopup(); }, true);
    window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
      if (window.innerWidth < 1200) clearDesktopPopupState();
      else scheduleDesktopPopup();
    });
    document.addEventListener('scroll', function () { scheduleDesktopPopup(); }, true);
    scheduleDesktopPopup();
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', mountDesktopToolbarFix);
  else mountDesktopToolbarFix();
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  var liveTrigger = null;
  var liveFrame = 0;
  var moduleSelector = [
    '[data-module="fonts"]', '[data-module="fonts-list"]', '[data-module="more-text"]',
    '[data-module="alignment"]', '[data-module="depth"]', '[data-module="size"]',
    '[data-module="opacity"]', '[data-module="stroke-width"]', '[data-module="transform"]',
    '[data-module="colors"]', '[data-module="image-editor"]'
  ].join(', ');
  var propertyNames = [
    '--ch-v1061-popup-top', '--ch-v1061-popup-left',
    '--ch-v1061-popup-width', '--ch-v1061-popup-max-height'
  ];

  function setPixels(node, name, value) {
    node.style.setProperty(name, Math.round(value) + 'px');
  }
  function clearLivePopupState() {
    document.querySelectorAll('#all-modules > [data-module="popup"].ch-v1061-live-context-popup').forEach(function (popup) {
      popup.classList.remove('ch-v1061-live-context-popup');
      propertyNames.forEach(function (name) { popup.style.removeProperty(name); });
    });
  }
  function findLivePopup() {
    var result = null;
    document.querySelectorAll('#all-modules > [data-module="popup"]').forEach(function (popup) {
      if (result || !popup.querySelector(moduleSelector)) return;
      if (!popup.getClientRects().length || getComputedStyle(popup).visibility === 'hidden') return;
      result = popup;
    });
    return result;
  }

  window.chKeepLiveContextPopupAtTop = function () {
    if (window.innerWidth < 1024) { clearLivePopupState(); return; }
    var container = document.getElementById('container');
    var toolbar = container && container.querySelector('[data-module="context"]');
    var stage = container && container.querySelector('#stage');
    if (!container || !toolbar || !stage || container.classList.contains('ch-save-preview-open')) {
      clearLivePopupState();
      return;
    }

    var popup = findLivePopup();
    document.querySelectorAll('#all-modules > [data-module="popup"].ch-v1061-live-context-popup').forEach(function (node) {
      if (node !== popup) {
        node.classList.remove('ch-v1061-live-context-popup');
        propertyNames.forEach(function (name) { node.style.removeProperty(name); });
      }
    });
    if (!popup) return;

    var module = popup.querySelector(moduleSelector);
    var stageRect = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
    var toolbarRect = toolbar.getBoundingClientRect();
    var anchor = liveTrigger && liveTrigger.isConnected ? liveTrigger : toolbar;
    var anchorRect = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
    var safeMargin = 16;
    var gap = 16;
    var popupWidth = module.dataset.module === 'image-editor' ? 600 : 320;
    var topBoundary = Math.max(safeMargin, stageRect.top + safeMargin);
    var availableHeight = Math.max(0, toolbarRect.top - gap - topBoundary);
    popupWidth = Math.min(popupWidth, window.innerWidth - safeMargin * 2);

    popup.classList.add('ch-v1061-live-context-popup');
    setPixels(popup, '--ch-v1061-popup-width', popupWidth);
    setPixels(popup, '--ch-v1061-popup-max-height', availableHeight);

    var left = anchorRect.left + anchorRect.width / 2 - popupWidth / 2;
    var minLeft = Math.max(safeMargin, stageRect.left + safeMargin);
    var maxLeft = Math.min(window.innerWidth - popupWidth - safeMargin, stageRect.right - popupWidth - safeMargin);
    left = Math.max(minLeft, Math.min(left, Math.max(minLeft, maxLeft)));
    setPixels(popup, '--ch-v1061-popup-left', left);

    var isFontList = module.matches('[data-module="fonts"], [data-module="fonts-list"]');
    var popupHeight = Math.min(popup.getBoundingClientRect().height, availableHeight);
    var top = isFontList ? topBoundary : Math.max(topBoundary, toolbarRect.top - gap - popupHeight);
    setPixels(popup, '--ch-v1061-popup-top', top);
  };

  function scheduleLivePopup(anchor) {
    if (anchor) liveTrigger = anchor;
    if (liveFrame) cancelAnimationFrame(liveFrame);
    liveFrame = requestAnimationFrame(function () {
      liveFrame = 0;
      window.chKeepLiveContextPopupAtTop();
      requestAnimationFrame(window.chKeepLiveContextPopupAtTop);
    });
  }
  function mountLivePopupFix() {
    var container = document.getElementById('container');
    if (!container || container.__chV1061LivePopupBound) return;
    container.__chV1061LivePopupBound = true;
    container.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      var trigger = event.target.closest && event.target.closest('[data-module="context"] > [data-cmd], [data-module="context"] > .font-display');
      if (!trigger) return;
      scheduleLivePopup(trigger);
      setTimeout(window.chKeepLiveContextPopupAtTop, 60);
    }, true);
    container.addEventListener('input', function () { scheduleLivePopup(); }, true);
    container.addEventListener('change', function () { scheduleLivePopup(); }, true);
    window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
      if (window.innerWidth < 1024) clearLivePopupState();
      else scheduleLivePopup();
    });
    document.addEventListener('scroll', function () { scheduleLivePopup(); }, true);
    scheduleLivePopup();
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', mountLivePopupFix);
  else mountLivePopupFix();
})();
</script>

  #container.ch-save-preview-open [data-module="preview"] .ch-order-subtitle {
    max-width: 100% !important;
    white-space: normal !important;
    overflow-wrap: anywhere !important;
  }

  #container[data-ch-flow-mode] [data-module="preview"] #ch-public-order-config {
    flex: 0 0 auto !important;
    height: auto !important;
    min-height: 0 !important;
    max-height: none !important;
    overflow: visible !important;
  }

  #container #ch-order-scroll {
    flex: 0 0 auto !important;
    min-height: 0 !important;
    overflow: visible !important;
    overflow-y: visible !important;
    overscroll-behavior: auto !important;
  }

  /* V10.93: public mobile reviews keep the order form below the mockup instead
     of letting the old fixed bottom-sheet rule cover the garment. */
  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"],
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) {
    --ch-public-review-mockup-lift: clamp(68px, 11vh, 116px);
    --ch-public-review-mockup-lift: clamp(68px, 11svh, 116px);
    grid-template-rows: auto clamp(480px, 76vh, 680px) auto !important;
    grid-template-rows: auto clamp(480px, 76svh, 680px) auto !important;
  }

  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public > [data-module="stage-parent"],
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] > [data-module="stage-parent"],
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) > [data-module="stage-parent"] {
    align-self: start !important;
    height: clamp(480px, 76vh, 680px) !important;
    height: clamp(480px, 76svh, 680px) !important;
    min-height: clamp(480px, 76vh, 680px) !important;
    min-height: clamp(480px, 76svh, 680px) !important;
    max-height: none !important;
    flex: 0 0 auto !important;
  }

  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage {
    align-items: start !important;
    align-content: start !important;
    justify-items: center !important;
    justify-content: center !important;
    padding-top: 0 !important;
  }

  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > .canvas-container,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > canvas,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > img,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > picture,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > svg,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > .canvas-container,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > canvas,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > img,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > picture,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > svg,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > .canvas-container,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > canvas,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > img,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > picture,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > svg {
    align-self: start !important;
    justify-self: center !important;
    margin-top: 0 !important;
    object-position: center top !important;
  }

  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > .canvas-container,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > canvas,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > .canvas-container,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > canvas,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > .canvas-container,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > canvas {
    top: 0 !important;
    bottom: auto !important;
  }

  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > .canvas-container,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > .canvas-container,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) > [data-module="stage-parent"] > #stage > .canvas-container {
    translate: 0 calc(-1 * var(--ch-public-review-mockup-lift, 64px)) !important;
    transform-origin: top center !important;
  }

  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public > [data-module="stage-parent"] > [data-module="navigator"],
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] > [data-module="stage-parent"] > [data-module="navigator"],
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) > [data-module="stage-parent"] > [data-module="navigator"] {
    align-self: start !important;
    padding-top: clamp(18px, 11vh, 84px) !important;
    padding-top: clamp(18px, 11svh, 84px) !important;
  }

  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public [data-module="preview"] #ch-public-order-config,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open.ch-flow-public [data-module="preview"] .ch-public-order-config,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] [data-module="preview"] #ch-public-order-config,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open[data-ch-flow-mode="PUBLIC_CUSTOMIZE"] [data-module="preview"] .ch-public-order-config,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) [data-module="preview"] #ch-public-order-config,
  #container.ch-save-preview-open:not(.ch-flow-creator):not([data-ch-flow-mode="CREATOR_DESIGN"]) [data-module="preview"] .ch-public-order-config {
    position: relative !important;
    inset: auto !important;
    top: auto !important;
    right: auto !important;
    bottom: auto !important;
    left: auto !important;
    z-index: 1 !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    max-width: 100% !important;
    height: auto !important;
    min-height: 0 !important;
    max-height: none !important;
    margin: 0 !important;
    padding: 0 !important;
    overflow: visible !important;
    transform: none !important;
  }
}
</style>
<script>
(function () {
  if (window.chMobileFontDismissInstalled) return;
  window.chMobileFontDismissInstalled = true;
  var mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 767px)');
  var dismissedStyle = {
    display: 'none',
    visibility: 'hidden',
    'pointer-events': 'none',
    width: '0',
    height: '0',
    'min-width': '0',
    'min-height': '0',
    'max-width': '0',
    'max-height': '0',
    margin: '0',
    padding: '0',
    border: '0',
    background: 'transparent',
    'box-shadow': 'none',
    overflow: 'hidden'
  };

  function visible(node) {
    return !!(node && node.getClientRects && node.getClientRects().length && getComputedStyle(node).visibility !== 'hidden');
  }

  function resetDismissedPickers() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.ch-mobile-font-dismissed, .ch-mobile-color-dismissed').forEach(function (popup) {
      popup.classList.remove('ch-mobile-font-dismissed', 'ch-mobile-color-dismissed');
      if (popup.dataset.chMobilePickerDismissed === '1') {
        Object.keys(dismissedStyle).forEach(function (name) { popup.style.removeProperty(name); });
        delete popup.dataset.chMobilePickerDismissed;
      }
    });
  }

  function collapseDismissedNode(node) {
    if (!node) return;
    Object.keys(dismissedStyle).forEach(function (name) {
      node.style.setProperty(name, dismissedStyle[name], 'important');
    });
    node.dataset.chMobilePickerDismissed = '1';
  }

  function fireSyntheticPointer(target, type) {
    var event;
    var init = {
      bubbles: true,
      cancelable: true,
      composed: true,
      clientX: 2,
      clientY: 2,
      screenX: 2,
      screenY: 2,
      button: 0,
      buttons: type === 'pointerup' || type === 'mouseup' || type === 'click' ? 0 : 1
    };
    try {
      if (type.indexOf('pointer') === 0 && window.PointerEvent) {
        event = new PointerEvent(type, Object.assign({ pointerId: 1, pointerType: 'touch', isPrimary: true }, init));
      } else {
        event = new MouseEvent(type, init);
      }
      target.dispatchEvent(event);
    } catch (error) {}
  }

  function closeNativePickerByOutsideTap() {
    var container = document.getElementById('container');
    var target = (container && container.querySelector('[data-module="menus"]')) || document.body || document.documentElement;
    if (!target) return;
    ['pointerdown', 'mousedown', 'pointerup', 'mouseup', 'click'].forEach(function (type) {
      fireSyntheticPointer(target, type);
    });
    try {
      document.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true, key: 'Escape', code: 'Escape' }));
      document.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keyup', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true, key: 'Escape', code: 'Escape' }));
    } catch (error) {}
  }

  function dismissPickerShell(module, dismissClass) {
    if (!module) return;
    module.classList.add(dismissClass);
    collapseDismissedNode(module);

    if (dismissClass === 'ch-mobile-color-dismissed') {
      var allModules = module.closest('#all-modules');
      if (allModules) {
        allModules.classList.add(dismissClass);
        collapseDismissedNode(allModules);
      }
    }

    var shell = module.closest('[data-module="popup"], .popup');
    if (shell) {
      shell.classList.add(dismissClass);
      collapseDismissedNode(shell);
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('#all-modules > [data-module="popup"], #all-modules > .popup, body > [data-module="popup"], body > .popup, [data-module="popup"].ch-tool-popover, .popup.ch-tool-popover').forEach(function (popup) {
      if (popup === shell || popup.classList.contains('ch-tool-popover') || visible(popup) || popup.querySelector('[data-module="colors"], [data-module="fonts"]')) {
        popup.classList.add(dismissClass);
        collapseDismissedNode(popup);
      }
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
    if (!mobile.matches || !event.target.closest) return;
    var trigger = event.target.closest('[data-cmd="fonts"], [data-module="context"] > [data-cmd="alignment"], [data-module="context"] > [data-cmd="depth"], [data-module="context"] > [data-cmd="size"], [data-module="context"] > [data-cmd="opacity"], [data-module="context"] > [data-cmd="transform"], [data-module="context"] > [data-cmd="stroke-width"], [data-module="context"] > [data-cmd="more-text"], [data-module="context"] > [data-cmd="edit-image"], [data-module="context"] > [data-cmd="colors"]');
    if (trigger) {
      resetDismissedPickers();
      return;
    }

    var option = event.target.closest('[data-module="fonts"] .flist [data-idx]');
    if (option && !option.classList.contains('input')) {
      var fonts = option.closest('[data-module="fonts"]');

      // Defer dismissal until the native click handler has applied the font.
      setTimeout(function () {
        if (!mobile.matches) return;
        // Public customization can replace or reparent the live panel on selection.
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-module="fonts"]').forEach(function (panel) {
          if (panel !== fonts && !panel.getClientRects().length) return;
          dismissPickerShell(panel, 'ch-mobile-font-dismissed');
        });
      }, 0);
      return;
    }

    var colorOption = event.target.closest('[data-module="colors"] [data-cmd="col"]');
    if (!colorOption) return;
    var colors = colorOption.closest('[data-module="colors"]');

    // Defer dismissal until the native click handler has applied the color.
    setTimeout(function () {
      if (!mobile.matches) return;
      closeNativePickerByOutsideTap();
      setTimeout(function () {
        if (!mobile.matches) return;
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-module="colors"]').forEach(function (panel) {
          if (panel !== colors && !panel.getClientRects().length) return;
          dismissPickerShell(panel, 'ch-mobile-color-dismissed');
        });
      }, 80);
    }, 0);
  }, true);

  window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
    if (!mobile.matches) resetDismissedPickers();
  });
})();
</script>
